iṉi, ittakaiya cīruñciṟappum vāynta ivviyal nūlai yaruḷiya āciriyarākum olkāpperumait tolkāppiyaṉāratu varalāṟu ēṉaippala āciriyarkaḷiṉ varalāṟu pōṉṟē nām cevvitiṉ aṟiya voṇṇātatāyiṟṟu. ivvāciriyariṉ varalāṟṟuk kuṟippukkaḷāka namakkuk kiṭaittiruppaṉa mikac cilavē. ivai koṇṭu ciṟcilar tām tām karutiyavāṟu ivvāciriyaraip paṟṟi eḻutiyirukkiṉṟaṉar. yāmum tol kāppiyar varalāṟāka aṟivaṉavaṟṟait tolkāppiya ārāycci muṭiviṟ kūṟuvēm. iṉittolkāppiya ārāycciyaip pala pakutikaḷākap pakuttuk koṇṭu, avaṟṟai muṟaiyē orō voṉṟāka eḻutivarakkarutiyuḷḷēm. iyalpilē curuṅkiya aṟiviṉēmākiya yām palaviṉai nalivukaṭku iṭaiyē ippērārāycciyai eṭuttuk koṇṭatu tamiḻaṉṉaiyiṉ ciṟumakār āṟṟum icciṟu tiruttoṇṭukku aṉparkaḷiṉ aṉpu muṉṉiṉṟu ūkkamaḷippatu pōṉṟu iṟaivaṉṟiruvaruḷ uṇṇiṉṟu ūkkamaḷikkum eṉṉum tuṇipu koṇṭēyām.
	vāḻi karantai vaḷarun tamiḻccaṅkam
vāḻi tamiḻppoḻil māṇpuṭaṉē- vāḻiyarō
maṉṉumataṉ kāvalarām vaṇmaipuri vōrevarum
uṉṉupuka ḻiṉpanalam uṟṟu.1


8. periyapurāṇamum poruḷilakkaṇamum
tiruttoṇṭarkaḷiṉ varalāṟu teritturaikkum periya purāṇat tirumuṟai yiṉkaṇṇē kāṇappaṭum poruḷilakkaṇak kuṟippukkaḷuḷ akapporuṭ pakutiyavāya cilavaṟṟaiyeṭuttuk kāṭṭutal icciṟu kaṭṭuraiyiṉ karuttām. cēkkiḻār perumāṉ paṇṭaittaṇṭamiḻ ilakkiya ilakkaṇat tuṟaikaḷellāñ ceṉṟu teḷiporuḷ kaṇṭa centamiḻp pērāciriyarkaḷuḷ oruvarāvareṉpataṉaip periyapurāṇac ceyyuṭkaḷ potuvāka vaṉṟic ciṟappuvakaiyāṉum uṇartti niṟkiṉṟaṉa. pāṇṭināṭṭu maturaiyilē tamiḻccaṅkam iruntatum, civaperumāṉ caṅkattu vīṟṟiruntatum, poruṇūl aruḷiyatum, tiripurameritta viricaṭaik kaṭavuḷākiya apporumāṉ talaiccaṅkap pulavarātalum ākiya tamiḻ varalāṟṟuk kuṟippukkaḷ piṉṉuḷḷa ceyyuṭkaḷil amaintiruttal kāṇalākum.
'	cālpāya mummait tamiḻtaṅkiya vaṅkaṇ mūtūr nūlpā yiṭattum muḷanōṉṟalai mēti pāyap
pālpāy mulaitōy matuppaṅkayam pāya veṅkuñ
cēlpāy taṭattum muḷaceyyuṇmik kēṟu caṅkam'1
'	mummaip puvaṉaṅkaḷiṉ mikkataṉ ṟēyam mūtūr
meymmaip poruḷān tamiḻnūliṉ viḷaṅku vāymaic
cemmaip poruḷun taruvārtiru vāla vāyil
emmaip pavantīrp pavarcaṅka mirunta teṉṟāl'.2
'	nīla māmiṭaṟ ṟālavā yāṉeṉa nilavu
mūla mākiya tiruviruk kukkuṟaṇ moḻintu
cīla mātavat tiruttoṇṭar tammoṭun tiḷaittār
cālu mēṉmaiyiṟ ṟalaiccaṅkap pulavaṉār tammuṉ'3
maṟṟum tirukkuṟipputtoṇṭa nāyaṉār purāṇattil
"	aṅkaṇ mullaiyiṉ ṟeyvameṉ ṟaruntamiḻuraikkum
ceṅkaṇ māṟoḻuñ civaṉmakiḻ tirumullai vāyil"1
eṉṟuraittiruppatu tolkāppiyampōlum tamiḻilakkaṇa muṉṉūlkaḷiṉ āṇaivarampu kaṭaippiṭittuykkum āciriyar iyalpiṉaip pulappaṭuttukiṉṟatu. toṇṭai nāṭṭiṉ mullaiyai aṇinturaikkumiṭattē attiṇaikkuriya teyvattiṉ iruppaiyuṭaṉpaṭṭuc caivattiṉ mēṉmai terikkum camaṟkāram peritum pōṟṟaṟpāla toṉṟākum. itaṉāl civaperumāṉ ennilattukkum iṟaiyeṉpatu kuṟippittavāṟumākum. tirunāṭṭuc ciṟappilē 'moyvaraiyulakam pōlum muḷarinīr marutavaippu' eṉpatiluḷḷa 'moyvaraiyulakam' eṉṉum coṟṟoṭar tolkāppiyattiluḷḷa 'cēyōṉ mēya maivarai yulakamum' eṉpataṉai niṉaintu eḻuntatātal vēṇṭum. purāṇattil 'maivaraiyulakam' eṉṟē pāṭaṅkoḷḷutalum ciṟappuṭaittātal karutaṟpālatu. 'tirunāṭṭuc ciṟappil' - (pā.12) 'intirateyvatan toḻutu nāṟu naṭuvar' eṉṟatu ilakkaṇaṅkūṟum 'vēntaṉmēya tīmpuṉalulakattiṟku ēṟpuṭaittāmāṟu kāṇka.
periyapurāṇattiṟ kaṇṇappanāyaṉār purāṇam mutaliya pala purāṇaṅkaḷil tiṇaikaḷiṉ peṟṟi virivākavum curukkamākavum kūṟappaṭṭiruppiṉum, tirukkuṟipput toṇṭanāyaṉār purāṇat tiṟṟāṉ āciriyar aintiṇaikaḷaiyum oruṅkē ārāyntu aḻaku peṟak kūṟivaittirukkiṉṟaṉar. civañāṉamuṉivar tamatu kāñcip purāṇattil 'tiruttoṇṭai naṉṉāṭṭu nāṉilattain tiṇai vaḷamun terittuk kāṭṭa maruttoṇṭaivāycciyar vāḻkuṉṟai nakarkkulak kaviyē vallāṉ' eṉṟu pārāṭṭiyatu ippakutiyai nōkkiyēyāku poruḷilakkaṇaṅ kaṟpāre varum itaṉ cuvaiyai nukarntiṉpuṟalvēṇṭum. itiluḷḷa tiṇaippakutiyellām eṭutteḻutiṉ mikaviriyumākaliṉ oṉṟiraṇṭu ceytikaḷē iṅkē kāṭṭappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
"	kuṟavar paṉmaṇi yarittitai vitaippaṉa kuṟiñci
kaṟavai yāṉirai māṉuṭaṉ payilvaṉa kāṉam
paṟavai tāmarai yiruntiṟa varuntuva paḻaṉam
cuṟava muṇmarup paṇaṅkayar vaṉakaḻic cūḻal."2
'	tēṉi ṟaintacen tiṉaiyiṭi tarumalaic cīṟūr
pāṉi ṟaintapuṟ patattaṉa mullainīḷ pāṭi
tūne laṉṉaney kaṉṉaliṉ kaṉiyataṇ ṭuṟaiyūr
mīṉi ṟaintapē ruṇaviṉa vēlaivaip piṭaṅkaḷ.'1
ivviru pāṭṭilum kuṟiñci, mullai, marutam, neytal eṉṉum nāṉku nilaṅkaḷiṉ peṟṟi naṉku kūṟappaṭṭiruttal kāṇka. muṟpāṭṭil neytalukkuk kūṟiya 'cuṟavamuṇmarup paṇaṅkayarvaṉa' eṉṉuñ ceyti 'ciṉaiccuṟaviṉ kōṭu naṭṭu maṉaiccērttiya vallaṇaṅkiṉāl'2 eṉṟu paṭṭiṉappālaiyiṟ kūṟappeṟṟuḷḷatu. 'neytaṉilattil nuḷaiyarkku valaivaḷantappiṉ ammakaḷir kiḷaiyuṭaṉ kuḻīic cuṟavukkōṭu nāṭṭip paravukkaṭaṉ koṭut taliṉ āṇṭu varuṇaṉ veḷippaṭumeṉṟār' eṉa nacciṉārkkiṉiyar tolkāppiya uraiyiṟ kūṟutalum iṅkē karutaṟpālatu.
iṉi, tiṇai aintāyiṉum āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār pālaittiṇaikku nilam vēṇṭiṟṟila reṉpatu,
"	naṭuva ṇaintiṇai naṭuvaṇa toḻiyap

paṭutirai vaiyam pāttiya paṇpē"3
eṉṉum akattiṇaiyiyaṟ cūttirattāṉ aṟiyalākum, pālait
tiṇaikku iyaṟkaiyil nila miṉṟāyiṉum vēṉiṟkālattu naṇpakaṟ poḻutil mullaiyum kuṟiñciyum cārnta iṭaṅkaḷ vemmai mikkup pālaiyeṉappaṭum eṉpatu tollāciriyar koḷkai. itaṉai,
"	vēṉiṟ kiḻavaṉoṭu veṅkatir vēntaṉ
tāṉa lanti rukat taṉmaiyiṟ kuṉṟi
mullaiyuṅ kuṟiñciyu muṟaimaiyiṟ ṟirintu
nalliyal piḻantu naṭuṅkutuya ruṟuttup
pālai yeṉpatōr paṭivaṅ koḷḷum"4
eṉṉum cilappatikārattuk kāṭukāṇkātaiyāṉ aṟika. itu,
"	kōla mullaiyuṅ kuṟiñciyu maṭutta cilliṭaṅkaḷ
nīla vāṭpaṭai nīlikōṭ ṭaṅkaḷu nirantu
kāla vēṉiliṟ kaṭumpakaṟ poḻutiṉaip paṟṟip
pālai yuñcola lāvaṉa vuḷaparaṉ murampu"5
eṉṉum pāṭṭil cēkkiḻār perumāṉāl evvaḷavu aḻaku peṟak kūṟappeṟṟuḷatu. cilliṭaṅkaḷ, poḻutiṉaip paṟṟi, pālaiyuñ colalāvaṉa eṉṉun toṭarkaḷellām āḻnta karuttiṉaik koṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. maṟṟu ippāṭṭil pālaikkut teyvaṅ kūṟiyiruppatu āciriyaratu teḷiviṉaik kāṭṭukiṉṟatu. 'māyōṉ mēya' eṉṉum tolkāppiyac cūttiravuraiyil nacciṉārkkiṉiyar, 'pālaikku ñāyiṟṟait teyvamākki avaṉiṟṟōṉṟiya maḻaiyiṉaiyum kāṟṟiṉaiyum atteyvap pakutiyākkik kūṟupavāleṉiṉ, ellāt teyvattiṟkum antaṇar avikoṭukkuṅkāl aṅki ātittaṉkaṭkoṭukkum eṉpatu vētamuṭipākaliṉ ātittaṉ ellā nilattiṟkum potuveṉamaṟukka' eṉṟum, 'avvannilattiṟṟeyvaṅkaḷē pālaikkun teyvamāyiṟṟu' eṉṟum, 'teyvamuṇāvē' eṉṉum cūttiravuraiyil 'itaṉāṉē pālaikkut teyvamum iṉṟāyiṟṟu' eṉṟum tam tuṇipuraittup pōntaṉar. uraiyāciriyarākiya iḷampūraṇavaṭikaḷō 'teyvam koṟṟavai' eṉṟu kūṟiṉar. ikkoḷkaikkē ilakkiya mēṟkōḷkaḷ mikutiyākak kāṇappaṭum. cēkkiḻār perumāṉum 'nīla vāṭpaṭainīli' eṉak koṟṟavaiyaik kūṟivaittiruppatu nām pōṟṟaṟ pālatoṉṟākum.


9. tamiḻ eḻuttukkaḷ
"	eḻutteṉap paṭupa,
akaramutal
ṉakara viṟuvāy muppa ḵteṉpa
cārntuvaral marapiṉ mūṉṟalaṅ kaṭaiyē."1
"	avaitām,
kuṟṟiya likaram kuṟṟiya lukaram
āyta meṉṟa
muppāṟ puḷḷiyum eḻuttō raṉṉa,"2
eṉṉum tolkāppiya nūṉmarapuc cūttiraṅkaḷ tamiḻeḻuttukkaḷ ivai yeṉavum, ittuṇaiya veṉavum uṇarttukiṉṟaṉa. 'eḻutteṉappaṭupa' eṉṉum ittalaic cūttirattāṉum, ivviyaliṟ piṉvarum pala cūttiraṅkaḷāṉum tamiḻeḻuttukkaḷiṉ muṟai neṭuṅkaṇakkil vaḻaṅkivarum muṟaiyē yāmeṉpatu aṟiyap paṭum. atu virivañci viḷakkātu viṭappaṭukiṉṟatu. akaram mutal oḷakāram iṟutiyākavuḷḷa paṉṉiraṇṭu uyireḻuttukkaḷum, kakaram mutal ṉakaram iṟutiyākavuḷḷa patiṉeṭṭu meyyeḻuttuk kaḷum, kuṟṟiyalikaram, kuṟṟiyalukaram, āytam eṉṟa mūṉṟu cārpeḻuttukkaḷum tamiḻeḻuttukkaḷāmeṉka. eḻuttuk kaḷai uruveḻuttu, uṇarveḻuttu, oliyeḻuttu, taṉmai yeḻuttu eṉṟum, piṟavāṟum pakuttukkoṇṭu ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟuvārumuḷar. iḷampūraṇarum, nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum, 'taṉmaiyum vaṭivum namakkuṇarttalarumaiyiṉ āciriyar kūṟiṟṟilar' eṉa iṅṅaṉa muraippatu taṉmaiyeḻutteṉa ōrpirivu koḷḷutalum uṇṭeṉ pataṟkup paḻañcāṉṟākum. veḷippaṭaiyākavuḷḷa oliyeḻuttu, vaṭiveḻuttu eṉpaṉavē iṅku ārāyccikkuriyaṉa.
eḻuttu eṉṉum peyariṉ kāraṇam
cuvāmināta tēcikar tamatu ilakkaṇak kotturaiyil eḻutteṉṉum peyar paṟṟi yeḻutuvatu piṉvarumāṟu:- "eḻutteṉṉun toḻiṟ peyar apporuḷai viṭṭup  pālpakāvaḵṟiṇaip poruṭpotup peyarāy, apporuḷai viṭṭu ōviya mutaliyaṉa pōlaṉṟi akaram ṉakaram mutaliyaṉa vaṭivai yuṇarttuñ ciṟappup peyarāy, apporuḷai viṭṭu oliyai yuṇarttum ākupeyarāy, apporuḷaiviṭṭu avvoliyiṉa tilakkaṇattai yuṇarttum irumaṭiyāku peyarāy, apporuḷaiviṭṭu avvilakkaṇattai yuṇarttum nūliṉaiyuṇarttum mummaṭiyāku peyarāy, apporuḷai viṭṭu iṅṅaṉaṅ kūṟiṟṟeḻuttu  iṅṅaṉa maṟivitta teḻuttu eṉak karumakaruttāvaiyum karuvik karuttāvaiyum uṇarttum nāṉmaṭi yākupeyarāy niṉṟu palaporuḷ paṭṭatu kāṇka".
itaṉāṉē, eḻutteṉṉuñ col toḻiṟ peyareṉpatum, atu mutaṟkaṇ eḻutaluṟum vaṭivaiyuṇarttip piṉpu oliyai yuṇarttaluṟṟa teṉpatum avar karuttātal peṟappaṭum. yāpparuṅkala virutti mēṟkōṭ cūttiramoṉṟu 'eḻutappaṭu taliṉeḻuttē' eṉṟu  kūṟuvatum, naṉṉūl uraikkaṇ 'eḻutap paṭutaliṉ eḻutteṉak koḷka' eṉṟu mayilai nātar kūṟuvatum eḻutteṉpatu eḻutu eṉṉum pakutiyaṭiyākap piṟanta ceyappaṭu poruṭpeyar eṉṉum karuttuṭaiyaṉavām. civañāṉa muṉivar tolkāppiyac cūttira viruttiyil eḻutappaṭuvateṉṉum poruṭkaṇ eḻuteṉṉu mutaṉilaiyuriccolliṉ muṉṉarc ceyappaṭu poruṇmai yuṇarttum aikāravikuti puṇarntu..... avvaikāraṅ keṭṭuk keṭṭa vaḻit takaramiraṭṭittu eḻutteṉa muṭintatu' eṉṟu kūṟuvatu ataṉait teḷivākkukiṟatu. maṟṟum avar, 'ivvāṟaṉṟi eḻuteṉṉu mutaṉilai eḻutteṉattāṉē tirintu niṉṟa teṉṟum, aḵtāku peyarāṟ ceyappaṭu poruḷai yuṇarttiṟ ṟeṉṟuṅ kūṟuvārumuḷar; atu poruntātu, eṉṟuraippatu cuvāmināta tēcikar kūṟṟai maṟuppatu pōṉṟuḷatu. eṉiṉum, ivaraṉaivarum eḻutteṉpataṟku eḻutu eṉpatē mutaṉilaiyeṉak koṇṭaṉar. iṉṉōr karuttiṉpaṭi eḻutteṉpatu eḻutappaṭum vaṭivaiyē mutaṟkaṭ kuṟittatākum. aṅṅaṉamāyiṉ, oliyaiyuṇarntu kōṭaṟkuk karuviyākavē vaṭivu vēṇṭappaṭṭateṉpatu ellārkkum uṭampāṭākaliṉ vaṭivākiya eḻuttuṇṭātaṟku muṉpu oliyaik kuṟippatoru peyar iruntiruttal vēṇṭum. tolkāppiyattuyāṇṭum eḻutteṉṉum peyarē payiṉṟu vantuḷatu. 'ōraḷa picaikkuṅ kuṟṟeḻuttu' 'īraḷa picaikku neṭṭeḻuttu' eṉṟāṟpōl varuvaṉa palavum oliyaiyē ciṟappākak kuṟittavum, vaṭiviṉaic ciṟappākak kuṟikkaluṟum 'uṭpeṟu puḷḷi yuruvā kummē' 'meyyi ṉiyaṟkai puḷḷiyoṭu nilaiyal' eṉṟaṟ ṟoṭakkattuc cūttiraṅkaḷil eḻutteṉṉum peyar kāṇappaṭāmaiyum cintikkaṟpālaṉa.
ivvaṟṟāl eḻutteṉṉum peyar moḻik karuviyām oliyaik kuṟippa toṉṟākavē tolkāppiyaṉār koṇṭuḷā reṉak karututal porutta muṭaitteṉat tōṉṟukiṟatu. tolkāppiya uraikārar yārum ippeyarp poruḷai viḷakkiṟṟilarāyiṉum 'ivveḻutteṉappaṭṭa ōcaiyai aruveṉpār aṟiyātār; ataṉai uruveṉṟēkōṭum' eṉa nacciṉārkkiṉiyar kūṟuvatu oruvāṟu ikkaruttiṟkuc cārpākavuḷatu. iṉi, āciriyar 'ellā veḻuttum' eṉṉum piṟappiyaṟ cūttirattu eḻuttolikaḷiṉ nilaivēṟupāṭukaḷai yuṇarttuṅkāl 'eḻutaru vaḷi'yil eṉavum, 'akatteḻu vaḷiyicai' eṉavum, 'eḻuntupuṟat ticaikkum meyterivaḷiyicai' eṉavum, vaḷiyicaiyiṉ eḻucciyai avaṟṟukkuk kāraṇamākak kōṭaliṉ eḻu eṉṉum pakutiyiṉiṉṟu eḻutteṉṉum peyar tōṉṟiṟṟākak koḷvatu porutta muṭaitteṉat tōṉṟu kiṟatu. atu piṉpu avveḻut tiṉaiyuṇartaṟkuk karuviyāka vēṇṭappaṭṭa vaṭiviṉaiyum kuṟittatākal vēṇṭum.
uyir mutaliya peyarkaḷiṉ kāraṇam
iṉi, akaramutaliya paṉṉiraṇṭukkum uyir eṉṟum, kakara mutaliya patiṉeṭṭukkum mey eṉṟum kuṟiyiṭṭiruppatu paṇṭaittamiḻariṉ uṇmai nūluṇarvukkuttakka cāṉṟākum. naṉṉūl uraiyil, mayilainātar 'āvi, uyir, mey, uṭampu eṉpaṉa iṭukuṟip potuppeyar' eṉṟu kūṟiyatu poruttamil kūṟṟām. 'am mutalīrā ṟāvi' eṉṉum naṉṉūṟ cūttiravuraik kaṇ 'āviyum meyyum pōṟaliṉ ivviruvakaiyeḻuttiṟkum āvi meyyeṉpaṉa uvamavāku peyarāyk kāraṇap potup peyarāyiṉa' eṉṟu caṅkara namaccivāyappulavar kūṟuvatum, 'oḷakāra viṟuvāyp, paṉṉīreḻuttu muyireṉa moḻipa' eṉṉun tolkāppiyar cūttira vuraikkaṇ 'ituvum āṭciyuṅ kāraṇamum nōkkiyatōr kuṟi; meypatiṉeṭṭiṉaiyum iyakkittāṉ aruvāy vaṭiviṉṟi niṟṟaliṉ uyirāyiṟṟu' eṉṟum, 'ṉakāra viṟuvāyp, patiṉeṇṇeḻuttu meyyeṉa moḻipa' eṉṉuñ cūttiravuraikkaṇ 'ituvum āṭciyuṅ kāraṇamum nōkkiya kuṟi, eṉṉai, paṉṉīruyirkkuntāṉ iṭaṅkoṭuttu avaṟṟāṉ iyaṅkuntaṉmai peṟṟa uṭampāy niṟṟaliṉ' eṉṟum nacciṉārkkiṉiyar uraippaṉavum uyir meyyeṉpavaṟṟiṉ poruḷai viḷakkuvaṉavām. kuṟṟiya likaram, kuṟṟiyalukaram eṉpaṉa ikara ukaraṅkaḷiṉ kuṟukkamāyiṉum avai muṉ oru māttiraiyāy niṉṟu piṉ arai māttiraiyāyk kuṟukiṉa eṉaṟkiṭaṉiṉṟi, iṭamum paṟṟukkōṭuñ cārntu iyaṟkaiyāy araimāttirai peṟṟu niṟṟalāṉum appeyark kuriyavāyiṉa. āytam eṉpataṉ peyark kāraṇattaip paḻaiya uraiyāḷar yārum uḷḷavāṟu pulappaṭuttiṉārallar. 'āyta meṉṟa ōcaitāṉ aṭuppuk kūṭṭuppōl mūṉṟu puḷḷi vaṭiviṟṟeṉpatu uṇarttaṟku āytameṉṟa muppāṟ puḷḷiyu meṉṟār' eṉat tolkāppiyavuraiyil nacciṉārkkiṉiyar kūṟiyirukkiṉṟaṉar. īṇṭu muppuḷḷi yeṉak kūṟiṉ avvaṭivu ......iṅṅaṉamō, aṉṟi uṅṅaṉamō, vēṟeṅṅaṉamō eḻutap paṭum eṉa aiyam nikaḻumākaliṉ, atu nikaḻāmaip poruṭṭu 'muppāṟpuḷḷi' eṉa āciriyar kūṟi vaittaṉar. nacciṉārkkiṉiyar 'aṭuppuk kūṭṭup pōla' eṉa uvamaṅkāṭṭi ataṉai viḷakkiṉar. āytameṉṉum peyarkku avar kāraṇaṅ kūṟiṟṟilar. piṟkālattē naṉṉūṟku uraiyeḻutap pukuntār cilar nacciṉārkkiṉiyariṉ karuttiṉaiyaṟiya māṭṭārāy ataṉait tiriyakkoṇṭu, 'aṭuppuk kūṭṭup pōla oruvakai āyuta vaṭivāka mūṉṟu puḷḷi vaittu eḻutappaṭutalāl āytameṉṟu peyar. āytam - āyutam;  iṅkē pōrk karuviyākiya kēṭakameṉṉum paṭaiyum, camaittaṟ karuviyākiya aṭuppum.' eṉṟu tammaṉam pōṉavāṟu kūṟuvārāyiṉar. iṅṅaṉam oru neṟippāṭumiṉṟit taṉittamiḻc collākiya āytam eṉpataṉaip paṭaikkalap potumai yuṇarttum āyutam eṉṉum vaṭacolleṉak koṇṭu nalintu poruḷ kūṟuvatu ārāyccikkum aṟivukkum ciṟitum ottataṉṟu. āyiṉ, āytameṉpataṉ poruḷtāṉ yātō eṉiṉ, āytal eṉpatu nuṇukutal eṉṉum poruṭṭākaliṉ nuṇukiya ōcaiyuṭaiyatu eṉpatu poruḷātal vēṇṭum. āytal apporuṭṭātalai
"	ōytal āytal niḻattal cāay
āvayiṉ nāṉkum uḷḷataṉ nuṇukkam"1
eṉṉum uriyiyal cūttirattāṉaṟika.
 mutal cārpu
iṉi, uyir, mey eṉpavaṟṟai mutaleḻutteṉṟum, kuṟṟiyalikaram mutaliyavaṟṟaic cārpeḻutteṉṟum piṉṉuḷōr kūṟivarukiṉṟaṉar. tolkāppiyaṉār akaramutal ṉakaraviṟuvāy muppataṉaiyum eḻutteṉṟataṉṟi, mutaleḻutteṉṟu kūṟiṟṟilar. kuṟṟiyalikaram mutaliya mūṉṟaṉaiyum cārntu varuvaṉa eṉakkūṟi, avaiyum eḻuttukkaḷōṭu oru nikaraṉa eṉṟār. eṉiṉum 'mūṉṟutalai yiṭṭamuppatiṟ ṟeḻuttiṉ' eṉac
cārpiṟṟōṟṟamum eḻutteṉṉum peyarāl āḷappaṭṭatu. āyiṉ, eḻutteṉappaṭupa muppattumūṉṟu eṉak kūṟātu 'muppaḵteṉpa......mūṉṟalaṅkaṭaiyē' eṉak kūṟiyatu eṉṉaiyeṉiṉ, ammūṉṟum taṉittēṉum, cāriyaiyoṭu poruntiyēṉum varutaliṉṟi, moḻiyaic cārntē varutalākiya ciṟappiṉmaiyaip paṟṟi eṉka. iṅṅaṉam ciṟappum ciṟappiṉmaiyum paṟṟi vēṟu pirittukkūṟuvatu āciriyarkkiyaṟkai yeṉpataṉai, 'colleṉap paṭupa peyarē viṉaiyeṉ ṟāyiraṇ ṭeṉpa vaṟinticiṉōrē' eṉavum, 'iṭaiccoṟ kiḷaviyum uriccoṟkiḷaviyum avaṟṟu vaḻimaruṅkiṟ ṟōṉṟu meṉpa', eṉavum coṟkaḷaip pirittōtutaṉ mutaliyavaṟṟāṉ aṟiyalākum. kuṟṟiyalikaram mutaliya mūṉṟum uyirum meyyumpōla attuṇaic ciṟappuṭaiyaṉa valla veṉpatē āciriyar karuttātal 'cārntuvaraṉ marapiṉ mūṉṟu' eṉa mutaliṟ kūṟiyataṉaiyē 'cārntuvariṉallatu tamakkiyal pilaveṉat', tērntuveḷippaṭutta ēṉai mūṉṟum, eṉap piṟappiyaluḷ valiyuṟuttik kūṟalāṉum naṉku tuṇiyappaṭum. avvāṟu kūṟiṉā rāyiṉum, mutaṟ cūttirattu 'eḻutteṉappaṭupa ...... muppaḵteṉpa... ...mūṉṟalaṅkaṭaiyē' eṉa, mūṉṟu mallāviṭattu muppatuñ ciṟantaṉa eṉpataṉāl, mūṉṟumē muppatiṉuñ ciṟantaṉa eṉapporuḷ kōṭalum amaiyumālō eṉiṉ, aṅṅaṉam poruḷ koḷḷāmaikkaṉṟē aṭutta cūttirattu avaṟṟai 'eḻuttōraṉṉa eṉa ōtiṉārāyiṟṟeṉpatu. iṉṟēl 'eḻuttōraṉṉa' eṉpatu poruḷil kūṟṟāmāṟu kāṇka. ivvuṇmai 'eḻuttōraṉṉa' veṉa vēṇṭā kūṟiyavataṉāṉ, muṉ 'eṉappaṭupa' eṉṟa ciṟappu ammūṉṟaṟkuṅ koḷḷak kiṭantamaiyiṉ, atu vilakkutal peṟutu meṉpatu eṉa iḷampūraṇarālum, iṅṅaṉamē nacciṉārkkiṉiyarālum viḷakkappeṟṟiruttaluṅ kāṇka. aṟṟāyiṉum, cārpiṟṟōṟṟa meṉpaṉa mūṉṟumē evaṟṟiṉuñ ciṟappuṭaiyaṉa veṉṟu kāṇkiṉṟāmeṉiṉ, aṅṅaṉamāyiṉ atu putiyatōrārāycciyiṉ payaṉeṉṟē koḷḷappaṭum. yāmum avvārāycciyiṉ payaṉai iḻattaṟku oruppaṭuvēmallēm. tamiḻp paṟṟuṭaiyār palarum aṉṉar eṉiṉum, ataṉait tolkāppiyaṉār karuttāka vaittu nalintu poruḷ kōṭaṟkum, putiya ārāycciyiṉ payaṉāl uḷatām perumaiyai ārāycciyāḷarkkaṉṟit tolkāppiyaṉārk kaḷittaṟkum karututal periyatōriḻukkāmeṉka.
iṉi, tolkāppiyaṉār cārpiṟṟōṟṟam mūṉṟeṉak kūṟiyiruppavum, naṉṉūlār cārpeḻuttup patteṉpārāyiṉar.
'	uyirmey āytam uyiraḷapu oṟṟaḷapu- 
aḵkiya i u ai oḷa maḵkāṉ -
taṉinilai pattuñ cārpeḻuttākum'1
eṉpatu naṉṉūl.
yāpparuṅkalamuṭaiyār uyirmey, aḷapeṭai, ai oḷa makāraṅkaḷiṉ kuṟukkam ivaṟṟait taṉittaṉi eḻutteṉa vēṇṭiṉarāyiṉum āytakkuṟukkameṉa oṉṟu koṇṭilar. eṉiṉum, yāpparuṅkalavirutti mēṟkōṭ cūttiraṅkaḷ cilavaṟṟil āytak kuṟukkamum kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ilakkaṇa viḷakkamuṭaiyār cārpeḻuttu ōṉpateṉak koṇṭaṉar. āytak kuṟukkaṅ koṇṭilar. ataṉ uraikārar, tolkāppiyaṉār kūṟiṟṟila rākaliṉ āytak kuṟukkameṉa oṉṟu iṉṟeṉṟiyampiṉar. iṅṅaṉam cārpeḻuttukkaḷ paṟṟik karuttu vēṟupaṭu vārellām tolkāppiyaṉāratu āṇai kaṭaippiṭit talaip perumaiyākak koḷḷum oru peṟṟiyarākaliṉ, yāmum tolkāppiyaratu karuttiṉai ārāyntu kāṇaṟpālam.
tolkāppiyaṉār kuṟṟiyalikaram, kuṟṟiyalukaram, āytam, uyiraḷapeṭai, makarakkuṟukkam, uyirmey eṉpavaṟṟai nūṉ marapiṟ potuvakaiyālum, moḻimarapiṟ ciṟappu vakaiyālum kūṟiyuḷḷār. ivaṟṟuṭ kuṟṟiyalukaram kuṟṟiyalukarappuṇariyaluḷ mēlum ciṟappākak kūṟappeṟṟuḷḷatu. aikāra oḷakāraṅkaḷ kuṟukumeṉpataṉai moḻi marapilum, oṟṟaḷapeṭaiyaic ceyyuḷiyalilum kūṟiyuḷḷār. āytaṅ kuṟukumeṉa yāṇṭuṅ kūṟiṟṟilar. iṅṅaṉam piṟar cārpeḻutteṉak koṇṭa oṉpatu tolkāppiyarāṟ kūṟappaṭṭiruppiṉum, avar cārpeḻuttu mūṉṟēyeṉṟum, avaṟṟuṭaṉāka eḻuttukkaḷ muppattumūṉṟē yeṉṟum  varaiyaṟai ceytu koṇṭār. ataṉ kāraṇam eṉṉai yeṉpatē iṅku ārāytaṟkuriyatu. uyirmeyyai ōreḻuttākak koḷḷutal poruntāteṉac civañāṉa muṉivar tamatu tolkāppiyac cūttira viruttiyil naṉku viḷakkiyuḷḷār. atu,
"iṉi im mūṉṟumēyaṉṟi uyirmey mutaliyavaṟṟaiyum cārpeḻutteṉpāru muḷarālōveṉiṉ, āl eṉpuḻi uyir muṉṉum mey piṉṉum niṉṟu mayaṅkiṉāṟpōla, lā eṉpuḻiyum meymuṉṉum uyir piṉṉum niṉṟumayaṅkiṉavēyallatu, uyiru meyyumākiya tantaṉmai tirintu vēṟākāmaikku 'meyyōṭiyaiyiṉu muyiriya ṟiriyā' eṉṟaṟ ṟoṭakkattuc cūttiraṅkaḷē cāṉṟākalāṉ, uyirmeyyākiya kālattum kuṟiṉmai neṭiṉmai yeṉṉum uyirttaṉmaiyum, vaṉmai meṉmai iṭaimai yeṉṉum meyttaṉmaiyum tammiyalpiṟ ṟirivu paṭāmaiyāṉum, uṭaṉ mēluyir vantoṉṟutal poṉmaṇi pōla iyalpu puṇarcciyā meṉpavākalāṉum, oṟṟumai nayattāṉ uyirmey yeṉpataṉait takara ñāḻal pōla ummait tokai nilaikkaḷattup piṟanta aṉmoḻittokai yeṉpārkkut takaramum ñāḻaluṅ kūṭiya cāntu piṉṉart takaramu ñāḻalu mākātavāṟu pōla meyyuyir nīṅkiṟṟaṉṉuruvātal poruntāmaiyāṉum, tuṇaṅkai yeṉpatu meymmutal uyirīṟu meymmayakkameṉavum varaku eṉpatu uyirttoṭar moḻik kuṟṟiyalukara meṉavum koḷvataṉṟi, uyirmey mutal uyirmeyyīṟu, uyirmeymayakkam, uyirmeyt toṭar moḻik kuṟṟiyalukaram. eṉak koḷḷāmaiyiṉ oṟṟumai nayampaṟṟi yoṉṟeṉpataṉāloru payaṉiṉmai
yāṉum, meykaḷellām uyirvantoṉṟātu taṉittu niṉṟa vaḻiyē māttirai koḷḷavum eḻutteṉṉavum paṭumeṉpatu avaṟṟiyal pākaliṉ atupaṟṟiyoṉṟeṉal vēṇṭāmai
yāṉum, avviyal paṟintu kōṭaṟkut taṉittu niṉṟameyyai yoṟṟeṉavum puḷḷiyeṉavum uyirōṭu kūṭiya meyyai uyirmeyyeṉavum ciṟappup peyariṭṭāḷutal āciriyar karuttākalāṉum, uyirōṭu kūṭiyaviṭattu vari vaṭivuvēṟu
paṭutaliṉ atupaṟṟip 'puḷḷiyillāvellā meyyum' eṉa meymēl vaittuc cūttirañ ceytu vaṭiveḻuttilakkaṇaṅ kūṟiṉāraṉṟi oliyeḻuttilakkaṇam vēṟupaṭak kūṟāmai
yāṉum - atu poruntāteṉa maṟukka" eṉpatu.
iṅṅaṉam uyirmey ōreḻuttākāteṉpataṟkuc civañāṉa muṉivar kūṟiya kāraṇaṅkaḷellām mikka valiyuṭaiyaṉa veṉpatil aiyamillai. eṉiṉum, kā mutaliyaṉa ōreḻuttoru moḻiyeṉak koḷḷappaṭutalāṉum, ceyyuḷiyalil, acai koḷḷumiṭattum piṟāṇṭum uyirmeyyai ōreḻutteṉavē koḷḷavēṇṭi iruttalāṉum uyirmeyyai oruvāṟṟāl ōreḻuttākak koḷḷutalum āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉārkkuṭaṉ pāṭeṉpatu peṟṟām. ataṉāṟṟāṉ piṉṉūlāciriyarkaḷ uyirmeyyaic cārpeḻuttuḷoṉṟākak koṇṭaṉarāvar. eṉiṉum, eḻuttiṉ taṉmaiyai nōkkuḻi ataṉkaṇ uyirum meyyum vēṟuvēṟu pulappaṭa niṟṟalāṉum' uyirmeyyeṉa oṉṟu koḷḷātu kā eṉpatu k ā eṉa eḻutappaṭiṉum, eḻututaṟkum payilutaṟkum ciṟitu varuttamāvataṉṟi vēṟoriḻukku
miṉmaiyāṉum tolkāppiyaṉār uyirmeyyai vēṟeḻuttāka vaitteṇṇiṟṟilar. iṅṅaṉamē vaṭanūlār mutalāyiṉāruñ uyirum meyyuñ cērntataṉai ōreḻuttāka vaitteṇṇāmaiyum kāṇka.


10. oru kuṟaṭpā
"	nattampōṟ kēṭu muḷatākuñ cākkāṭum
vittakark kallā laritu"1
itu teyvap pulamait tiruvaḷḷuvatēvar aruḷiya tirukkuṟaḷiṟ pukaḻ eṉṉum atikārattiluḷḷatu. itaṟkup parimēlaḻakar kūṟiya poḻippurai "pukaḻuṭampiṟku ākkamākum kēṭum, pukaḻuṭampu uḷatākum cākkāṭum caturappāṭuṭaiyārkkallatu illai" eṉpatu.
vaṟumaiyāl uṭampai meliyac ceytu pukaḻai vaḷarttalum, uṭampait tuṟantu pukaḻai nilaipeṟac ceytalum vittakarkkē kūṭumeṉpatu parimēlaḻakar karuttu; itu avarālēyē naṉku viḷakkappeṟṟuḷḷatu. "nantu eṉṉun toḻiṟ peyar vikārat tāl nattu eṉṟāy, piṉ am eṉṉum pakutip poruḷ vikuti peṟṟu nattam eṉṟāyiṟṟu. pōl eṉpatu īṇṭu uraiyacai eṉa nattam, pōl eṉṉum irucoṟkaḷukkum avar ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟiyuḷḷār.
kālattāṟ parimēlaḻakarkku muntiyavareṉṟu karutappaṭukiṟa maṇakkuṭavar, ippāṭṭiṟku "ākkam pōlak kēṭum uḷatāṉāṟ pōlac cātalum vallavaṟ kallatu aritu" eṉa urai kūṟi "itu pukaḻpaṭa vāḻtal makkaḷellārkkum ariteṉṟatu" eṉak karutturaittuḷḷār. maṇakkuṭavar tam karuttai naṉku viḷakkiṟṟila rāyiṉum, iruvar karuttum muraṇpaṭāmai kāṇaṟpālatu. nattam eṉpataṟku ākkameṉṟē iruvarum poruḷ kūṟiṉar. parimēlaḻakar pōl eṉpataṉai uraiyacaiyeṉak koṇṭu ākum eṉpataṉai muṉṉuṅkūṭṭiṉamaiyum, maṇakkuṭavar pōl eṉpataṉai uvamaiccollākavē niṟutti ataṉaip piṉṉum kūṭṭiṉamaiyum  tammuḷ vēṟupāṭām. vittakaṉ vittakar eṉa orumaiyum paṉmaiyumākap pāṭaṅkoṇṭamaiyum ām.
iṉi, peyaraṟiyappaṭāta oruvaraturaiyuṭaṉ kūṭiya tirukkuṟaṭ paṉaiyēṭṭuc cuvaṭiyoṉṟu eṉṉiṭam uḷatu. avvurai parimēlaḻakar mutalāyiṉāriṉ uraiyuṭaṉ cērtteṇṇaṟ kēṟṟa takutiyuṭaiyatākat tōṉṟāviṭiṉum, cila kāraṇaṅ kaḷāl aṉṉōr uraikkum muṟpaṭṭatāmō eṉa aiyuṟattakka tākavuḷatu. ciṟcila pākkaṭkup piṇṭamākak karuttu māttiramē kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. vēṟupaṭṭa pāṭaṅkaḷum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ataṉkaṇ ippāviṟku eḻutappaṭṭirukkum urai:-
"caṅkāyirañ cūḻavāḻum valampuripōlē kiḷaiyāṉatu taṉṉaic cūḻa vāḻvatu kīrttimāṉukkukkaivarum eṉṟavāṟu" eṉṟatē. itiliruntu ippāviluḷḷa 'kēṭu uḷatākuñ cākkāṭum' eṉṉuñ coṟkaṭkup poruḷaṟiyak kūṭavillai; eṉiṉum, 'nattam' eṉpataṟku 'valampuri' eṉap poruḷ koḷḷappaṭṭiruppatu veḷiyākiṟatu.
iṉi, cīvakacintāmaṇiyil "caṅkuṭaintaṉaiya veṇṭāmarai malart taṭaṅkaḷ pōlum-naṅkuṭi" (civakacīntā - 547) eṉpataṟku "taṭaṅkaḷilē uṭainta taṉmaiyavākiya malarum caṅkum pōlum naṅkuṭi. itu tūymaikkuvamai; iṉic caṅku keṭṭālum niṟaṅkeṭātatu pōlak keṭṭālun taṉṟaṉmai keṭāta kuṭiyumām; 'nattampōṟ kōṭum' eṉpa" eṉa nacciṉārkkiṉiyar urai kūṟiyuḷḷār. piṟporuḷil caṅku uṭaintaṉaiyakuṭi eṉa iyaiyum; caṅku keṭṭālum niṟaṅ keṭātatu pōlak keṭṭāluntaṉṟaṉmai keṭātakuṭi eṉṟu kūṟiyavar ataṟku mēṟkōḷāka  'nattampōṟ kēṭum' eṉpataṉaik kāṭṭiyiruppati liruntu, avar nattam eṉpataṟkuc caṅkeṉṟu poruḷ koṇṭuḷḷamai pulaṉām. 'uḷatākuñ cākkāṭum' eṉpataṟku nacciṉārkkiṉiyar koṇṭa poruḷ iṉṉateṉa veḷippaṭāviṭiṉum, 'nattam pōṟkēṭum' eṉpataṟku avar koṇṭa poruḷ' pukaḻ kuṉṟutalillāta vaṟumai eṉpatākal vēṇṭum. pukaḻ, kuṭiyiṉṟaṉmai, aṉṟi vittakar taṉmai ākum. eṉavē ikkuṟaḷukku maṇakkuṭavarum parimēlaḻakarum koṇṭa karuttuṭaṉ nacciṉārkkiṉiyar karuttu māṟupaṭṭilatu eṉpatum, eṉiṉum nacciṉārkkiṉiyar nattam eṉpataṟkuc caṅkeṉṟu poruḷ koṇṭu ataṉai uvamai yākkiyuḷḷāreṉpatum pōtarutal kāṇka.
yāṉ ciṟiyaṉāyt tiṇṇaippaḷḷiyil tirukkuṟaḷ paṭittu vanta poḻutu eṉ tantaiyār mutalāyiṉōr 'nattam' eṉpataṟku nattai yeṉṟum, caṅkeṉṟum ētētō poruḷ kūṟak kēṭṭuḷēṉ. āṉāl appoḻutu avarkaḷ poruntamuṟap poruḷ colliyatāka nāṉ niṉaikkavillai aḵtev vāṟāyiṉum nattam eṉpataṟkuc caṅkeṉum poruḷ uccimēṟ pulavarkoḷ nacciṉārkkiṉiyarālum koḷḷappaṭṭuḷḷateṉa 
ippoḻutaṟiyalāṉēṉ.


11. nantampōṟkēṭu eṉṉum
 kuṟaḷurai ārāycci
'oru kuṟaṭpā' eṉat talaippeyariṭṭu yāṉeḻutiya kaṭṭurai yoṉṟu 'tamiḻp poḻil' tuṇar-1 pakkam 429-431il veḷivantatu. ataṉai maṟuttu, "nattampōṟkēṭu eṉṉuṅ kuṟaḷurai" eṉṉun talaippiṉkīḻ, tiruvāḷar T.N. appaṉaiyaṅkār avarkaḷ tamiḻp poḻilukku eḻutiya kaṭṭurai eṉpārvaikku vantatu. ciṟṟaṟivum ciṟu kēḷviyumuṭaiya yāṉ palaviṉaikaḷuk kiṭaippaṭṭa poḻutukaḷil eḻutivarum iṉṉōraṉṉa uraikaḷil iṟappavum palavāya piḻaikaḷiruttal iyalpē. mūtaṟivuṭaiya periyārkaḷ eṉ uraikaḷilum karuttaic celutti ārāyntu, avaṟṟiluḷḷa piḻaikaḷaiyeṭuttuk kāṭṭuvārkaḷāyiṉ, atu eṉakkup periyatōr ūtiyamā meṉpatiṟ ciṟitum aiyamillai. aiyaṅkāravarkaḷ eḻutiyatu attaṉmaiyatāyiṉ avarkaṭku eṉ makiḻcciyaiyum naṉṟiyaṟivaiyum terivittu amaiyum attuṇaiyaṉṟiyāṉ ceyyaṟpālatu piṟitillai; eṉ poḻutum vaṟitākātu. āṉāl, avarkaḷ eṉ karuttiṉait teḷiya aṟiyāmalum, maṇakkuṭavar, nacciṉārkkiṉiyar mutalāyiṉāriṉ uraip poruḷkaḷaik kāṇāmalum, muṉṉoṭupiṉ muraṇumāṟum, tamiḻ vaḷarccikkut taṭaipayakku muṟaiyilum eḻutiyiruttaliṉ, kaṟpār malaivuṟāvaṇṇam avaṟṟai viḷakkutal eṉ kaṭaṉāyiṟṟu.
"	nattampōṟ kēṭu muḷatākuñ cākkāṭum vittakark kallā laritu"1
eṉṉuṅ kuṟaḷukku maṇakkuṭavarum, parimēlaḻakarum eḻutiya uraiyiṉ oṟṟumai vēṟṟumaikaḷai nāṉ naṉku eṭuttuk kāṭṭiyirukkiṟēṉ. maṇakkuṭavar tam karuttai naṉku viḷakkiṟṟila rāyiṉum, iruvar karuttum muraṇpaṭāmai kāṇaṟpālatu' eṉa nāṉ eḻutiyiruppatu aiyaṅkāravarkaḷukkup poruttamākat tōṉṟavillai. avarkaḷ ‘ivviruvaruraiyum orē karuttuṭaiyaṉa eṉṟal evvāṟu poruntu meṉpatu viḷaṅkavillai' eṉṟum, ‘ākkam pōlakkēṭu' uḷatāṉāṟpōlac cātal eṉpaṉa teḷivāyp poruḷuṇarak kiṭappaṉavāmō' eṉṟum kūṟip pōntu, piṉ, ākkampōlakkēṭu' eṉṟataṉai ‘ākkam pōlat tōṉṟuṅkēṭu' eṉa virittup poruḷ ceytu pārttum, attoṭarai muṉpiṉṉāka māṟik ‘kēṭu pōṟ ṟōṉṟum ākkam' eṉa vaittup poruḷ ceytu pārttum, muṭivil avviru poruḷum poruntāveṉak kaḻittu, avvaḷaviṉamaiyātu, ‘ātalāl ataṉai uvamac collākavē niṟutti uṇmaip poruḷ kāṇutal eḷitiṉiyalāta teṉpatu teḷivāyiṟṟeṉka' eṉṟuraittaṉar. itiliruntu, maṇakkuṭavaruraiyiṉ karuttai avar aṟintu koḷḷavillai yeṉpatum, ataṉait tammāl aṟiya muṭiyavillaiyeṉa avar ottuk koṇṭiruppatum veḷiyākiṉṟaṉa. eṉavē, ‘eḷitiṉiyalāta teṉpatu teḷivāyiṟ ṟeṉka' eṉṟu kūṟiyatu avaraḷavil uṇmaiyē. piṟarukkum iyalātateṉak kūṟutal muṟaiyaṉṟākaliṉ, akkaruttuṭaṉ kūṟiyatākak koḷḷutal vēṇṭā. maṇakkuṭavar kūṟiya ‘ākkampōlakkēṭum' eṉṉun toṭarukku, ākkampōlumāṟu keṭuvatum eṉpatē poruḷ. atu oruvāṟṟāṟkēṭāyiṉum, ākkam payappatākaliṉ ākkampōlak karutappaṭuvatāyiṟṟu. iṉṉum viḷakkamākak kūṟiṉ, ākkattuḷ vaitteṇṇattakum kēṭum, nilaipēṟṟil vaitteṇṇattakum cāvum vallavaṟ kallatu ariteṉpatu maṇakkuṭavar uraip poruḷākum.
"	oppuravi ṉālvaruṅ kēṭeṉi ṉaḵtoruvaṉ
viṟṟukkōṭ ṭakka tuṭaittu"1
eṉṉuṅ kuṟaḷum,
"	vīntava reṉpavar vīntava rēṉum
īntava ralla tiruntavar yārē"2
eṉṉum kamparāmāyaṇac ceyyuṭ pakutiyum mēl ārāynta uraik karuttiṉai viḷakkattakuvaṉa. aiyaṅkāravarkaḷukku itu pulaṉākāmaiyāl ‘ākkampōlat tōṉṟuṅkēṭu' ‘kēṭupōṟ ṟōṉṟum ākkam' eṉṟiṅṅaṉam iṭarppaṭṭuraittu, maṇak kuṭavarurai parimēlaḻakaruraiyuṭaṉ muraṇuvateṉṟum, avaruraiyiṉ uṇmaipporuḷ kāṇaviyalāteṉṟum palavāṟu kūṟuvārāyiṉar. ‘ākkampōlak kēṭu' eṉṉum toṭariṉ poruḷ avarkku eḷitiṉ viḷaṅkavillaiyēṉum, "itu pukaḻpaṭa vāḻtal makkaḷellārkkum ariteṉṟatu' eṉa maṇakkuṭavar karutturaittatiṟ karuttūṉṟiyirupparēl ittakaiya maruṭci avarkku uṇṭākāmaliruttaluṅ kūṭum. pukaḻaip perukac ceytalum pukaḻai niṟuttalum ellārkkum iyalvataṉṟu eṉpatē parimēlaḻakar uraiyiṉ karuttu. maṇakkuṭavarum pukaḻpaṭa vāḻtal ellārkkum aritu eṉṟē kūṟiyirukkiṟār. iṅṅaṉamirukka iruvar karuttum muraṇpaṭāmai kāṇaṟpālatu' eṉa yāṉeḻutiyatil iḻukkeṉṉuḷatu?
iṉi, tirukkuṟaṭ paṉaiyēṭṭuccuvaṭi yoṉṟiliruntu nāṉ eṭuttukkāṭṭiya maṟṟoruvar uraik kuṟippiṉai ārāyvāṉpukku, ataṉait tirukkuṟaḷiṉ nērāṉa patavurai  eṉṟātal aṉṟip poḻippurai yeṉṟātal koḷḷa iṭamillai. maṟṟu atu ētō oruvakaiyāṉa karuttiṉaip pulappaṭukkiṉṟa oru pōliyurai yeṉavē puṟakkaṇikkattakkatu' eṉṟaṉar. 'avvurai parimēlaḻakar mutalāyiṉāriṉ uraiyuṭaṉ cērtteṇṇaṟ kēṟṟa takutiyuṭaiyatākat tōṉṟāviṭiṉum' eṉa yāṉ kūṟiyiruppavum avar avvuraiyiṉai ‘oru pōliyurai' eṉpataṉāṟpōnta payaṉeṉṉai? avvuraiyai eṭutteḻuti "itiliruntu, ippāviluḷḷa ‘kēṭum uḷatākum cākkāṭum' eṉṉuñ coṟkaṭkup poruḷaṟiyakkūṭavillai' eṉa yāṉē kūṟiyiruppavum, avar, ‘tirukkuṟaḷiṉ perumpāṉmaiyāṉa coṟkaḷukku avvuraiyiṟ poruḷ kāṇumāṟillai' eṉṟuraippatu payaṉil ceyalām. "caṅkāyirañ cūḻavāḻum valampuripōlak kiḷaiyāṉatu taṉṉaic cūḻa vāḻvatu kīrttimāṉukkuk kaivarum' eṉṉum avvuraiyaiyum ūṉṟi nōkkiṉ, atu, kiḷaiyāyiṉār mutalāṉōr taṉṉaiccūḻa avarkkup poruḷai yīntu vaṟumaiyuṟuvatu pukaḻpeṟum paṇpuṭaiyāṉukkē iyalvatām eṉa nalintēṉum poruḷ koḷḷa iṭantantu niṟkiṉṟatu. ‘uḷatākuñ cākkāṭum' eṉpataṟkē itiṟ poruḷ kāṇappaṭavillai. ‘nattam pōl' eṉpataṟku ‘valampuripōla' eṉṟu avvurai kūṟuvatu yāvarkkum eḷitiṉ viḷaṅkaṟpālatākavum, aiyaṅkāravarkaḷ ataṉai maṟup pataṟkum periyatōr ārāycciyai mēṟkoḷvatu viyappā kiṉṟatu. maṟṟu, avar 'ātalāl nattameṉṉuñ coṟ poruḷ aṟitaṟku ivvuraiyaiyē pōtiya cāṉṟākak koḷḷutal iyalāteṉka' eṉkiṉṟaṉar. nattameṉṉuñ coṟporuḷ tuṇitaṟku ivvuraiyiṉaiyē pōtiya cāṉṟākak koṇṭirun tālaṉṟō ataṉai iṅṅaṉam maṟutturaikka vēṇṭum? 'ivvuraiyil nattameṉpataṟku valampuriyeṉap poruḷ koḷḷappaṭṭiruppatu veḷiyākiṟatu' eṉpatum, "nattameṉṉuñ coṟporuḷ tuṇitaṟku ivvuraiyē pōtiya cāṉṟu" eṉpatum orē karuttuṭaiyaṉaveṉak koṇṭaṉar pōlum?
iṉi, cīvakacintāmaṇiyil oru toṭarukku nacciṉārkkiṉiyar eḻutiya uraiyiṉai eṭuttukkāṭṭi, avar karuttituveṉa nāṉ viḷakkiyiruntataṟku maṟuppāka aiyaṅkāravarkaḷ eḻutiyuḷḷavaṟṟai nōkkuḻi, avar nilaimaikkup peritum iraṅka nērkiṉṟatu. "caṅkuṭaintaṉaiya veṇṭāmarai malarttaṭaṅkaḷ pōlum- naṅkuṭi" eṉpataṟku nacciṉārkkiṉiyar "taṭaṅkaḷilē uṭainta taṉmaiyavākiya malarum caṅkum pōlum naṅkuṭi; itu tūymaikkuvamai" eṉa mutaliṟ kūṟip, piṉ, iṉic caṅku keṭṭālum niṟaṅkeṭātatu pōlak keṭṭālun taṉṟaṉmai keṭāta kuṭiyumām; 'nattampōṟ kēṭum' eṉpa" eṉṟuraittaṉar. "piṟporuḷil caṅku uṭaintaṉaiya kuṭi eṉa iyaiyum. caṅku keṭṭālum niṟaṅ keṭātatu pōlak keṭṭālun taṉṟaṉmai keṭātakuṭi eṉṟu kūṟiyavar ataṟku mēṟkōḷāka 'nattam pōṟ kēṭum' eṉpataṉaik kāṭṭiyiruppatiliruntu, avar nattam eṉpataṟkuc caṅkeṉṟu poruḷ koṇṭuḷḷamai pulaṉām' eṉayāṉ eḻutiyiruntēṉ. "iraṇṭuraiyilum ‘uṭaintaṉaiya' eṉpatu malarukku aṭai" eṉa aiyaṅkāravarkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. nacciṉārkkiṉiyar entavuraiyilum coṟkaḷaip poruḷ kōḷ muṟaiyil muṉṉiṟuttip piṉ poruḷuraikkavillai. avar poruḷuraittiruppatiliruntē coṟkaḷai iṉṉavāṟu iyaiyavaittirukkiṉṟaṉaraṉe nām aṟital vēṇṭum. mutaluraiyil, taṭaṅkaḷ uṭaintaṉaiya veṇṭāmarai malar caṅkupōlum nam kuṭi eṉa avar coṟkaḷai iyaiya vaittiruk kiṉṟaṉar  eṉpatu  avvuraiyaik koṇṭutāṉē aṟiya vēṇṭiyuḷḷatu? avvāṟāka, ‘iṉic caṅku keṭṭālum niṟaṅkeṭātatu pōlak keṭṭālun taṉṟaṉmai keṭāta kuṭiyumām' eṉṉum uraiyiliruntu, caṅku uṭaintaṉaiya kuṭi eṉa avar coṟkaḷai iyaittirukkiṉṟaṉareṉpatu aṟivuṭaiyār evarkkum eḷitiṟ pulaṉāva toṉṟākavum, ‘piṟa poruḷil caṅku uṭaintaṉaiya kuṭiyeṉa iyaiyum eṉṟal avaruraikku māṟukoḷak kūṟalām' eṉa aiyaṅkāravarkaḷ kūṟiyateṉṉai? māṟukoḷakkūṟal eṉpataṉai avar yāṅṅaṉam aṟintaṉar? uṭaintaṉaiya eṉpataṉai malarukkēṟṟi, uṭainta taṉmaiyavākiya eṉa iṭarppaṭṭuraikkum muṟ poruḷilum, caṅku uṭaintāṟ pōlum kuṭi eṉṉum piṟporuḷē ciṟantatāmeṉka. 'uṭainta kālattu niṟam vēṟupaṭāmai maṟṟellāp poruḷkaḷiṭattu miruppavum ataṟkuc caṅkiṉaiyē eṭuttuk kāṭṭutal ēṟpuṭaiya taṉṟām' eṉkiṉṟaṉar. cuṭṭa kālattu niṟaṅkuṉṟāmai poṉ mutaliya piṟa poruḷkaḷiṭattu miruppavum ataṟkuc caṅkiṉaiyē eṭuttukkāṭṭutal ēṟpuṭaiyataṉṟām eṉpārkku ivar yātu kūṟuvar? poṉṉiṉ ṟaṉmai atuvātal "cuṭaccuṭarum poṉ pōl" eṉat tamiḻ maṟaiyuḷḷum kūṟappaṭutal kāṇka.
iṉi, "nattampōṟkēṭu" eṉpataṟkup parimēlaḻakaruraiyait taḻuviyē nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum ākkamākuṅkēṭu eṉap poruḷ karutiyuḷḷāreṉṟalum āṇṭaik kēṟpuṭaiyatākiṉṟatu. keṭṭālum taṉ taṉmai keṭātakuṭi eṉakkuṭiyiṉ perumaiyai mēlē kūṟippōntavar. apporuḷai valiyuṟuttaṟku, aṅṅaṉam vaṟumaiyāṟ kēṭaṭainta kālattum tam taṉmaiyiṟkuṉṟātu ākkamuṟṟu viḷaṅkutal pukaḻpaṭa vāḻuṅkuṭip piṟantār kaṇṇē uḷatām eṉṉuṅ karuttuḷḷa ‘nattam------ laritu' eṉṉun tirukkuṟaḷai mutaṟ kuṟippiṉāl niṉai'd2vūṭṭic ceṉṟa vareṉak koḷḷutalil yātōriḻukkumiṉṟu' eṉkiṉṟaṉar. itil 'parimēlaḻakaruraiyait taḻuviyē' eṉap puṟappaṭṭavar piṉ avaruraiyai naṭṭāṟṟil viṭṭuviṭṭamai kāṇalākum. ‘vaṟumaiyāṟ kēṭaṭainta kālattum tam taṉmaiyiṟ kuṉṟātu ākkamuṟṟu viḷaṅkutal pukaḻpaṭa vāḻum kuṭippiṟantār kaṇṇē uḷatām eṉpatutāṉā parimēlaḻakar koṇṭa poruḷ? vaṟumai yeṉpatu yātu? kēṭeṉpatu yātu? iṅṅaṉam vaḻuppaṭak kūṟiyiruppaṉa pala. avaṟṟai ārāytal vīṇ kālappōkkē. tirukkuṟaḷai mutaṟkuṟippiṉāl niṉaippūṭṭic ceṉṟaṉar eṉa aiyaṅkāravarkaḷ kūṟuvatu kuṟi kūṟuvatē pōlum. akkuṟitāṉ poyk kuṟiyātal iṅkē kāṭṭappaṭum. nacciṉārkkiṉiyar mēṟkōḷ eṭuttāḷum muṟaimaiyaic cīvaka cintāmaṇiyiṉ mutaṟ ceyyuḷuraiyaip paṭitta tuṇaiyāṉē aṟintu koḷḷalākum. avar oru ceyyuḷō cūttiramō muḻuvatum mēṟkōḷākakkāṭṭappaṭa vēṇṭiṉ ataṉai aṅṅaṉamē muḻutum eṭutteḻutiyiruppar: aṉṟi ataṉ mutalum iṟutiyum  eḻutiyiruppar: aṉṟi mutaliluḷḷa collaiyō toṭaraiyō eḻuti, eṉṉuñ ceyyuḷ, eṉṉuñ cūttiram eṉṟiṅṅaṉam viḷaṅka veḻutiyiruppar: ceyyuḷ allatu cūttirattiṉ oru pakutiyē vēṇṭumāyiṉ, eppakuti vēṇṭumō ataṉai māttiram eḻutiyiruppar; ivvuṇmaikaḷai avarurai muḻutuṅ kāṇalākum. itaṉai avar tirukkuṟaṭpākkaḷai yeṭuttāṇṭirup patiliruntē kāṭṭukiṉṟēṉ: cintāmaṇi, muttiyilampakam, 189ām ceyyuḷuraiyil "kollāmai mēṟkoṇ ṭoḻukuvāṉ vāḻnāṇ mēṟcellā tuyiruṇṇuṅ kūṟṟu" eṉa muḻutum eṭuttuk kāṭṭiṉar. avvilampakattu 292ām ceyyuḷuraiyil "ciṟaikākkuṅ kāppevaṉ ceyyum" eṉa mutaṟpakutiyaik kāṭṭiṉar. vimalaiyārilampakattu 3-ām ceyyuḷuraiyil. "oṉṉār, aḻutakaṇṇīrumaṉaittu" eṉa iṟutip pakutiyaik kāṭṭiṉar. ivaṟṟiliruntu nāṉ mēlē kūṟiyaṉa piḻaiyā vāymaiyātal teḷika. aiyaṅkāravarkaḷ, kūṟṟiṉpaṭi, ‘nattam pōṟkēṭum' eṉṉum mutaṟ kuṟippiṉāl appāṭṭu muḻutaiyumē nacciṉārkkiṉiyar uṇarttiṉareṉak koḷḷiṉ, cintāmaṇiyiṉ mutaṟ ceyyuḷuraiyil, avar "īṇṭu ulakameṉṟatu uyirkkiḻavaṉai; ‘ulakamuvappa' eṉṉum mutaṟkuṟippāl tirumurukāṟṟuppaṭai muḻutum uṇarttiṉā reṉṟaṉṟō koḷḷa vēṇṭum? maṟṟum avar pattuppāṭṭu eṭṭuttokai mutaliyavaṟṟiliruntu kāṭṭiya vellām attaṉmaiyavātal vēṇṭumē? ivaṟṟaiyellām ciṟitum ōrtaliṉṟi avar maṟuppeḻutap pukuntataṉai eṉṉeṉṟuraippatu? yāṉ itukāṟum eḻutiyavaṟṟiliruntu aiyaṅkāravarkaḷ kūṟṟu muḻutum, ciṟitēṉum uṇmai viravāta veṟum pōliyurai yeṉpatu yāvarkkum iṉitu viḷaṅkā niṟkum.
iṉi, nattam eṉṉuñ collukkuc caṅkeṉṉum poruḷ nacciṉārkkiṉiyar uraiyilaṉṟi vēṟiṭattuk kāṇappaṭu vatuṇṭō eṉpatu iṅku ārāytaṟkuriyatu tivākarattil "nattupaṇilam vaḷaināku curimukam, kampukōṭu caṅkeṉappaṭumē" eṉṟum, piṅkalattil "nantu cutti nāku paṇilam, vaṇṭu kēṭu vaḷaiyē curimukam, kampu veḷḷai iṭampuricaṅkē" eṉṟum, cūṭāmaṇi nikaṇṭil "nalantarunantu cuttinākoṭu vaḷaikampukkō, ṭilaṅkiyavāra ṇamvaṇ ṭiṭampuri veḷḷai caṅkām" eṉṟum caṅkiṉ peyarkaḷ kūṟiyavaṟṟil nantu eṉṉum peyar mutaliṭattil niṟkiṉṟatu: ippeyar ammuccāriyai peṟṟu nattam eṉṟāku meṉpatu aṟiñarellārum uṭaṉpaṭaṟ kuriyatē; maṟṟu, cūṭāmaṇi nikaṇṭil "nattamē yiruḷum ūrum nantuṭaṉ iravu nāṟpēr" eṉa nattam eṉṉum peyarkku nantu eṉṟu poruḷ kūṟappaṭṭiruttaluṅ kāṇka. immūṉṟu nikaṇṭukaḷum nacciṉārkkiṉiyarkkuk kālattāl muṟpaṭṭaṉa veṉpatum cintikkaṟpālatu.
iṉi, aiyaṅkāravarkaḷ tam kaṭṭuraiyil ōriṭattil ‘aṉṟiyum, atu ciṟappuṭaiyatāyiṉ, maṇakkuṭavarukkuk kālattiṉāṟ piṟpaṭṭa parimēlaḻakar tāmum avvāṟu uraiceytalait tavirntu, pōleṉpatai uraiyacaiyākak kūṟiviṭuttateṉṉai?' eṉṟum, piṟitōriṭattil ‘ātalāl ataṉai uccimēṟ pulavar koḷ nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum uṭaṉpaṭṭaṉareṉak kūṟutal attuṇaip poruttamuṭaiya taṉṟeṉṟu tōṉṟukiṟatu' eṉṟum kūṟiyuḷḷaṉar. iṅṅaṉam kōṭalē nam muṉaṉōrākiya pulavar perumakkaḷai nām pōṟṟumuṟaiyeṉa ikkālattilum karutuvār cilarirukkiṉṟaṉar. itu kuṟittu yāṉ mika viritteḻutuṅ karuttuṭaiyēṉāyiṉum, ivvurai viriyāmaip poruṭṭu, aḵtu ārāyccimuṟai yaṉṟeṉṟum, aṅṅaṉaṅ kōṭal tamiḻ vaḷarccikku vaḻiyaṭaiyākumeṉṟum kūṟumaḷavil niṟuttu kiṉṟēṉ. "caṅkuṭaintaṉaiya veṇṭāmarai malarttaṭaṅkaḷ pōlum, naṅkuṭi"1 eṉpataṟku nacciṉārkkiṉiyar kūṟiya poruḷkaḷ ivvārāyccikku vēṇṭun tuṇaiyākavē mēlē taḻuvappaṭṭaṉa. coṟkiṭakkai muṟaiyai ūṉṟi nōkkiṉ avar kūṟiya iru poruḷumē āciriyar karuttoṭu paṭṭaṉa eṉṟu koḷvataṟku iṭamillai. ‘caṅkuṭaintaṉaiya' eṉpataṉai malarukku aṭaiyākki, caṅku uṭaintu virintu niṉṟāṟ pōlum veṇṭāmarai malar eṉak kūṟutalē poruttamuṭaittām.
"	caṅkuṭain taṉaiyatāḻait taṭamalart toṭaiyalāṉē"2
eṉac cūḷāmaṇi kūṟuvatum itaṉai valiyuṟuttāniṟkum. maṟṟum taṭaṅkaḷ pōlum kuṭiyeṉṟu nērē poruḷ ceyvatē peritum porutta muṭaittātal kāṇka. ikkālattil, tamiḻukku akattiyarāki viḷaṅkum perun tiruviṉarākiya makāmakō pāttiyāya u.vē. cāminātaiyaravarkaḷum ik karuttuṭaiyavarkaḷeṉpataṉai, avarkaḷeḻutiya ‘cintāmaṇi ārāycci viḷakkattil, ‘caṅkuṭaintaṉaiya veṇṭāmarai malar' eṉpataṉōṭu otta karuttuṭaiyatākac, cūḷāmaṇiyiluḷḷa ‘caṅkuṭain taṉaiyatāḻai' eṉpataṉai eṭuttukkāṭṭiyiruppatum, taṭaṅkaḷpōlum kuṭi eṉap poruḷ paṭutalkuṟippiṭa,
"pāṟkaṭaṉ muḷaittatōr pavaḷap pūṅkoṭi"1
eṉṉuñ cintāmaṇic ceyyuḷai ilakkattāṟ kuṟittiruppatum naṉku viḷakkum eṉka.


12.  tamiḻiṉ ciṟappiyalpu
ulakilē ēṉaiyuyirkaḷiṉiṉṟum makkaḷai vēṟupaṭuttik kāṭṭuvatāy, makkaḷiṉ aṟivu viḷakkattiṟkuc ciṟanta karuviyā vatāy uḷḷatu moḻi. aṉṟiyum moḻiyāṉatu ataṉaip pēcivarum makkaḷiṉ karuttup poruḷkaḷaittiraṭṭi vaittirukkum karu'd2vūlamāyiruttaliṉ, ataṉ amaippu mutaliyaṉa koṇṭē ammakkaḷiṉ nākarika nilaiyai uṇartalum cālum. ippoḻutu ettaṉaiyō palanūṟumoḻikaḷ ulakattil vaḻaṅkuvaṉa eṉpa. eṉiṉum, tamiḻ, āriyam, epirēyam, kirikku, ilattīṉ, mutaliya cilamoḻikaḷē mikka paḻamaiyum perumaiyum uṭaiyaṉavāka aṟiñarkaḷāṟ koḷḷappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟuḷ iruvakai vaḻakkiṉum niṉṟu nilavuvatu tamiḻ. tamiḻiṉ ciṟappiyalpu eṉṉiṉ piṟa moḻiyiliṉṟit tamiḻiṉ maṭṭumēyuḷḷa taṉmai yeṉṟākum. piṟamoḻi eṉpatu tamiḻ allāta ellā moḻiyaiyum kuṟikkumāyiṉum īṇṭut tamiḻukku etirāka niṟutti ārāytaṟkuriyatu caṅkatam eṉappaṭum āriya moḻiyē yākum; parata kaṇṭattu vaḻaṅkum moḻikaḷukkuḷḷē teṉmoḻi eṉṟum vaṭamoḻi eṉṟum ticaip peyaraṭuttu etir niṟīi vaḻaṅkappeṟuvaṉa tamiḻum āriyamumē yaṉṟō? tamiḻiṉ taṉit taṉmaiyaiyō, uyarvaiyō kūṟappukiṉ atu maṟṟoṉṟai veṟuppatō iḻivupaṭuttuvatō ākumeṉac cilar karutukiṉṟaṉar. attakaiya maṉappāṉmaiyiṉar ivvavaiyiṉ kaṇ irār eṉa eṇṇukiṟēṉ.
ellā moḻikkum āriyamē tāyākumeṉṟum, ammuṟaiyē tamiḻum āriyattiṉiṉṟē tōṉṟiyatāku meṉṟum koḷḷum ārāyntu tuṇiyāta koḷkaiyiṉai yuṭaiyār cilar ciṉṉāḷiṉ muṉputoṭṭu iruntu varukiṉṟaṉar. moḻiyārāycci yeṉpatu putiyakalai muṟaikaḷil oṉṟāki uṇmaiyai viḷakki varukiṉṟa innāḷil akkoḷkai arukiviṭṭateṉpatil aiyamillai. eṉiṉum akkoḷkai aṟavē oḻiyumāṟu, tamiḻiṉ, ciṟappiyalpu kaḷai uḷḷavāṟu eṭuttuk kāṭṭit teruṭṭutal nammaṉōr kaṭaṉ. icciṟṟuraiyum ikkaruttiṉaik koṇṭē eḻunta tākum. moḻinūl ārāycciyāḷar moḻikaḷai avvavaṟṟiṉ paṇpukaḷ koṇṭu vevvēṟu kuṭumpam allatu kuḻuvākap pākupaṭuttiyirukkiṉṟaṉar. ivvuraipalkāmaip poruṭṭu avaṟṟaiyellām iṅkuk kūṟātu viṭukiṉṟēṉ.
vaṭamoḻi teṉmoḻi eṉṉum peyar vaḻakkē avai vevvēṟu taṉi moḻiyāmeṉpatuṇartti niṟkavum, vaṭakkiṉiṉṟu teṟku utittateṉpār pōla vaṭamoḻiyiṉiṉṟu teṉmoḻi vantateṉpār cilar tōṉṟiṉamai parivuṟaṟpālatē. atilum, tamiḻ ilakkaṇaṅkaḷait tuṟaipōkak kaṟṟu ilakkaṇak kottu iyaṟṟiya cuvāminātatēcikar 'ainteḻuttālorupāṭai yeṉṟaṟaiyavunāṇuvaraṟivuṭaiyōrē' eṉṟu kūṟiyatutāṉ peritum iraṅkaṟkuriyatu. tēcikarkkum atu karuttākāteṉpatum, ōr muṉaippup paṟṟiyē aṅṅaṉam kūṟiviṭṭaṉareṉpatum 'akattiyar' eṉṉum kaṭṭuraiyil muṉ viḷakkiyeḻutiyuḷḷēṉ. vaṭamoḻip paṟṟu mikkārāya cuppiramaṇiya tīkkitar tām iyaṟṟiya pirayōka vivēkattil 'vaṭamoḻikkum tamiḻukkum vēṟṟumai ciṟitu kūṟi itu vaṭamoḻikkum tamiḻ moḻikkum' pētaṅ kōṭi kūṟiṭṭu oru kūṟuṇṭō iṉṟōveṉpatu kūṟukiṉṟatu'. eṉṟaṉar aṅṅaṉaṅ kūṟiya avar tāmē 'vaṭamoḻikkum tamiḻmoḻikkum' ilakkaṇamoṉ ṟeṉpataṟiyātu camñāpētat tālum pāṭaivēṟṟumaiyālum ikaḻntu vēṟeṉpārai nōkkiyeṉka" eṉṟum kūṟivaittaṉar. tēcikar karuttum tīkkitar karuttum āriyamum tamiḻum vevvēṟu moḻikaḷē yāmeṉpatum, eṉiṉum avvirumoḻikkum ilakkaṇam oṉṟē yām eṉpatumākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. moḻiyiṉ amaitiyē ilakkaṇamākavum avarkaḷ moḻiyaiyum ilakkaṇattaiyum vēṟu pirittōtiyatu eṅṅaṉam poruntum? avarkaḷ vāḷā ilakkaṇam oṉṟeṉa moḻintārēyaṉṟi, eṅṅaṉam oṉṟākumeṉa ārāyntu taṅkōḷ niṟuviṉārallar.
iṉi, irumoḻip pulamaiyum oruṅkuṭaiya periyārākiya civañāṉa muṉivar tām iyaṟṟiya tolkāppiyap pāyira viruttiyil "aintiram nōkkit tokuttāṉeṉiṉ, tamiḻ moḻip puṇarccikkaṭpaṭuñ ceykaikaḷum, kuṟiyīṭukaḷum, viṉaikkuṟippu, viṉaittokai mutaliya cila collilak kaṇaṅkaḷum, uyartiṇai aḵṟiṇai mutaliya coṟpāku pāṭukaḷum, akam puṟameṉṉum poruṭ pākupāṭukaḷum, kuṟiñci veṭci mutaliya tiṇaippākupāṭukaḷum, avaṟṟiṉ pakutikaḷum, veṇpā mutaliya ceyyuḷilakkaṇamum, iṉṉōraṉṉa piṟavum vaṭamoḻiyiṟ peṟappaṭāmaiyāṉum" eṉa eḻutiya urai cintikkaṟpālatu. tolkāppiyattiṟku mutaṉūl aintiram eṉak kūṟiya cuppiramaṇiya tīkkitar kūṟṟaiyē āciriyar civañāṉa muṉivar iṅṅaṉam maṟutturaittārāvar. tamiḻilakkaṇattiṟku vaṭamoḻi ilakkaṇam mutaṉūlātal evvāṟṟāṉum poruntā teṉpatu itaṉāl valiyuṟuttappaṭṭatu. tēcikar kūṟṟum itaṉāṉē maṟukkappaṭṭamai ōrka. vaṭamoḻiyiṟ peṟappaṭāta tamiḻc ciṟappiyalpukaḷ ivaiyeṉat tamiḻilakkaṇap pakutikaḷ palavaṟṟaik kiḷanteṭuttōtiya āciriyar, ataṉōṭamaiyātu, 'iṉṉōraṉṉa piṟavum' eṉavum kūṟippōntaṉar. vaṭamoḻiyiṟ peṟappaṭum tamiḻiyalputāṉ vēṟu yātuḷatu? peyarccol, viṉaiccol, viṉaimutal, ceyappaṭuporuḷ, payaṉilai, mūviṭam, mukkālam, mutaliyaṉa vaṭamoḻiyiṟ peṟappaṭuvaṉa eṉṉalāmō? ivaiyillāta moḻikaḷē ulakattilillai. uvakaiyil iṉ collum vekuḷiyil vaṉcollum eḻutal pōla ivaiyellām ellā moḻiyiṉum iyalpilē amaitaṟ kuriyaṉa. ivaṟṟai oṉṟiṉiṉṟu maṟṟoṉṟiṟku vantaṉaveṉṟu kūṟuvōṉ "maruntiṟ ṟaṇiyāta pittaṉeṉ ṟeḷḷappaṭuvāṉ" eṉpatu tēṟṟam.
iṉi, ainteḻuttāloru moḻiyeṉavum aṟivuṭaiyōr nāṇuvar eṉṉuṅ kūṟṟai iṉṉuñ ciṟitu ārāyātu viṭutal takkataṉṟu. tamiḻeḻuttukkaḷ muppattu mūṉṟeṉa āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār kūṟiyiruppavum ataṉaic ciṟitum cintiyātu ainteḻutteṉṟu kūṟiyatu eṉ karuti? tamiḻ  mutaleḻuttukkaḷ muppatil irupattainteḻuttu vaṭamoḻiyiliruttaliṉ avaṟṟaik kaḻittu, eñciya aintumē tamiḻukkuriyavākak kūṟalāyiṟ ṟeṉiṉ, vaṭamoḻi yeḻuttukkaḷ yāvum piṟamoḻikaḷil kāṇappaṭutaliṉ vaṭamoḻikkeṉa ōreḻuttēṉum iṉṟeṉa ēṉ kūṟutal kūṭātu? iṉi avar eḷḷikkūṟiya ainteḻuttum attuṇai eḷimaiyuṭaiyaṉa vallaveṉpataiyum ōr eṭuttukkāṭṭil vaittu viḷakkukiṉṟēṉ.
"	naṟṟā ṭoḻāa reṉiṉ"1
eṉpatu ōr kuṟaṭpāviṉ piṉṉaṭi. ivvaṭiyai vaṭamoḻiyil eḻututal cālumō? itaṉ mutaliluḷḷa 'na' eṉṉum oṉṟaṉṟi maṟṟaiya eḻuttukkaḷ eḻutappaṭāvāyiṉ vaṭamoḻiyaṉṟō peritum eḻuttuk kuṟaipāṭuṭaiyateṉa itu kāṭṭā niṟkum? ippoḻutu ainteḻuttu ettuṇaip perumaiyuṭaiyaṉa veṉpatu kāṇka.
iṉi, uyir, mey, eṉṉum tamiḻ eḻuttukkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ uyarnta tattuvak koḷkaiyuṭaṉ poruntiyaṉa. vaṭaveḻuttuk kaḷiṉ peyarkaḷākiya accu, uṟal eṉpaṉavō yātoru ciṟanta koḷkaiyum uṭaiyaṉa valla; 'akara mutal ṉakaraviṟuvāy' eṉṟāṟpōla eḻuttukkaḷiṉ mutal iṟutikaḷai uṇarttum attuṇaip payaṉṟāṉum uṭaiyaṉavalla; mākēccura cūttiram eṉpavaṟṟiṉ mutalaiyum iṟutiyaiyum cērttup pirattiyākarit tavaiyē avai. tamiḻāṉatu pakuttaṟivuṭaiya makkaḷ mutaliya uyirai uyartiṇai eṉṟum, pakuttaṟivillāta uyiraiyum uyiril poruḷaiyum aḵṟiṇai eṉṟum aṟivupaṟṟip pākupāṭu ceytiruppatu pōla vaṭamoḻi ceyyavillai; vēṟu emmoḻiyum ceyyavillai. ivviru vakaip peyarkaḷē tamiḻ makkaḷiṉ uyariya aṟivaiyum, koḷkaiyaiyum, tamiḻiṉ taṉi māṇpaiyum viḷakkutaṟkup pōtiyaṉavākum. tamiḻ eḻuttuk kaḷil moḻikku mutalil varuvaṉa ivai, iṟutiyil varuvaṉa ivai, ivaṟṟōṭu mayaṅkutaṟkuriyaṉa. ivai eṉac ceytirukkum varaiyaṟaiyum, tamiḻc coṟkaḷ niṟutta collum kuṟittu varu kiḷaviyumākap puṇarum poḻutu uṇṭākum mey piṟitātal, mikutal, kuṉṟal eṉṉum tirivukaḷum, iyalpum ellām tamiḻukkuc ciṟappākavuriyavai. oliyiyalpiṟku māṟupaṭātavai. tamiḻ ilakkaṇam kūṟukiṉṟa cantiyum iḷañciṟār uḷḷiṭṭa makkaḷ pēcciluḷḷa cantiyum oṉṟē; vaṭamoḻiccanti aṉṉataṉṟu.
tamiḻiluḷḷa pāl vakuppil muṟaippiṟaḻvu ciṟitumillai; vaṭamoḻiyiluḷḷa pāl vakuppō coṉṉōkkaṅ koṇṭu āṇaippeṇṇākavum, peṇṇai āṇākavum, iraṇṭaiyum aliyākavum kūṟum piṟaḻcciyuṭaiyatu. orumai paṉmai eṉpatu tamiḻ vaḻakku. orumai irumai paṉmai eṉpatu vaṭamoḻi vaḻakku; vaṭamoḻiyilē oru peyaraic cārntu niṟṟaṟkuriya vēṟṟumai yurupu ataṉ aṭaimoḻikaḷ ellāvaṟṟoṭum kūṭiyirukkum. aṅṅaṉam varuvatu tamiḻ vaḻakkaṉṟu: piṉ varuvatai eṭuttukkāṭṭākak koḷka: 'kaṭaṟkaraiyil uḷḷatum pāvaṅkaḷai aḻippatum muṉipuṅkavarkaḷukku anukūlamāṉatumākiya cukantam eṉṉum malaiyiṉatu kukaiyil vacippōṉum taṉ oḷiyāl viḷaṅkuvōṉum makkaḷiṉ tuṉpattaip pōkkuvōṉum ākiya murukaṉai vaḻipaṭukiṉṟēm eṉṟu tamiḻil varaṟpālatu, 'kaṭaṟkaraiyil uḷḷatil pāvaṅkaḷai aḻippatil muṉipuṅkavar kaḷukku anukūlamāṉatil cukantam eṉṉum malaiyil kukaiyil vacippōṉai taṉ oḷiyāl viḷaṅkuvōṉai makkaḷiṉ tuṉpattaip pōkkuvōṉai murukaṉai vaḻipaṭukiṉṟēm' eṉa vaṭamoḻiyil varum. maṟṟum vaṭamoḻiyil vēṟṟumai ēḻēyākat tamiḻiṉ kaṇ eṭṭākum. 'intiraṉ eṭṭām vēṟṟumai yeṉṟaṉaṉ' eṉpār uḷarēṉum vaṭamoḻiyiṉ pōkku viḷiyaittaṉi vēṟṟumaiyākak koṇṭataṉṟu; tamiḻilē viḷivēṟṟumai parantiruppatoṉṟu; tolkāppiyar 'viḷi marapu' eṉa ōr iyal koṭuttu muppattēḻu cūttiraṅ kaḷālē ataṉai virittu viḷakkutaluṅ kāṇka. tamiḻiluḷḷa viṉaiccoṟkaḷ tiṇai pāl iṭam kālam eṉpavaṟṟai oruṅkuṇarttum ciṟappuṭaiyaṉa; vēṟu emmoḻiyilum attakaiya ciṟappuk kāṇappaṭavillai. kaṇṭāṉ eṉṉum viṉaimuṟṟilē mutaṉilaiyāṉatu kāṇṭalākiya toḻilaiyum, iṟuti nilai yāṉatu uyartiṇai āṇpāl paṭarkkai viṉai mutalaiyum iṭai nilaiyāṉatu iṟanta kālattaiyum veḷippaṭaiyākak kāṭṭi niṟṟal kāṇka. iṅṅaṉamāka eḻuttuc col ilakkaṇaṅ kaḷilkāṇappaṭum vēṟupāṭukaḷum tamiḻiṉ taṉimāṇpukaḷum palavām.
iṉi, vaṭamoḻiyiṉum piṟamoḻikaḷilum ilakkaṇam eṉṟu kūṟappaṭuvatu eḻuttuc collamaiti yaḷavēyākum. vaṭanūlār ilakkaṇattaic catta nūl eṉa vaḻaṅkutalum kāṇka. tamiḻilakkaṇamō eḻuttuñ collumākiya oliyaḷavil aṭaṅkuvataṉṟu: makkaḷ uṟutip poruḷ eytutaṟkuriya oḻukalāṟṟiṉai yellām varaiyaṟai ceytuṇarttum poruṭ pakutiyaiyuṭaiyatu. poruḷilakkaṇam vēṟem moḻiyilumiṉṟit tamiḻil maṭṭumē yuḷḷateṉpatu aṉaiva rālum naṉkaṟiyappaṭṭatu. tamiḻāṉatu piṟa ilakkaṇaṅkaḷaik kāṭṭiṉum poruḷilakkaṇattaiyē ciṟantatākavum iṉṟiyamai yātatākavum koṇṭuḷḷa teṉpatu "eḻuttum collum yāppum ārāyvatu poruḷatikārattiṉ poruṭṭaṉṟē poruḷatikāram peṟēmēyeṉiṉ ivai peṟṟum peṟṟilēm" eṉṉum iṟaiyaṉārakap poruḷ uraiyāṉum aṟiyappaṭum. poruḷilakkaṇam akam puṟam eṉṉum irukūṟuṭaiyatāki aṟam poruḷ, iṉpam vīṭu eṉṉum nāṟporuḷaiyum nutaluvatu. akamāṉatu kuṟiñci, mullai, marutam, neytal, pālai, kaikkiḷai, peruntiṇai eṉṉum ēḻu tiṇaikaḷaiyum, puṟamāṉatu veṭci, vañci, uḻiñai, tumpai, vākai, kāñci, pāṭāṇtiṇai eṉṉum ēḻu tiṇaikaḷaiyum ovvoru tiṇaiyum paṟpala tuṟaikaḷaiyum uṭaiyaṉa. tamiḻiluḷḷa cāṉṟōr ceyyuṭkaḷellām attiṇai tuṟaikaḷōṭu poruntiyaṉavākavē irukkum: oru ceyyuḷēṉum poruḷilak kaṇattiṟku ilakkiyamākāta tāyirātu. poruḷilakkaṇam tamiḻukkēyuriyatu. eṉavē avvilakkaṇattuṭaṉ porunta eḻunta ceyyuṭkaḷellāmum tamiḻukkuriyaṉa veṉpatu kūṟātēyamaiyum. ivvuṇmai yuṇarvārkkut tamiḻāṉatu vēṟemmoḻikkum kaṭamaippaṭṭirāta taṉic cemmoḻi yeṉpatu pulappaṭum. uṇarātārē palavāṟu pitaṟṟāniṟpar.
iṉi, tamiḻiṉ ciṟappai viḷakkum poruḷilakkaṇak kuṟippukkaḷ cilavaṟṟaic curukkamākak kūṟi ivvuraiyai muṭippām. puṟapporuḷilakkaṇamāṉatu aracariṉ pōr muṟaikaḷ mutaliyaṉa kūṟu mukattāl aṟam poruḷkaḷaiyum vīṭṭaiyum uṇarttuvatu. ap pōr muṟai yoṉṟē tamiḻar kaḷiṉ uyariya nākarikattaik kāṭṭutaṟkup pōtiyatākum. pōr ceytaṟkuriya kāraṇaṅkaḷ iṉṉiṉṉa eṉpatum, appōr kaḷai iṉṉiṉṉa muṟaiyāṉē āṟṟuvar eṉpatum puṟattiṇai kaḷil aḻakākac collappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. iṅkē mikavum pārāṭṭutaṟkuriya ceyti yoṉṟuḷatu. aḵtāvatu tamiḻ nāṭṭu vēntarkaḷum vīrarkaḷum pōr nikaḻttumiṭattē appōr nikaḻccikkēṟpa ovvoru vakaiyāṉa malarkaḷaic cūṭiyiruppar eṉpatē. aṅṅaṉam cūṭumpūkkaḷāṉē avvoḻukkaṅkaḷum veṭci, vañci, mutaliya peyarkaḷaip peṟuvaṉavāyiṉa. tām cūṭiyirukkum pūviliruntē appōr iṉṉa kāraṇattāl nikaḻvateṉpatai aṟiya vaippatu evvaḷavu makiḻccikkuriya ceyalām! itiliruntu, tamiḻaracarkaḷ tamakkuḷḷē potuvāṉa cila oḻuṅkukaḷai yēṟpaṭutti avaṟṟiṉ varampu kaṭavātu oḻukivantaṉa reṉpatum peṟappaṭum. tamiḻnāṭṭāriṉ ippōrmuṟaikaḷum vaḻakkaṅkaḷum piṟa nāṭṭavarkaḷālum virumpi ēṟṟuk koḷḷappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. pāratattil vantuḷḷa niraikōṭalum mīṭciyumākiya ceytikaḷ tamiḻaruṭaiya pōrmuṟaiyai āriyar ēṟṟuk koṇṭamaikku aṭaiyāḷamākum, "nam pōr vīrar teṉṉāṭṭāraip pōlattalaikkumalarkaḷai aṇikiṉṟaṉar" eṉap parataṉ kūṟuvatāka vāṉmīki muṉivar tamatu irāmāyaṇattu ayōttiyā kāṇṭattiṟ kūṟiyiruppatu tamiḻaruṭaiya vaḻakkam āriyarāl virumpi yēṟṟuk koḷḷappaṭṭamaiyai naṉku teḷivuṟuttal kāṇka.
iṉi, akapporuḷiṉ māṇpu aḷaviṭaṟ kariyatoṉṟaṉṟu. iyaṟkaiyum tūymaiyum poruntiya talaiyaṉpiṉāl iṉpantuykkum muṟaimaiyai eḻil peṟa uṇarttuvatu tamiḻukkēyuriya ciṟappiyalpām. ivvuṇmaiyaip paṇṭaic cāṉṟōr palar naṉkuṇarntu kūṟi vaittirukkiṉṟaṉar.
"	iṉṟamiḻ iyaṟkai yiṉpam nilaipeṟa neṟiyiṟ
tuyttār nikartamak kilāta nīrār"1 (cin.curamañcari 69)
eṉṟār tiruttakkatēvar.
akapporuḷ kūṟum kaḷavu, kaṟpu eṉappaṭum irukaikōḷum uyariya aṉpuṭaṉ poruntiyavē yeṉiṉum, avviraṇṭaṉuḷḷum kaḷavē iyaṟkaiyum ciṟappu muṭaiyatāmeṉpatu
"kaṟpeṉappaṭuvatu kaḷaviṉ vaḻittē"2
eṉpataṉālum iṟaiyaṉār akapporuḷukkuk kaḷaviyal eṉap peyar pōntamaiyālum aṟiyappaṭum. kaḷaviṉatu ciṟappum, atu tamiḻ āyātavarāl aṟiyappaṭāta māṇpiṉateṉpatum, paripāṭal oṉpatām pāṭṭil naṉku valiyuṟuttappaṭukiṉṟatu. atilē nāṉmaṟaip pulavarai etirmukamākki,
"	nāṉmaṟai virittu nallicai viḷakkum
vāymoḻip pulavīr kēṇmiṉ ciṟantatu
kātaṟ kāmam kāmattuc ciṟantatu
viruppō rottu meyyuṟu puṇarcci
. . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . 
taḷḷāp poruḷiyalpiṟ ṟaṇṭamiḻ āyvantilār
koḷḷārik kuṉṟu payaṉ"3
eṉak kūṟit tamiḻiṉatu ciṟappai viḷakkiyiruttal kāṇka. iṅṅaṉam tamiḻarkku muḻu urimaiyuṭaiya kaḷavukkūṭṭam pōlvatorumaṇam āriyariṉ eṇvakai maṇattuḷ oṉṟāka vuḷḷatu. atuvē, yāḻōr kūṭṭam allatu kāntaruvamaṇam eṉpatu. tolkāppiyattilum, iṟaiyaṉār. akapporuḷilum tamiḻaruṭaiya kaḷavukkūṭṭattiṉ iyalpai āriyaruṭaiya yāḻōr kūṭṭattōṭu oppittu viḷakkiyiruttalum,
"	tēvar paṇṇiya tīntoṭai yiṉcuvai mēvar teṉṟamiḻ meypporu ḷātaliṉ"1
eṉat tiruttakkatēvar kūṟiyiruttalum nōkkaṟpālaṉa. āriyar maṇaṅkaḷil oṉṟu tamiḻar maṇattuṭaṉ oruvāṟu ottiruppiṉum tamiḻaruṭaiya oḻukkamum nākarikamum mika mēmpaṭṭavai yeṉpataṉaip pala ētukkaḷāl niṟuvutal kūṭum. ōr āṭavaṉ tāṉ kātalitta naṅkaiyai aṭaiyappeṟāvaḻi maṭalūrntēṉum avaḷai aṭaitaṟku muyalvaṉ eṉpatu akapporuḷil kūṟappaṭuva toru ceyti maṭalūrvōr nāṇiṉai aṭiyuṭaṉ iḻakka vēṇṭutaliṉ tamiḻnāṭṭu makaḷir maṭalūrvatāka niṉaittaluñ ceyyār. ātaliṉ, tolkāppiyaṉārum
"	ettiṇai maruṅkiṉum makaṭūu maṭaṉmēṟ
poṟpuṭai neṟimai yiṉmaiyāṉa"2
eṉa maṭalūrtalai makaḷirkku vilakkiṉār. teyvappulamait tiruvaḷḷuvaṉārum itukoṇṭē tamiḻnāṭṭu makaḷiriṉ oppuyarvaṟṟa māṭciyai viḷakkuvārāy,
"	kaṭalaṉṉa kāma muḻantum maṭalēṟāp
peṇṇiṟ peruntakka til"3
eṉṟār. āṉāl, āriyarukkuḷ makaḷirum ittakaiya ceyal ceyyalāmeṉa aṉṉāruṭaiya kāmanūl vitippatākum. atu tirumālaik kāmuṟṟa peṇṇorutti maṭalūrtal kūṟuvatāka
"	aṉṉa naṭaiyār alarēca āṭavarmēl
maṉṉu maṭalūrār eṉpatōr vācakamum
teṉṉuraiyiṟ kēṭṭaṟiva tuṇṭataṉai yām teḷiyēm
maṉṉum vaṭaneṟiyē vēṇṭiṉōm"4
eṉattirumaṅkai maṉṉaṉ periya tirumaṭaliṟ pāṭiyiruppataṉāṟ peṟappaṭum. peṇṇorutti ataṟkut tamiḻ nūl iṭantarāmaiyiṉ vaṭanūl muṟaiyait taḻuvukiṉṟāḷ eṉpatu itil viḷakkamākac collappaṭṭuḷatu. itiliruntu āriyar oḻukkamum tamiḻar oḻukkamum ittaṉmaiyaṉa eṉpatu naṉku viḷaṅkum.
iṉi, tūymaiyākiya aṉpiṉ ṟaṉmaiyaik kāṇavēṇṭiṉ paṇṭai akapporuṭ ceyyuṭkaḷiliruntē ataṉaik kāṇalākum. piṉ varuñceyyuḷ aintiṇaiyaim patil uḷḷatu.
"	cuṉaivāyc ciṟunīrai yeytāteṉ ṟeṇṇip
piṇaimā ṉiṉituṇṇa vēṇṭik kalaimāttaṉ
kaḷḷatti ṉūccuñ curameṉpar kātalar
uḷḷam paṭarnta neṟi"1
poruḷvayiṟ ceṉṟa talaivaṉ. pirivukku āṟṟātu varuntum talaiviyait tōḻi āṟṟuvikkum poruḷaiyuṭaiyatu. icceyyuḷ kātalar viraintu varuvar eṉpatait teḷiyac ceyvatē talaiviyai āṟṟuvikkum neṟiyām. ataṉaiyē tōḻi iṅṅaṉam kūṟukiṉṟāḷ. talaivar ceṉṟa neṟi ōr ariya curaneṟiyākum: accurattilē oṉṟaiyoṉṟu piritalillāta aṉpiṉaiyuṭaiya āṇmāṉum peṇmāṉum nīr vēṭkai mikku ōr vaṟuvāyc cuṉaiyaik kaṇṭaṭaikiṉṟaṉa. atiluḷḷa nīrō avaṟṟuḷ yātēṉum oṉṟē parukakkūṭiya ciṟṟaḷaviṉaiyuṭaiyatu, iraṇṭum parukiṉ oṉṟaṟkēṉum viṭāy taṇivatākātu: innilaimaiyil eṉceyvatu: āṇmāṉāṉatu tāṉ viṭāyāl varuntiṉum taṉuyirēyākiya mēṉmaiyuṭaiya peṇmāṉ annīraip paruki iḷaippāṟa vēṇṭumeṉa virumpukiṉṟatu; āṉāl ataṉaip parukac ceytālō taṉ kātalaṉākiya āṇmāṉ uṇṇātirukkat tāṉ maṭṭum uṇpatu taṉatu kaṟpiṟkum talaiyaṉpiṟkum poruntāmaiyiṉ, atu parukamāṭṭātu; itaṉaik karutiya āṇmāṉ ivvāṟāyatōr cūḻcci ceyyum; aḵtāvatu, peṇmāṉ nīruṇṭu muṭiyuṅkāṟum tāṉum nīruṇpatupōl atil vāy vaittu vāḷā uṟiñcik koṇṭirukkum; akkāṭu
tāṉ talaivar, karutic ceṉṟa neṟiyākum eṉkiṉṟāḷ tōḻi. itaṉāṟ pōtarum poruḷāvatu tām cellum neṟiyilē vilaṅkukaḷum iṅṅaṉam aṉpuṭaiyaṉavāyiruttalaik kāṇum talaivar tam uyirē pōlum niṉṉaip pirintirukkalāṟṟāta
varāy uṭaṉē mīḷvar eṉṟu kūṟit tēṟṟiyavāṟām. eṉṉē kātaliṉ perumai; kātal kaṭavuḷiṉ uruvameṉṟāl vēṟu kūṟuvāṉēṉ. iṟaivaṉ tirukkailaiyil vīṟṟirukkuṅ kōlattai nam tirunāvukkaracu perumāṉ tiruvaiyāṟṟil eṅṅaṉam taricittār?
"	kātaṉ maṭappiṭi yōṭuṅ kaḷiṟu varuvaṉa kaṇṭēṉ"2
"	varikkuyil pēṭaiyō ṭāṭi vaiki varuvaṉakaṇṭēṉ"3
"	pēṭai mayiloṭuṅ kūṭip piṇaintu varuvaṉakaṇṭēṉ"1
"	karuṅkalai pēṭaiyō ṭāṭik kalantu varuvaṉakaṇṭēṉ"2
eṉṟu iṅṅaṉam tām kaṇṭavāṟu kūṟi, ivaṟṟaikkaṇṭa muṟaimaiyāl,
"	kaṇṭē ṉavartirup pātam kaṇṭaṟi yātaṉa kaṇṭēṉ"3
eṉṟu aruḷic ceytāraṉṟō?
"	kātal virinilat tārā vakai kāṇār
cātalnaṉ ṟeṉpar takaimaiyōr - kātalum
ākki yaḷittaḻikkum kantaḻiyiṉ pēruruvē
nōkkilarai nōva tevaṉ"4
eṉṉum iṉṉilaic ceyyuḷil kātal kaṭavuḷiṉ ciṟanta vuruvameṉak kūṟappaṭṭiruttaluṅ kāṇka. pala kūṟuvāṉēṉ. iṟaivaṉai niṉainturukippāṭum meyyaṭiyārkaḷ tam aṉpiṉ mutircciyil avaṉait tammōṭu vēṟṟumaiyillāta kātaṟ kiḻamaiyuṭaiyavaṉāka vaittup pāṭuvatoṉṟē kātaliṉ perumaiyai naṉku viḷakkuvatākum. aṅṅaṉam pāṭappeṟṟa āḷuṭaiya piḷḷaiyār tiruppāṭṭil iraṇṭu kūṟi niṟuttutum.
"	poṉṉampūṅ kaḻikkāṉaṟ puṇartu ṇaiyōṭuṭaṉvāḻum
aṉṉaṅkāḷ aṉṟilkāḷ akaṉṟumpōy varuvīrkāḷ
kaṉṉaviltōṭ ciṟuttoṇṭaṉ kaṇapatīc caramēya
iṉṉamutaṉ iṇaiyaṭikkīḻ eṉatallal uraiyīrē"5
"	ciṟaiyāru maṭakkiḷiyē yiṅkēvā tēṉoṭupāl
muṟaiyālē uṇattaruvaṉ moypavaḷat toṭutaraḷam
tuṟaiyāruṅ kaṭaṟṟōṇi purattīcaṉ ṟuḷaṅkumiḷam
piṟaiyāḷaṉ ṟirunāmam eṉakkorukāṟ pēcāyē"6

13. tiṇaimayakkam
"	tiṇaimayak kuṟutaluṅ kaṭinilai yilavē
nilaṉoruṅku mayaṅkuta liṉṟeṉa moḻipa
pulaṉaṉ kuṇarnta pulamai yōrē"1
"	uripporu ḷallaṉa mayaṅkavum peṟumē"2
"	ennilai maruṅkiṟ pūvum puḷḷum
annilam poḻutoṭu vārā vāyiṉum
vanta nilattiṉ payatta vākum"3
eṉṉum tolkāppiya akattiṇaiyiyaṟ cūttiraṅkaḷ tiṇaimayakkam uṇartta eḻuntaṉa.
"	muttiṟap poruḷun tattan tiṇaiyoṭu
marapiṉ vārātu mayaṅkalu muriya"4
eṉṉum akapporuḷ viḷakkac cūttiramum atu. ivai oru nilattil vēṟu tiṇaikkuriya uripporuḷākiya oḻukkamum karupporuḷkaḷum mayaṅkivarutalaiyē perumpāṉmai uṇarttuvaṉa eṉpatu ivaṟṟiṉ uraiyāl aṟiyalākum. aṅṅaṉam mayaṅki varutaṟkuc caṅkattuc cāṉṟōr ceyyuṭkaḷi liruntu uraiyāḷarkaḷ pala utāraṇaṅ kāṭṭiyuḷḷārkaḷ. kapilar pāṭiya kuṟiñcip pāṭṭiṉuḷ vēṟu tiṇaikkuriya pūkkaḷ mayaṅki vantavāṟuṅ kāṇka.
ippoḻutu tiṇaimayakkameṉṟu palarāl aṟiyap paṭuvatu akapporuḷ kūṟum ittiṇaimayakkamaṉṟu: orunilam maṟṟoru nilattuṭaṉ neruṅkiyiruttalāl oṉṟaṟkuriya karup poruḷkaḷ mutaliyaṉa maṟṟoṉṟiṟ ceṉṟu poruntutal eṉpatē.
cēraṉceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ kaṇṇakikkuk kaṟkālkoṇṭu nīrppaṭai ceytu mīḷumuṉ avaṉ maṉaivi vañci nakarattiṉ araṇmaṉaiyil paḷḷikkaṭṭiliṉ mītu tuyiliṉṟiyiruttalai varuṇikkum iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ,
"	kuṟattiyar pāṭiya kuṟiñcip pāṇiyum"1
"	toṭuppē ruḻava rōtaip pāṇiyum"2
"	kōvala rūtuṅ kuḻaliṉ pāṇiyum"3
"	veṇṭirai poruta vēlaivā lukattu"4
"	añcoṟ kiḷaviya rantīm pāṇiyum"5
"	ōrttuṭa ṉirunta kōpperun tēvi"6
eṉṟu kūṟi, nālvakai nilamum vañcikku aṇimaiyil iruttalai viḷakkiṉār. arumpatavuraikārarum ōrttu uṟaṅkāta tēvi yeṉṟatu nālunila aṇimaiyuṅ kūṟiṟṟu eṉṟār. iṅṅaṉam oru nāṭṭiṉkaṇ nilaṅkaḷ oṉṟōṭoṉṟu neruṅki avaṟṟiṉ karupporuḷ mutaliyaṉa mayaṅkiyiruttalai annāṭṭiṟku ōr ciṟappākak koṇṭu pulavarkaḷ aḻakuṟap puṉaintu kūṟi vantirukkiṉṟaṉar. caṅkac ceyyuḷākiya porunarāṟṟuppaṭaiyil kaaya-mutal uuru muṭiyavuḷḷa aṭikaḷil ittiṇai mayakkam kūṟappaṭṭirukkiṟatu. atil orupakuti piṉvaruvatu:
"	tēṉeyyoṭu kiḻaṅkumāṟiyōr
mīṉeyyoṭu naṟavumaṟukavum
tīṅkarumpō ṭavalvakuttōr
māṉkuṟaiyoṭu matumaṟukavum,
kuṟiñci paratavar pāṭa, neytal
naṟumpūṅ kaṇṇi kuṟavar cūṭak,
kāṉavar marutam pāṭa, akavar
nīṉiṟa mullaip paḵṟiṇai nuvalak
kāṉakkōḻi katir kutta,
maṉaikkōḻi tiṉaikkavara,
varaimanti kaḻimūḻkak,
kaḻinārai varaiyiṟuppat,
taṇvaippiṉ nāṉāṭukuḻīi"7
kuṟiñci nilattuk kuṟavar tēṉaiyum kiḻaṅkaiyum neytaṉilat tilēviṟṟu aṅkuḷḷa mīṉiṉ neyyum matuvum vāṅkutalum, marutanilattuk kaḷamar karumpaiyum avalaiyum kuṟiñci nilattil viṟṟu aṅkuḷḷa māṉṟacaiyum tēṉum koḷḷutalum, neytaṉilattup paratavar kuṟiñcip paṇpāṭutalum, kuṟiñcinilattuk kuṟavar neytaṟpūc cūṭutalum, mullainilattu āyar marutap paṇṇum maruta nilattārākiya akavar mullaippaṇṇum pāṭutalum, mullaiyiluḷḷa kōḻi marutattil neṟkatiraiyum, marutat tiluḷḷa maṉaikkōḻi kuṟiñci cārnta mullaiyiluḷḷa tiṉaiyaiyum kavarntuṇṭalum, kuṟiñciyiluḷḷa manti neytaliṉ kaḻiyil mūḻkutalum, neytaliṉ kaḻiyiluḷḷa nārai kuṟiñci yiṭattu malaiyiṟ ceṉṟu taṅkutalum iyaṟkai neṟi piṟaḻāmal itiṟ kūṟiyiruppatu kaṟpārkkup periyatōr iṉpam payappatākum.
"	koṭicciyar puṉattayaṟ kuṟiñci neypakar
iṭaicciyar katuppayaṟ kamaḻum; ēḻaiyaṅ
kaṭaicciyar kaḷaiyeṟi kuvaḷai kāṉalvāyt
toṭuttalar piṇaiyalār kuḻaluṭṭōṉṟumē"1
"	kalavartañ ciṟupaṟai yicaiyiṟ kaiviṉaip
pulavartēm piḻimakiḻ kuravai poṅkumē
kulavukāṟ kōvalar koṉṟait tīṅkuḻal
ulavunī ḷacuṇamā vuṟaṅku meṉpavē"2
ivviraṇṭum cūḷāmaṇic ceyyuṭkaḷ (33, 34). mullai nilattu āycciyar kuṟiñcippūvaiyum neytaṉilattu nuḷaicciyar marutattup pūvaiyum cūṭikkoḷvar eṉṟum, neytaṉilattu nuḷaiyar paṟaiyōcaiyāl maruta nilattu uḻavar kuravai viḷakkamuṟum eṉṟum, mullainilattuk kōvalar kuḻalicaiyāl kuṟiñci nilattu acuṇamā uṟaṅkum eṉṟum ivaṟṟiṟ kūṟappaṭṭamai kāṇka.
iṉi, teyvamaṇakkuñ ceyyuḷiyaṟṟavalla cēkkiḻār perumāṉ tiruttoṇṭar purāṇattē toṇṭai nāṭṭiṉ vaḷamuraikkumiṭattu āṟu ceyyuṭkaḷil tiṇaimayakkaṅ kūṟiyuḷḷār. avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟu piṉvaruvatu:
"	kavaru mīṉkuvai kaḻiyavar kāṉavark kaḷittuc
civaluñ cēvalu māṟiyuñ ciṟukaḻic ciyarkaḷ
avarai yēṉaluk keyiṟṟiyar pavaḷamut taḷantum
uvari neytalum kāṉamuṅ kalantuḷa voḻukkam"1
neytaṉilamākkaḷ mīṉiṉaṅkaḷaip pālaimākkaḷukku viṟṟu avariṭamiruntu civalum cēvalum vāṅkuvar eṉṟum, pālainila makaḷir avarai tiṉaikaḷai neytal makaḷirkku viṟṟu avariṭa miruntu pavaḷamum muttum vāṅkuvar eṉṟum itiṟ kūṟappaṭṭuḷatu. ippāṭṭil amaintuḷḷa cila nuṇporuḷkaḷ aṟiyaṟpālaṉa. īṇṭuk kāṉavareṉpār pālaimākkaḷ. pālaiyāvatu mullaiyum kuṟiñciyum kalantu tirintateṉpatu.
"	mullaiyuṅ kuṟiñciyu muṟaimaiyiṟ ṟirintu
nalliyal piḻantu naṭuṅkutuya ruṟuttup
pālaiyeṉpatōr paṭivaṅ koḷḷum"2
eṉṉum cilappatikāra aṭikaḷāl aṟiyalākum. cēkkiḻār kuricilō
"	kōla mullaiyuṅ kuṟiñciyu maṭuttacil liṭaṅkaḷ
nīla vāṭpaṭai nīlikōṭ ṭaṅkaḷu nirantu
kāla vēṉiliṟ kaṭumpakaṟ poḻutiṉaip paṟṟip
pālai yuñcola lāvaṉa vuḷaparaṉ murampu"3
eṉṟu kūṟi vaittuḷḷār. iṅṅaṉam mullaiyum kuṟiñciyum pālaiyāyiṉamaiyiṉ avviru tiṇaikkumuriya karupporuḷkaḷ pālaikkuriyavātal cālum. ikkaruttāṉē mullaikkuriya civalum avaraiyum, kuṟiñcikkuriya kuyiṟcēvalum tiṉaiyum itiṟ pālaikkuriyavākak kūṟappaṭṭaṉaveṉka. icceyyuḷiluḷḷa maṟṟoru nayam tiṇai mayakkamuṇarttum.  itaṉil, coṟkaḷaiyum mayaṅkavaittiruppatākum. eyiṟṟiyar avaraiyēṉalukkuc ciṟukaḻicciyarkaḷ pavaḷamuttaḷantum eṉaniṟkaṟpālavākiya coṟkaḷ māṟi mayaṅki niṟṟal kāṇka.
iṉi, parañcōtimuṉivar, civañāṉamuṉivar, kacciyappa muṉivar, makāvittuvāṉ mīṉāṭcicuntaram piḷḷaiyavarkaḷ mutalāṉōr pāṭiya tiṇai mayakkaṅkaḷukku aḷavillai. avaṟṟaiyellām iṅku eṭuttuk kāṭṭappukiṉ ivvurai mikaviriyumākaliṉ, aṇimaik kālattirunta aṟiñar cuntaram piḷḷaiyavarkaḷ iyaṟṟiya maṉōṉmaṇiyattiluḷḷa tiṇaimayakkap pakutiyoṉṟaimāttiram kāṭṭi itaṉai muṭikkutum.
"	vañcinā ṭataṉil naṉcey nāṭeṉac
centamiḻ vaḻaṅkum tēyamoṉ ṟuḷatu: ataṉ
antamil peruvaḷam aṟivār yārō
marutamum neytalum mayaṅki aṅkeṅkum
puraiyaṟu celvam nilaipeṟa vaḷarum;
maḻalai vaṇṭāṉam pularmīṉ kavara,
ōmpupu nuḷaicciyar eṟikuḻai, tēṉpoḻi
puṉṉainuṇ ṭātāṟ poṉṉiṟam peṟṟa
erumaiyiṉ puṟattirun tiruñciṟai pularttum
alaikaṭaṟ kākkaikku alakkaṇ viḷaikkum;
kētakai malarniḻal iṉameṉak karutit
tārāt taḻuviṭac cārtarac ciritta
āmpalvāy koṭṭiṭum kōṅku alarttātē:
vālvaḷai cūlu ḷaintu īṉṟaveṇ muttam
ōtimak kuṭampaiyeṉṟu uṉṉupu kālāṟ
paruntiṉam kavarntu ceṉṟu aṭampiṭaip putaikkum;
karumpaṭu cālaiyiṉ perumpukai maṇṭak
kūmpiya neytaṟ pūntaḷir kuḷira
mēyntakal kārā tīmpāl tuḷikkum;
alamukam tākkuḻi alamarum āmai
nuḷaicciyar kaṇavarō ṭiḻaittiṭum ūṭalil
vaḻitteṟi kuṅkumac cēṟṟiṭai oḷikkum;
pūñciṉai marutiṭai vāḻntiṭum aṉṟil
naḷimīṉ kōṭpaṟai viḷikēṭ ṭuṟaṅkā;
vēyeṉa vaḷarnta cāykulaic cāliyil
uppār paḵṟi yoruniṟai piṇippar;
ipperun tēyattu eṅkum irāppakal
tappiṉum māri taṉkaṭaṉ tavaṟā;
koṇmū veṉṉum koḷkalam koṇṭa
amiḻtiṉai avvayiṉ kaviḻttapiṉ celvuḻi
vaṭiyum nīrē nammiṭitīr cāral;
naṉṉīrp perukku munnīr nīttamum
eyyā teṉṟum etirttiṭum piṇakkil
naṭukkaṭal naṉṉīr cuvaittiṭum orupāl;
marakkalam vantiṭum vayaṟkarai orukāl"1



14. iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷum poruḷilakkaṇamum
"kumariyoṭu vaṭavimayat torumoḻivait tulakāṇṭa" imayavarampaṉ neṭuñcēralātaṉ eṉṉum cērar perumāṉ makkaḷuḷ iḷavalum, vēttu muṉiyumākiya iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ muttamiḻp pulamaiyum nirampiya vittakak kaviyaracar eṉpatu avariyaṟṟiya paḻutaṟṟa muttamiḻk kāppiyamākiya cilappati kārattāl naṉku tuṇiyappaṭuvatākum, ittoṭar nilaic ceyyuḷil iyaṟṟamiḻiṉ pakutiyākiya poruḷilakkaṇattai, aṭikaḷ eṅṅaṉam kaiyāṇṭuḷḷaṉareṉac cila eṭuttuk kāṭṭukkaḷāl viḷakkuvatu ic ciṟṟuraiyiṉ nōkkam. ikkāppiya muḻutum poruṭ pakutikaḷ palavum viravi amaintu kiṭattalai itaṉ patikattoṭakkattē aṭiyārkku nallār virittuḷḷa uraiyāl iṉituṇaralākum. avar akap poruṭṭiṇai kaḷākiya mullai, kuṟiñci, marutam, neytal, pālai eṉṉum aintiṇaikkuriya nilaṉum, poḻutumākiya mutaṟ poruḷkaḷum, teyvam, uṇā, mā, maram, puḷ, paṟai, toḻil, yāḻ, paṇ, nilamakkaḷ, nīrnilai, vārkoṭi, pū eṉṉum karupporuḷkaḷum, iruttalum ataṉ nimittamum mutalākiya uripporuḷkaḷum itaṉkaṇ amaintu kiṭattalai eṭuttukkāṭṭi, ataṉ muṭivil, "ic collappaṭṭa aintiṇaiyiṉ mutal karuuripporuḷum piṟavumākiya akattiṇaip poruṭ payaṉ uṭampoṭu puṇartti yuṇarttip puṟattiṇaip poruṭpayaṉum piṟavum ivvāṟu uṭampoṭu puṇarttiyuṇarttuṅkāṟ puṉainturaikkappaṭṭuk kēṭpōrkku iṉpam payavātu uṇarvu calikkumātaliṉ vantaṉa vantuḻik kāṭṭutalaik karutiṟṟu ivvurai yeṉka" eṉak kūṟiṉar. akap poruṭpakuti palavum āṇṭē avarāṟ kāṭṭap peṟṟaṉavākaliṉ avaṟṟai viṭuttu, avvuḻi avar kāṭṭātu viṭutta puṟapporuṭ pakutiyuḷ oru cilavē īṇṭuk kāṭṭa niṟpaṉa. cilappatikāra intira viḻa'd2vūreṭutta kātaiyil cōḻaṉ karikāṟ peruvaḷavaṉ vaṭaticaimēṟ paṭaiyeṭuttuc ceṉṟa varalāṟu.
"	irunila maruṅkiṟ porunaraip peṟāac
ceruveṅ kātaliṟ ṟirumā vaḷavaṉ
vāḷuṅ kuṭaiyu mayirkkaṇ muracum
 nāḷoṭu peyarttu naṇṇārp peṟukavim
 maṇṇaka maruṅkiṉeṉ valikeḻu tōḷeṉap
 puṇṇiyat ticaimukam pōkiya vannāḷ
acaivi lūkkattu nacaipiṟak koḻiyap
pakaivilak kiyatip payaṅkeḻu malaiyeṉa
imaiyava ruṟaiyuñ cimaiyap piṭarttalaik
koṭuvari yoṟṟik koḷkaiyiṟ peyarvōrkku."1
eṉa iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷāṟ kūṟappeṟṟuḷatu.
karikālaṉatu ippaṭaiyeṭuppāṉatu puṟattiṇaiyuḷ etaṉpāṟ paṭumeṉpatu ārāyccikkuriyatu. puṟattiṇai ilakkaṇappaṭi pōr mēṟ cellutaṟkuriya kāraṇattaiyēṉum, tiṇaip peyarai yēṉum, tiṇaikkuriya aṭaiyāḷap pūvaiyēṉum āciriyar īṇṭuk kūṟātu, 'vāḷum kuṭaiyum mayirkkaṇ muracum, nāḷoṭu peyarttu' eṉṉum tuṟaik kuṟippāl iḵtu iṉṉa tiṇaiyāku meṉpataṉai uyttuṇara vaittirukkiṉṟār. nāḷoṭu peyarttalāvatu nāṭ koḷḷutal. aḵtu iṉṉateṉ pataṉai nāṭkōṭalāvatu nāḷum ōraiyum taṉakkēṟpak koṇṭu celvuḻi akkālattiṟku iṭaiyūṟu tōṉṟiya vaḻit taṉakku iṉṟiya maiyātaṉavaṟṟai atticai nōkki akkālattē muṉṉē cella viṭutal" eṉa nacciṉārkkiṉiyar viḷakki yiruppatu koṇṭu aṟika. eṉavē, karikālvaḷavaṉ tāṉ pōrk kōlam, pūṇṭu, vaṭaticai nōkkip puṟappaṭumuṉ taṉṉuṭaiya veṟṟivāḷ, koṟṟakkuṭai, vīramuracu eṉpavaṟṟai naṉṉāḷiṉ naṉmuḻuttattil vaṭaticai nōkkip puṟappaṭa viṭuttāṉ eṉpatu peṟṟām.
iṉi, iṅṅaṉam vāḷnāṭ koḷḷutal mutaliyaṉa ettiṇaiyiṉ pāṟ paṭumeṉa ārāyvuḻi āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār karuttiṉ paṭi uḻiñaippāṟpaṭumeṉal pōtarukiṉṟatu. eṉṉai? avar uḻiñait tiṇaiyiṉ tuṟaippakuti kūṟum nūṟpā oṉṟiṉ māttiram "kuṭaiyum vāḷum nāḷ kōḷ" eṉak kuṭaināṭ kōḷum vāṇāṭ kōḷum kūṟip piṟitōriṭattum kūṟāmaiyiṉ eṉka. āciriyar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar vañcittiṇaikkum ivai uriya veṉṉum karuttiṉarāyiṉum, uḻiñaikkuppōla vañcikku ivai iṉṟiyamaiyātaṉavalla eṉpatum avar karuttātal "iyaṅkupaṭaiyaravam" eṉṉum cūttiravuraiyāl aṟiyappaṭum. avar, ivai vañcikku iṉṟiyamaiyātaṉavaṉmaiyiṉ "iyaṅku paṭai yaravam" eṉpataṉuḷ aṭaṅka vaittu, uḻiñaikku iṉṟiyamaiyāmaiyiṉ āciriyar āṇṭuk kiḷantōtiṉar eṉpar. paṉṉiru paṭalattiṉ vaḻi nūlākiya puṟapporuḷ veṇpā mālaiyuṭaiyārō vañci, kāñci, uḻiñai eṉṉum mūṉṟu tiṇaikkaṇṇum kuṭai nāṭkōḷum, vāṇāṭ kōḷum kūṟuvar. ivaṟṟuṭ kāñciyai vañciyiṉ pāl aṭakkiṉum vañci, uḻiñai, eṉṉum irutiṇaikku avviru tuṟaiyum uriya veṉpatu avar karuttākum. cilappatikārattiṟ pōnta eḻuttiṉ ṟiṟaṉaṟintum iṉcoṟ poruḷiṉ aḻuttantaṉai; aṟintum ippaṭiyārkku nallurai pālitta aṭiyārkku nallār 'vāḷuṅ kuṭaiyum - nāḷoṭu peyarttu" eṉṉun toṭarkkuc ciṟappurai kūṟumiṭattu veṇpāmālaiyiṉ vañcip paṭalattil avviru tuṟaikkum amainta,
"	ceṟṟārmēṟ celavamarntu
koṟṟavāṇāṭ koṇṭaṉṟu"1
"	peytāmañ curumpimirap perumpulavar pukaḻpāṭak
koytār maṉṉavaṉ kuṭaināṭ koṇṭaṉṟu"2
eṉṉum koḷukkaḷaiyum,
"	aṟintava rāyntanā ḷāḻittēr maṉṉaṉ
eṟintila koḷvā ḷiyakkam - aṟintikalip
piṉpakalē yaṉṟiyum pēṇā rakanāṭṭu
naṉpakaluṅ kūkai nakum.3
"	muṉṉar muraciraṅka mūrik kaṭaṟṟāṉait
tuṉṉarun tuppiṟ ṟoḻuteḻā - maṉṉar
uṭaināḷu lantaṉavā lōtanīr vēlik
kuṭainā ḷiṟaivaṉ koḷa".4
eṉṉum veṇpākkaḷaiyum mēṟkōḷākak kāṭṭiyiruppati
liruntu avar karikālaṉatu pōrc celavai vañcittiṇaiyiṉ
pāṟ paṭuttaṉar pōlum eṉak karuta iṭaṉuṇṭākiṉṟatu. avar karuttu yātāyiṉum karikālaṉ kūṭār maṇkoḷal karutiyō, aṉṟit taṉatu maṇṇiṉaik koḷḷutal karutiya māṟṟārōṭu porutal kuṟittō puṟappaṭṭāṉeṉpatu ciṟitum peṟappaṭāmaiyiṉ ataṉai vañci yeṉṟal poruttamuṭaittaṉṟu. iṉi, tirumāvaḷavaṉ pōrilēmiku viruppuṭaiyaṉākaliṉ vaṭaticai nōkkip pōyiṉaṉ eṉpatu koṇṭu ataṉait tumpaittiṇai eṉṉalāmeṉiṉ, āciriyar oruvarum vāṇāṭ kōṭal mutaliyavaṟṟai attiṇaikkuk kūṟāmaiyiṉ aṅṅaṉamuraippatum poruntuvataṉṟām. ivvāṟṟāl, karikālaṉatu vaṭaticaip paṭaiyeṭuppu uḻiñait
tiṇaiyā meṉṟalē cevvitu.
āyiṉ, 'muḻumutalaraṇam muṟṟalum kōṭalum' ākiya uḻiñaiyilakkaṇantāṉum ataṉkaṭ peṟappaṭṭilatēyeṉiṉ, imayamalaiyāṉatu kuṟukkiṭṭu vilakkiṟṟeṉṟu avaṉ muṉintu ataṉ piṭarilē taṉatu puliyaip poṟittu mīṇṭāṉ eṉṟu collappaṭutaliṉ ataṉaiyē pakaivaratu araṇmuṟṟiya ceyalākak koṇṭu uḻiñaiyilakkaṇam poruntiyuḷḷavāṟu kāṇka. maṟṟu, ataṉai uḻiñai eṉpataṟku vēṟu ētukkaḷum uḷa. āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār kuṭaināṭkōḷum vāḷ
nāṭkōḷum kūṟiṉāraṉṟi, muracu nāṭkōḷ eṉa oṉṟu kūṟiṉārallar. veṇpā mālaiyuṭaiyārum aṅṅaṉamē muracu nāṭkōḷ kūṟiṟṟilarēṉum, uḻiñaip paṭalattil vāṇāṭ
kōḷ eṉṉum tuṟaiyai aṭuttu muracavuḻiñai eṉpatōr tuṟai kūṟiyuḷḷār. eṉavē, iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ kūṟiya muracu nāṭkōḷukku veṇpāmālaiyiṉ uḻiñaip paṭalattil māttiramē oruvāṟu ilakkaṇam kāṇappaṭutalāṉum, ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉatu vaṭaticaimēṟ celavai vañciyiṉpāṟ paṭutturaikkak karutiya aṭikaḷ itaṉōṭu ataṉiṭai vēṟṟumai pulappaṭa
"	irunila maruṅkiṉ maṉṉarel lāniṉ
tirumalart tāmaraic cēvaṭi paṇiyum
muḻutta mīṅkitu muṉṉiya ticaimēl
eḻuccip pālai yākeṉ ṟētta
mīḷā veṉṟi vēntaṉ kēṭṭu
vāḷuṅ kuṭaiyum vaṭaticaip peyarkkeṉa"1 
eṉṟu kālkōṭ kātaiyil, ceṅkuṭṭuvaṟku vāṇāṭ kōḷum kuṭaināṭkōḷum kūṟi, muracu nāṭkōḷ kūṟāmai yāṉum karikālaṉatu pōrccelavu uḻiñaittiṇaiyā meṉpatu valiyuṟutal kāṇka. iṉṉum muracu nāṭkōḷukku aṭiyārkku nallār eṭuttukkāṭṭiya "mācaṟa vicitta" eṉṉum puṟappāṭṭil,
"	maññai olineṭum pīliyoṇpoṟi maṇittār
polaṅkuḻai yuḻiñaiyoṭu poliyac cūṭṭik
kuruti vēṭkai yurukeḻu muracam
maṇṇi vārā vaḷavai"1
eṉa uḻiñaimālai kūṟappaṭṭiruttal itaṉai naṉku valiyuṟuttu māṟum kāṇka. itaṉāl aṭiyārkkunallārum itu uḻiñai yaṉṟeṉṉum karuttiṉareṉat tuṇital cellāteṉka.
iṉi, itukāṟum kāṭṭiyavaṟṟiliruntu iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ tolkāppiyattaiyē yaṉṟip paṉṉiru paṭalattaiyum taḻuviyuḷḷāreṉpatu pōtarum. eṉiṉum,
"	poṉpuṉai yuḻiñai cūṭi maṟiyaruntun
 tiṇpiṇi muraca nilaiyuraittaṉṟu"2
eṉṉum veṇpāmālaiyiṉ muraca vuḻiñaik koḷuvāṉum.
"kuruti vēṭkai yurukeḻu muracam"3
eṉṉum puṟappāṭṭaṭiyāṉum vīramuraciṟkup paliyūṭṭutal peṟap paṭuvataṉṟi, ataṉai nāṭkoṇṭu peyarttal peṟappaṭāmaiyiṉ, aṭikaḷ ivviru nūlēyaṉṟi akkālattu vaḻaṅkiya vēṟu nūlkaḷiṉum ilakkaṇaṅ kaṇṭō, aṉṟit taṟkālattu vēntaratu vaḻakkiṟkaṇṭō vīramuraciṉai nāṭkoḷḷumāṟum kūṟippōntaṉareṉka. iṅṅaṉamē, tolkāppiyam mutaliya vaṟṟiṉ vēṟāka aṭikaḷ taṅkōḷkaiyai nāṭṭiccellutal piṟāṇṭum kāṇappaṭum.


15. tolkāppiya ārāycci
nūlāciriyar varalāṟu
1. tolkāppiyar jamatakkiṉi maintareṉpatum akattiyar māṇākkareṉpatum poruntumā eṉṉum ārāycci.
aṟivu cāṉṟa āṉṟōrkaḷē!
ivvārāycciyuraikkup poruḷākavuḷḷa tolkāp piyattil,
"	avaiyaṭakkilē ariṟavat teriyiṉ
vallā kūṟiṉum vakuttaṉar koṇmiṉēṉ
ṟellā māntarkkum vaḻimoḻin taṉṟē"
eṉa avaiyaṭakkattiṟku ilakkaṇam kūṟappaṭṭiruttaliṉ, yāṉum ammuṟai paṟṟi eṉ uraikaḷiluḷḷa kuṟaikaḷai nuṅkaḷ cīriya matiyāṟ ceppañ ceytu koḷkaveṉa vēṇṭi, eṉatu ārāycciyait toṭaṅkukiṉṟēṉ. ippoḻutu namakkuk kiṭaittuḷḷa tamiḻ nūlkaḷ ellāvaṟṟuḷḷum kālattāl muntiyatum,  tamiḻiyal aṉaittaiyum muṟṟaveṭuttuk kūṟuvatum tolkāppiyamē eṉpatu tamiḻvallār aṉaivarkkum oppa muṭinta karuttākum. innūliṉai naṉku ārāytalāṉē tamiḻ moḻiyiṉ iyalpukaḷaiyum, tamiḻakattiṉ paḻaiya nilaimai kaḷaiyum oruvāṟu kāṇṭal kūṭumeṉpatil aiyamillai. yāṉ ivvārāycciyai naṭāttuḻip  paṇṭai nūl, uraiyāciriyaṉmār koṇṭa koḷkaikaḷukku orōvaḻi māṟākac cellutaluṅ kūṭum aṉṟi, itukāṟum yāṉ koṇṭirunta karuttukku muraṇākavum cila iruttal kūṭum. ārāycciyiṉ peṟṟi ituveṉavaṟinta aṟivuṭaiyār ivai kuṟittu eṉṉai muṉiyāreṉṉum tuṇivuṭaiyēṉ.
innūliṉ āciriyarākiya tolkāppiyar camatakkiṉi muṉivarkku maintar eṉṟum, akattiyaṉārkku māṇākkar eṉṟum kūṟappaṭukiṉṟār. ivaṟṟuḷ muṉṉaiyataṟku ātaravu, uraiyāciriya rellāruḷḷum piṉ vantavarākiya āciriyar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar, tolkāppiyap pāyiravuraiyil, "tēva rellāruṅ kūṭi yām cēraviruttaliṉ mēruntāḻntu teṉṟicaiyuyarntatu, itaṟku akattiyaṉārē āṇṭiruttaṟ kuriyareṉṟu avarai vēṇṭikkoḷḷa, avarum teṉṟicaikkaṭ pōtukiṉṟavar kaṅkaiyāruḻaic ceṉṟu kāviriyārai vāṅkik koṇṭu, piṉṉar yamataṅkiyāruḻaic ceṉṟu avar makaṉār tiraṇatūmākkiṉiyārai vāṅkik koṇṭu, pulattiyaṉāruḻaic ceṉṟu avaruṭaṉ piṟanta kumariyār  ulōpāmuttiraiyārai avar koṭuppa nīrēṟṟu irīip, peyarntu... potiyiliṉ kaṇiruntu, irāvaṇaṉaik kantaruvattāṟ piṇittu, irākkatarai āṇṭu iyaṅkāmai vilakki, tiraṇatūmākkiṉiyārākiya tolkāppiyaṉārai nōkki 'nī ceṉṟu kumariyāraik koṇṭu varuka'  eṉak kūṟiṉar" eṉa uraittirukkum kataiyaṉṟi vēṟillai.
akattiyaṉār eṉap peyariya tamiḻāciriyar oruvar paṇṭu viḷaṅkiyiruntaṉareṉpataṟkum, muttamiḻkkum ilakkaṇamāka avarāl iyaṟṟap peṟṟatoru virinta nūl akattiyameṉṉum peyaruṭaṉ tikaḻntateṉpataṟkum aḷaviṟanta cāṉṟukaḷ uḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟai nāṉ eḻutiya 'akattiyar' eṉṉum urai nūlil virivākak kāṭṭiyuḷḷēṉ. īṇṭuc cilavaṟṟaik kuṟippiṭu kiṉṟēṉ.
iṟaiyaṉār kaḷaviyal uraiyil muccaṅka varalāṟu kūṟu miṭattu, talaiccaṅkap pulavaruḷḷum iṭaiccaṅkap pulavaruḷḷum akattiyaṉār mutalil vaittuk kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. akattiyam muccaṅkattārkkum nūlāyiruntateṉavum atiṟkūṟappeṟṟuḷatu. piṉ vanta uraiyāḷarkaḷ palarum akattiyaṉāraippaṟṟiyum akattiyattaip paṟṟiyum āṇṭāṇṭuk kuṟippiṭṭiruppatuṭaṉ, akattiyameṉac ciṟcila nūṟpākkaḷaiyum eṭuttuk kāṭṭiyiruk kiṉṟaṉar.
"taraviṉṟākit tāḻicai peṟṟum"
eṉṉum tolkāppiyac ceyyuḷiyaṟ cūttiravuraiyil 'iṉi ivvāṟu vanta koccakaṅkaḷaiyellām oru varaiyaṟaip paṭuttup pāttōṟum iṉañcērttip paṇṇiṟkut tiṟam pōlap piṉṉuḷḷa. āciriyar aṭakkuvar.  ataṉai akattiyamum tolkāppiyamum uṇarntu avartam karuttaṟinta āciriyar avvāṟaṭakkāmaikkuk kāraṇaṅ kūṟuvar' eṉa nacciṉārkkiṉiyar kūṟiyuḷḷār. avvāciriyar kūṟuṅ kāraṇam ivaiyeṉa avar piṉpu eṭuttuk kāṭṭiyavai yellām pērāciriyaruraiyāka viruttaliṉ, akattiyamum tolkāppiyamum uṇarntavareṉa avarāṟ cuṭṭappeṟṟavar pērāciriyarēyeṉpatu tuṇipu. "āṅṅaṉam virippiṉ' eṉṉum ceyyuḷiyaṟ cūttiravuraiyil 'iḵtu eṟṟāṟ peṟutumeṉiṉ, itaṉ mutaṉūl ceyta āciriyaṉ akattiyaṉār collumāṟṟāṟ peṟutum eṉṟavāṟu" eṉap pērāciriyar tāmē kūṟiyiruppatum itaṉai valiyuṟuttum.
itiliruntu kaṭaiccaṅka kālattiṉ piṉ neṭunāḷ varai akattiyam vaḻaṅki vantateṉṟu tuṇintu kūṟalākum. eṉiṉum ataṟku yārum urai kaṇṭāreṉat teriyāmaiyiṉ, urai yāḷarkaḷ kālattil annūl muḻuvuruvuṭaṉ kāṇak kūṭātavāṟu citaintu pōyiṟṟeṉṟu karututal cālum. iṉi, talaiccaṅkap pulavarāyiruntu tamiḻilakkaṇam iyaṟṟiya ivvakattiyarum, muṉivar palaruḷḷum ciṟantavarāka vaittup purāṇavitikācaṅkaḷ kūṟum akattiyarum, irukku vētappatikaṅkaḷiṟ palavaṟṟai iyaṟṟiya akattiyarum oruvareṉṟō, vevvēṟāvar eṉṟō aṟutiyiṭṭuraittal mika aritākum. akattiyar eṉpatu ōr kuṭumpap peyarē eṉṟum, ap peyaruṭaiyār palar iruntiruk kiṉṟaṉar eṉṟum, mutalil vintamalaiyiṉ vaṭakkil ōr akattiyarum, irāmāyaṇa kālattil kōtāvariyāṟṟaṅkaraiyil ōr akattiyarum, pārata kālattil potiyiṉ malaiyil maṟṟōr akattiyarum iruntuḷāreṉṟum oru cārār kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. maṟṟoru cārār akattiyar peyariṉar palareṉak kūṟuvatuṭaṉ talaiccaṅkattu akattiyar iruntāreṉpatu tavaṟu eṉṟum, nakkīraṉārkku ilakkaṇam aṟivuṟuttuvarākat tiruviḷaiyāṭaṟ purāṇam kūṟukiṉṟa akattiyaroruvar kaṭaiccaṅka nāḷil iruntārāvareṉṟum kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. āṉāl avartam kūṟṟukku yātoru ātāramum kāṭṭappaṭavillai. orē peyaruṭaiya palar orupeṟṟiyē nikaraṟṟa perumai vāyn tiruntaṉar eṉpatu putumaiyākat tōṟṟiṉum aṅṅaṉam nikaḻtal kūṭāteṉpatillai. eṉiṉum avar oruvarallar eṉpataṟkuc cāṉṟu iṉṟiyamaiyātataṉṟō? enta nūlkaḷiṉ vāyilāka akattiyar eṉṉum peyarai nām aṟikiṉṟōmō anta nūlkaḷaṉaittum akattiyarai oruvarākavē vaittuk kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. appeyariṉar palareṉpataṟkut tiṉaiyaḷavu cāṉṟum kāṇappaṭavillai. vintattiṟku vaṭakkilirunta akattiyaraiyum potiyil akattiyaraiyum oruvarākavē kanta purāṇam mutaliyaṉa kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. kōtāvari maruṅkirunta akattiyarum potiyilakattiyarum vēṟallareṉpatu irāmāyaṇattāl aṟiyappaṭum.
"	nīṇṭa tamiḻālulakai nēmiyiṉaḷantāṉ"
"	taḻaṟpurai cuṭarkkaṭavuḷ tantatamiḻ tantāṉ"
"	eṉṟumuḷa teṉṟami ḻiyampiyicai koṇṭāṉ"
eṉa akattiyap paṭalattuk kampar kūṟumāṟuṅ kāṇka. maṟṟum avar 'vintameṉum viṇṭōy nākamatu nākamuṟa nākameṉa niṉṟa' maiyum, 'īcaṉika rāyulaku cīrpeṟa virunta' maiyum kūṟutalum karutaṟpāṟṟu. akattiyar palareṉpār, muṉṉuḷḷa nūlāciriyar palarum peyarorumaiyāl maruṇṭu akattiyaroruvareṉak koṇṭu vaḻuvi viṭṭaṉareṉak kūṟuṅkaṭappāṭuṭaiyarākiṉṟaṉar. aḵtu evvāṟāyiṉum ākuka. irāma rāvaṇap pōrukku muṉṉarē akattiyar potiyiliṉ kaṇ iruntaṉa reṉpataiyum, avarē akattiya miyaṟṟiya tamiḻāciriyarāvāreṉpataiyum namakkuk kiṭait tuḷḷa ātaravukaḷaṉaittum valiyuṟuttukiṉṟaṉa. kāvirip pūmpaṭṭiṉattilirunta kāntaṉ eṉṉum cōḻa maṉṉaṉ aracar kulattaik kaḷaitalaiyē nōṉpākak koṇṭa paracurāmaṉ taṉṉoṭu pōrkuṟittu varutalaiyaṟintu akattiya muṉivaraic caraṇaṭaintāṉeṉṟu maṇimēkalaikkāppiyaṅ kūṟuvatum, akattiyar irāvaṇaṉai veṉṟu pōkkiyatākat tolkāppiya uraiyilum maturaik kāñciyuraiyilum nacciṉākkiṉiyar kūṟuvatum pōlvaṉa vellām ikkaruttai araṇ ceytu niṟṟal kāṇka.
iṉi, tolkāppiyaṉār akattiyarkku māṇākkar tāmō eṉpatu ārāytaṟkuriyatu. tamiḻuraikaḷil muṟpaṭṭa tākiya iṟaiyaṉār kaḷaviyaluraiyāṉatu tolkāppiyarai iṭaiccaṅkattup pulavarākavum, tolkāppiyattai iṭaic caṅkattārkkum kaṭaiccaṅkattārkkum nūlākavum kūṟuvataṉṟi avarai akattiyaṉāroṭu toṭarpupaṭuttu yātum kūṟiṟṟilatu.
"	maṉṉiya ciṟappiṉ vāṉōr vēṇṭat
teṉmalai yirunta cīrcāl muṉivaraṉ
ṟaṉpāṟ ṟaṇṭamiḻ tāviṉ ṟuṇarnta
tuṉṉaruñ cīrttit tolkāppiyaṉ mutaṟ
paṉṉiru pulavarum pāṅkuṟap pakarnta
paṉṉiru paṭalam paḻippiṉ ṟuṇarntōṉ"
eṉa varum puṟapporuḷ veṇpāmālaip pāyiramē mutaṉ mutalākat tolkāppiyarai akattiyar māṇākkar eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu. itaṉaip piṉpaṟṟiyē piṉvanta uraiyāciriyar cilarum kūṟuvārāyiṉar. veṇpā mālaikku mutaṉūlākiya paṉṉirupaṭalam kaḷaviyaluraip pāyirattil aḷaviṉāṟ peyar peṟṟataṟku utāraṇamākak kāṭṭappaṭṭiruttaliṉ innūlkaḷ ki.pi. 6ām nūṟṟāṇṭukkup piṟpaṭṭavaiyalla veṉṟu karutal poruntum. eṉiṉum tolkāppiyar mutaṟ paṉṉiru pulavarum cērntiyaṟṟiyatu paṉṉiru paṭalam eṉpatil iḷampūraṇa vaṭi kaḷum, nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum karuttoruppaṭātavarākiṉṟaṉar. tolkāppiyap puṟattiṇaiyiyaluraiyil iḷampūraṇar "akattiṇai ēḻākip puṟattiṇai paṉṉiraṇṭākil moḻinta poruḷō ṭoṉṟa vaittal eṉṉum tantiravuttikkum poruntātāki, mikai paṭakkūṟal, taṉṉāṉoru poruḷ karutik kūṟal eṉṉuṅ kuṟṟamum payakkum eṉka" eṉṟum, "paṉṉiru paṭalattuḷ 'taṉṉuṟu toḻilē vēntuṟu toḻileṉ-ṟaṉṉaviruvakaittē veṭci' eṉa iraṇṭu kūṟu paṭak kūṟiṉā rāyiṉum... avar avvāṟu kūṟal mikaipaṭak kūṟalām. ataṉāl paṉṉirupaṭalattuḷ veṭcippaṭalam tolkāppiyar kūṟiṉār eṉṟal poruntātu" eṉṟum, "paṉṉiru paṭalattuḷ karantaikkaṇ puṇṇoṭu varutal mutalāka vēṟupaṭac cila tuṟai kūṟiṉārāl eṉiṉ, puṇpaṭuttal māṟṟōr ceyta maṟattuṟai yākaliṉ aḵtu ivarkku māṟākak kūṟutal mayaṅkakkūṟutalām; ēṉaiyavum ivvāṟu mayaṅkak kūṟalum, kuṉṟak kūṟalum, mikaipaṭak kūṟalum āyavāṟu eṭuttuk kāṭṭiṉ perukumākaliṉ uyttuṇarntu kaṇṭu koḷka" eṉṟum iṅṅaṉam palaviṭattum paṉṉiru paṭalattai maṟuttiruppatuṭaṉ, veṭcippaṭalam tolkāppiyaṉār kūṟiyataṉṟeṉat tuṇintum uraittuḷḷār. nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum paṉṉiru paṭalak koḷkaiyaip palaviṭattu maṟuttiruppatuṭaṉ, 'koṭuppōrētti' eṉṉum cūttiravuraiyil "tattam putu nūl vaḻikaḷāṟ puṟa nāṉūṟṟiṟkut tuṟai kūṟiṉārēṉum, akattiyamum tolkāppiyamumē tokaikaḷukku nūlākaliṉ avar cūttirap poruḷākat tuṟaikūṟa vēṇṭumeṉṟuṇarka" eṉavum urait tuḷḷār. ivaṟṟiliruntu paṉṉiru paṭalam eṉpatu piṟkālattu yārō oruvarāl iyaṟṟappeṟṟu, akattiyar māṇākkar paṉṉiruvarālum iyaṟṟappeṟṟateṉak kaṭṭiyuraikkappaṭṭa teṉpatē avarkaḷ karuttātal peṟappaṭum.  'viṉaiyiṉīṅki' eṉṉum marapiyaṟ cūttiravuraiyiṟ pērāciriyar "paṉṉiru paṭalattup puṉainturai vakaiyāṟ pāyirac cūttirattuḷ uraikkappaṭṭatu" eṉṟu kūṟiyiruttal ūṉṟiyuṇariṉ avar karuttum ituveṉpatu pōtarum. ivvāṟṟāl akattiyarkku māṇākkar tolkāppiyar eṉṉum koḷkaiyum valiyaṟṟoḻital kaṇkūṭām. tolkāppiyattilē vaṭacoṟ kaḷai eṭuttāḷutaṟku viti kūṟiyiruppatuṭaṉ ampōtaraṅkam, kuñcaram, vaiciyaṉ mutaliya vaṭacoṟkaḷ payiṉṟiruppatum, ikara aikāravīṟṟu tiṅkaṭ peyarum nāṭpeyarum niṉṟu puṇartaṟku vaḻivakuttiruppatum, vaḻi nūl vakaiyuḷ moḻi peyarttiyaṟṟalum oṉṟāka vitittiruppatum, piṟavum ōrntuṇariṉ āriyamakkaḷiṉ kalappup peritum ēṟpaṭṭa piṉṉarē innūl iyaṟṟap peṟṟatākumeṉil poruttamām. irāmāyaṇa kālattil attuṇai mikutiyāka āriyakkalappu iruntirukka iṭamiṉmaiyāl āciriyar akattiyaṉārkku neṭunāḷiṉ piṉpu tolkāppiyam tōṉṟiyatākumeṉalē nēritu. ivvāṟṟāl tolkāppiyaṉār akattiyaṉārkku māṇākkarākkutal cellāteṉpatu peṟappaṭum. ākavē, avar camatakkiṉiyiṉ putalvar eṉpatum, akattiyarāl aḻaittu varappeṟṟaṉa reṉpatum poruntāmai kūṟal vēṇṭā eṉka. tolkāppiyar akattiyaṉārkku uṭaṉ kālattiṉarallarāyiṉum avaratu koḷkaiyaip pōṟṟiyōrarukiṉamaiyāl avarkku māṇākkareṉap peṟṟaṉar eṉak karututal cālum. iṉi pārata nikaḻccikkup piṉpē tolkāppiyam iyaṟṟappeṟṟa teṉpataṟku uṟutiyuṭaiya cāṉṟu oṉṟu uḷḷatu. tol
kāppiyattu uyir mayaṅkiyalil,
"	paṉaiyiṉ muṉṉar aṭṭuvaru kālai
nilaiyiṉ ṟākum aiyeṉuyirē
ākāram varuta lāvayi ṉāṉa."
"	koṭimuṉ variṉē aiyavaṇiṟpak
kaṭinilaiyiṉṟē valleḻuttu mikuti"
eṉṉuñcūttiraṅkaḷāl paṉai eṉpatu koṭi eṉpata ṉōṭu puṇarttup paṉaikkoṭiyeṉac ceykai ceytu muṭikkappeṟṟuḷḷatu. tamiḻ vaḻakkuc ceyyuṭkaḷil it toṭar payiṉṟiruntālaṉṟi āciriyar iṅṅaṉam vitikūṟā reṉpatu tēṟṟam. tokai nūlkaḷilum piṟavaṟṟilum nampi mūttapirāṉākiya palarāmaṉ palaviṭattuk kūṟappaṭṭiruttal kāṇalākum. avaṟkuk koṭi paṉaiyeṉpatum avaṟṟuḷ kāṇakkiṭakkiṉṟatu.
"putaiyiruḷuṭukkaip polampaṉaik koṭiyōṟku"
eṉavum,
"niṉṉoṉ ṟuyarkoṭi paṉai"
eṉap paripāṭalilum
"aṭalvēn nāñciṟ paṉaik koṭi yōṉum"
eṉap puṟanāṉūṟṟilum,
"poṟpaṉai veḷḷaiyai yuḷḷā toḻukiṉṉā"
eṉa iṉṉā nāṟpatilum kūṟappaṭutal kāṇka. ivvāṟṟāl palarāmaṉōṭu toṭarpuṭaiya pārata nikaḻccikkup piṉpē innūl iyaṟṟap peṟṟatātal orutalai. pārata kālattaip paṟṟi ārāycci ceytuḷḷār palar. pāratap pōr naṭantatu ki.mu.1000-ām āṇṭil eṉṟu oruvarum, ki.mu.1100-ām āṇṭil eṉṟu maṟṟoruvarum kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. ki.mu.1194-ām āṇṭil nikaḻnta teṉṟu cittūr kōpālaiyar ārāynturaikkiṉṟaṉar.  R.C. tattar kūṟuṅkālam iṉṉuñ ciṟitu muṉṉarc celkiṉṟatu. C.V. vaittiyā eṉpavar ki.mu. 3100 tāṉ pāratappōriṉ kālamāmeṉṟu pala cāṉṟukaḷāl niṟuvukiṉṟaṉar. tuvāpara kaliyuka caktiyil pāratam nikaḻntateṉṉum paramparaik koḷkai vaittiyar avarkaḷāl vēṟu cāṉṟukaḷ koṇṭum niṟuvappaṭutaliṉ atuvē pāratappōriṉ kālamākumeṉa yāmum koḷḷaṟpālam. iṉi, māpāratappōriṉ piṉpu tolkāppiyam eppoḻutu tōṉṟiyateṉpatu ārāytaṟ kuriyatu. ki.pi. eṭṭām nūṟṟāṇṭukku muṉ tamiḻ moḻi tiruntiya nilaiyaiṭainta tiṉṟeṉṉum karuttuṭaiya oruvar tolkāppiyam annūṟṟāṇṭaiyaṭuttut tōṉṟiyatākum eṉpar. ki.pi. aintām nūṟṟāṇṭil iyaṟṟappaṭṭa jainēntiramē tolkāppiyappāyirattil ‘aintiram’ eṉak kuṟikkappaṭṭateṉṉum karuttuṭaiya orucārār aḵtu ki.pi. ēḻām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ iṭaiyil utittatākum eṉpar. cātavākaṉaṉ eṉṉum āntiramaṉṉaṉukku amaiccaṉāyirunta cārvavarmaṉāl iyaṟṟappaṭṭa ‘kātantiram’ eṉpatē tirāviṭa ilakkaṇaṅkaḷukku vaḻikāṭṭiṟṟu eṉakkoṇṭa, maṟṟoru cārār innūl ki.pi. iraṇṭām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ piṉpu eḻuntatākumeṉpar.
iṅṅaṉam airōppiya āciriyar cilarālum intiya āciriyar cilarālum palavāṟākak kūṟappaṭṭaṉa vellām paḻaṅkataiyāyp pōṉamaiyiṉ iṉi avaṟṟiṉ poruntāviyalpukaḷai eṭuttu viḷakkutal vēṇṭā. kaṭaic caṅkattiṉ iṟutikkālamāṉatu ki.pi. mūṉṟām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ toṭakkattiṟkup piṟpaṭṭataṉṟu eṉpatu varalāṟṟaṟivuṭaiya tamiḻ vallōr palarālum ippoḻutu naṉku tuṇiyappeṟṟuḷḷatu. kaṭaiccaṅkap pulavaruḷḷē perum pakutiyiṉar ki.pi. mutal nūṟṟāṇṭilum iraṇṭām nūṟṟāṇṭilum iruntōrāvar. avarkaḷāl iyaṟṟap peṟṟa ceyyuṭkaḷil cakaṭam, camam, camaḻppu, ñamali, ñamaṉ, yavaṉar mutaliya coṟkaḷ payiṉṟu vantiruppavum, tolkāppiyaṉār,
“	cakarak kiḷaviyum avaṟṟōraṟṟē
a ai oḷaveṉum mūṉṟalaṅ kaṭaiyē”
“āe,
o eṉum mūvuyir ñakārat turiya”
āvō ṭallatu yakaram mutalātu”
eṉṉum cūttiraṅkaḷāl ca, ña, ya, mutaliya eḻuttukaḷ moḻikku mutalākā eṉa vilakkiyuḷḷār. maṟṟum avar,
“	nāṟcīr koṇṭa taṭiyeṉappaṭumē”
