iṉi, ivvāciriyar innūlai amaittirukkum tiṟappāṭukaḷ nōkkaṟpālaṉa. eḻuttum collum poruḷumākiya mūṉṟaṉaiyum mūṉṟu atikārattāṟ kūṟaluṟṟa ivar, ovvōratikārattaiyum oṉpatu oṉpatu iyalkaḷāka vakuttuk koṇṭuḷār. iṅṅaṉam ovvōratikāramum iyalvakaiyāl ōraḷaviṉavāka vakut tiruppatu tām kūṟakkarutiya poruḷkaḷai ōrellaiyuḷ aṭakka muyaṉṟu iṭarppaṭuvatākumeṉac cilar karutavuṅkūṭum. eṉiṉum avviyalkaḷ yāvum iṉṟiyamaiyātaṉavum, avvav vatikāraṅkaḷōṭu iyaipuḷḷaṉavumātal nuṇṇaṟivāṉ ārāyvārkkup pulaṉākā niṟkum. āyiṉ ōratikārattai oṉpāṉ iyaliṟ kuṟaiyavō mikavō vakuttamaittal ollātō eṉiṉ, aṅṅaṉañ ceytal ollumāyiṉum, oru nikarāṉa varaiyaṟai yuṭaimaiyiṉ payaṉ karuti aṅṅaṉam iyalkaḷai varaiyaṟai ceytu, poruḷaḷavuk kēṟpac cūttiraṅkaḷai mikuttum kuṟaittum iṭarppāṭiṉṟi yāttamaittuḷḷā reṉal vēṇṭum. paṇṭaiyāciriyarkaḷ tām karutiya poruḷ kaḷai eñcāmaṟ kūṟuvatil nōkkuṭaiyarāyiruntamai pōṉṟē, avai piṟkālattil tiripiṉṟi niṉṟu nilavavum, kaṟpōruḷḷattil eḷitiṟ pukuntu akalātirukkavum uriya muṟaikaḷai iyalu maḷavukaikkoṇṭu avaṟṟaik kūṟum karuttuṭaiyārumāvar. pāṇiṉiyum tamatu nūliṉ eṭṭu attiyāyaṅkaḷaiyum nannāṉku pākaṅkaḷāka vakuttuḷḷaṉar. tirukkuṟaḷ, tēvāram, tiruvāy moḻi, mutaliya tamiḻ maṟaikaḷum, āriya vētappakutiyākiya aitarēyap piramāṇam mutaliyaṉavum, caṅkac ceyyuṭkaḷil patiṟṟuppattu mutalāyiṉavum ovvōraḷaviṉavāka iyaṉṟirut talum kāṇka. iṉi, ivvāciriyar meyppāṭṭiyaliṉkaṇ eṇvakai meyppāṭum nikaḻuṅ kāraṇaṅkaḷai uṇarttu miṭattu,
"	kalvi taṟukaṇ icaimai koṭaiyeṉac
collap paṭṭa perumitam nāṉkē"1
"	celvam pulaṉē puṇarvu viḷaiyāṭṭeṉ
ṟallal nītta vuvakai nāṉkē"2
eṉṟiṅṅaṉam ovvoru meyppāṭṭukkum nannāṉku kāraṇaṅ kūṟuvatum, uvamaviyalilē viṉai, payaṉ, mey, uru eṉṉum nālvakai uvamattiṟkum urupukaḷ taṉittaṉi evveṭṭāka varaiyaṟutturaippatum, peyariyalilē,
"	avaṉivaṉ uvaṉeṉa varūum peyarum,
avaḷivaḷ uvaḷeṉa varūum peyarum,
avarivar uvareṉa varūum peyarum,
yāṉyām nāmeṉa varūum peyarum,
yāvaṉ yāvaḷ yāva reṉṉum
āvayiṉ mūṉṟō ṭappati ṉaintum
pālaṟi vanta uyartiṇaip peyarē"3
eṉavum,
"	atu vitu utuveṉa varūum peyarum,
avaimuta lākiya āytap peyarum,
avai yivai uvaiyeṉa varūum peyarum,
avaimuta lākiya vakarap peyarum,
yātu yā yāvai eṉṉum peyarum,
āvayiṉ mūṉṟō ṭappati ṉaintum
pālaṟi vanta aḵṟiṇaip peyarē"4
eṉavum ōriṉamāṉa mummūṉṟu peyarkaḷai ovvōraṭiyil niṟutti aḻakum teḷivum poruntak kūṟuvatum pōlvaṉa kaṟpōruḷḷattil avai naṉku patiyumāṟu ceyya mēṟkoṇṭa muṟaikaḷām eṉka.
iṉi, ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷil potuvum ciṟappum mutaliya vitikaḷ oṉṟaṉai yoṉṟu pōṟṟiyum maṟuttum cellum iyalpiṉavākaliṉ ilakkaṇaṅkaṟpār nūliṉ orupakuti kaṟṟu amaiyātu nūṉ muḻutuṅ kaṟkum kaṭappāṭuṭaiyarāvar. ak kaṭappāṭṭiṉiṉṟum avar vilakāmaip poruṭṭu ivar nūṟpākkaḷai amaittirukkuntiṟaṉ mika viyattaṟkuriyatu. iruḷ eṉṉuñ col niṉṟu vēṟṟumaip poruṭ puṇarcciyil varumoḻiyōṭu puṇarumāṟu kūṟavantavar.
"	iruḷeṉ kiḷavi veyiliyal nilaiyum"1
eṉṟār. veyil eṉṉuñ coṟku aṅṅaṉam viti kūṟumiṭattō
"	veyileṉ kiḷavi maḻaiyiyal nilaiyum"2
eṉṟār. maṟṟu; maḻai eṉṉuñ coṟku viti kūṟuṅkāl
"	maḻaiyeṉ kiḷavi vaḷiyiyal nilaiyum"3
eṉṟār.
vaḷi eṉṉuñ collukku vitiyum
"	vaḷiyeṉa varūum pūtak kiḷaviyum
avviyal nilaiyal cevvi teṉpa"4
eṉa ataṟku muṉṉuḷḷa cūttirattōṭu māṭṭeṟintu kūṟiṉār. accūttirattāṉ
"	paṉiyeṉa varūuṅ kāla vēṟṟumaik
kattum iṉṉum cāriyai yākum"5
eṉpatu. iṅṅaṉam uyirmayaṅkiyal 39ām cūttirattiṟ kūṟiya vitiyai 161 cūttiraṅkaṭkuppiṉ puḷḷimayaṅkiyaliluḷḷa 'iruḷeṉkiḷavi' eṉṉuñ cūttiraṅkāṟum toṭarntuvarac ceytiruttaliṉ ivaiyaṉaittaiyum oruṅku kaṟṟuṇarntālaṉṟi, iruḷ, veyil, maḻai, vaḷi eṉṉum coṟkaḷ attuccāriyaiyāvatu iṉcāriyaiyāvatu peṟṟu muṭiyum eṉpataṉai eṅṅaṉam aṟital cālum? ivai vevvēṟu uyirum puḷḷiyum iṟutiyākavuṭaiya coṟkaḷātaliṉ ivaṟṟai oruvaḻik koṇarntu oruṅku muṭittalum poruntāmai kāṇka. ivvāṟu muṭippaṉa maṟṟum palavuḷa. iṅṅaṉam cūttiraṅkaḷaik kōvaippaṭutti mutalum muṭivum māṟu kōḷiṉṟi ivvāciriyar amaittirukkum muṟaiyai uṇaralāṟṟāta cilar 'tolkāppiyattuḷ iccūttiram īṇṭiruttal poruntātu, āṇṭu iruttal vēṇṭum' eṉṟu tamakkut tōṟṟiyavāṟellām uraittal nakai eṉṉum meyppāṭṭiṟkuk karuviyām. attuṇaiyaṉṟivēṟeṉ payaṉuṭaittu?


17.paraṇip peyarkkāraṇam
tamiḻc ceyyuṭkaḷ taṉinilai, tokainilai, toṭarnilai eṉa muttiṟappaṭum. avaṟṟuḷ toṭarnilai, coṟṟoṭar nilai, poruṭṭoṭar nilai eṉa iruvakaippaṭum. poruṭṭoṭar nilaic ceyyuḷ nāṟporuḷ payakkum naṭaineṟittākip palliyalpāṉum virintu naṭappatum, avvāṟiṉṟik kuṟai pāṭuṭaiyatum eṉa iraṇṭām. avaṟṟuḷ muṉṉataṉaip peruṅkāppiyameṉpar. piṉṉataiyum atupōṉṟa coṟ ṟoṭarnilaic ceyyuḷaiyum cila taṉic ceyyuṭkaḷaiyum pirapantam eṉa vaḻaṅkuvar. tamiḻp pirapantaṅkaḷait toṇṇūṟṟāṟāka vakuttirukkiṉṟaṉar. avaṟṟuḷ paraṇi eṉpatoṉṟu. atu poruṭṭoṭar nilaiyākum. pōriṉ kaṇ āyiram yāṉaiyaiveṉṟa vīraṉmēl atu pāṭappaṭuvatu eṉpa,
"	āṉaiyāyiram amariṭaiveṉṟa māṉavaṉukku vakuppatu paraṇi"1
			    (ilakkaṇa viḷakkappāṭṭiyal)
paraṇiyiṉ ilakkaṇam,
"	kaṭavuḷ vāḻttuk kaṭaitiṟap puraittal
kaṭumpālai kūṟal koṭuṅkāḷi kōṭṭaṅ
kaṭikaṇa muraittal kāḷin ticolal
aṭupēyk kavaḷ cola lataṉāṟ ṟalaivaṉ
vaṇpuka ḻuraitta leṇpuṟat tiṇaiyuṟa
vīṭṭa laṭukaḷam vēṭṭa livaimē
laḷavaṭi mutalā vaṭiyiraṇ ṭāka
vuḷamakiḻ paraṇi yuraikkap paṭumē"2
			   (ilakkaṇa viḷakkappāṭṭiyal)
eṉpataṉāl uṇarka.
iṉi, paraṇiyeṉap pirapantap peyar pōnta kāraṇam palarāl palavāṟu kūṟappaṭum. avaiyellām, īṇṭuraippiṉ viriyum, kālañceṉṟa vi.kō.cūriyanārāyaṇa cāstiriyār, pi.ē., avarkaḷ tām acciṭṭa kaliṅkattup paraṇi mukavuraiyil paraṇiyeṉṉum peyarkkāraṇam vicārippāṉpukkup palaruṭaiya koḷkaikaḷaiyum eṭuttukkāṭṭi iṟutiyil tam koḷkaiyaip piṉvarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟaṉar:-
"īṇṭaik kūṟiya yāvarum paraṇi yeṉṉum collait tām nāṭōṟum vaḻakkamāyp pārkkum akarātikaḷi
leṭuttup pārttu, ataṉkaṇ accolliṟkuk kūṟappaṭṭa ovvoru poruḷaiyum ivvāṟeṭuttamaittup peritu
miṭarp paṭṭuc camarppittut tamakkuṭṭāmē maṉappāl kuṭittu makiḻuṟuvārāyiṉār. ituniṟka, iṉit tolkāppiyam poruḷatikārattuc ceyyuḷiyaliṟ koccakavorupōkiṉ ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟuñ cūttiravuraiyil tēvapāṇi mutalāyiṉa taṉakkiṉamākiya vaṇṇakattiṟkōtiya taraviṉṟit tāḻicai peṟumeṉavum  pāṇiyuḷellā miraṇṭaṭiyāṉē tāḻampaṭṭa
vōcai virāay varutalum muṭukivarutalu muḷaveṉavum,
"	uḷaiyāḻi yōrēḻu moru celavu ḷaṭaṅkutalāṉ
viḷaiyāṭa nīrpeṟā miṉṉuruvam parāvutumē."
eṉṟāṟ pōlum pāṇic ceyyuḷuṭ payiṉṟu varum pala tāḻicai toṭar poruḷavākaliṉ avaṟṟaip palavaṭukki variṉum tāḻicaiyā meṉka veṉavum, maṟṟup pāṇiyuṭ puṟattiṇai palavum virāay varutaliṉ atu tēvapāṇiyā
meṉṟateṉṉai yeṉiṉ, avaiyellāṅ kāṭukeḻu celvikkup pāṇi nāṭ kūḻum tuṇaṅkaiyuṅ koṭuttu vaḻipaṭuvatōr vaḻakkup paṟṟi ataṉuṭ pāṭṭuṭait talaivaṉaip peytu collappaṭuvaṉa ātalāṉ avaiyellā vāṟṟāṉun tēvapāṇiyēyām eṉpateṉavum kūṟāniṟṟaliṉ īṇṭuyām ārāyap pukunta paraṇi yeṉpatu pāṇiyeṉṟē yiruttal vēṇṭumeṉal peṟutum. aṉṟiyum muṟkālattu ōlaic
cuvaṭikaḷiṉ varivaṭiviṉkaṇ rakāra maṉaittuñ cila vuyirmeyn
neṭi lākārattōṭiyalum kāleṉa veḻutappaṭṭiruttalum oruvāṟu ematu kūṟṟai valiyuṟuttikiṉṟamai kāṇka."
piṟaraiyellām maṉappāl kuṭippavar eṉṟu kūṟi viṭṭuc cāstiriyār niṟuviyirukkum koḷkaiyai īṇṭārāyvōm. avar eṭuttuk kāṭṭiya tolkāppiya vuraiyiṉai nuṇṇitiṉ ārāyvārkkut "tēvapāṇi" eṉpatu tavira, maṟṟup pāṇi yeṉṟuḷḷaṉa vellām paraṇiyeṉṟē yiruttal vēṇṭum eṉpatu pulappaṭum. ōlaiccuvaṭikaḷiṉ varivaṭivil rakarattiṟkum neṭṭeḻuttiṉ kālukkum vēṟṟumai kāṇappaṭāmaiyiṉ piṟar iṅṅaṉam vaḻuviṉar eṉak karutiya cāstiriyār tāmē atil vaḻuvi yirukkiṉṟamai viyappākum! 'pāṇi nāṭ kūḻum tuṇaṅkaiyuṅ koṭuttu vaḻipaṭuvatu' eṉpataṟkuyātum poruḷillāmaiyai avar nōkkiṟṟilar. paraṇippāṭṭellām tēvapāṇiyām eṉpatē nacciṉārkkiṉiyar karuttu. ataṟkuk kāraṇamum avar kūṟiyuḷḷār. ivvāṟṟāl paraṇi vēṟu, pāṇivēṟu eṉpatu teḷika. 'paraṇiyāvatu kāṭukeḻu celvikkupparaṇi nāṭ kūḻum tuṇaṅkaiyum koṭuttu vaḻipaṭuvatōr vaḻakkup paṟṟiyatu' eṉa avar eḻutiyatu paraṇiyeṉṉum pirapantap peyariṉ kāraṇattaip puvappaṭuttukiṉṟatu. koṟṟavai yeṉpāḷ veṟṟit teyvamākaliṉ veṉṟi vēṇṭi avaḷai vaḻipaṭuvār veṟṟikkuriya paraṇi nāḷil kūḻiṭṭut tuṇaṅkaiyāṭi vaḻipaṭutal marapākum. veṉṟapiṉ kaḷakkūḻaṭṭup  pēykaṭku iṭuvatuṇṭu.  pēykaḷ koṟṟavaiyiṉ kaṇaṅkaḷākaliṉ, akkūḻum itaṉ kaṭ kūṟappaṭutaliṉ, paraṇiyeṉap peyar vantateṉka. kaliṅkattup paraṇi, kaḷampāṭiya pakutiyil 'kaḷapparaṇik kūḻpoṅki' eṉavum, 'paṇṭu mikumō paraṇik
kūḻ' eṉavum, 'varukūḻp paraṇik kaḷaṅkaṇṭu vantapēyai muṉṉūṭṭi, yorukūḻpparaṇi nāmirukku mūrkkaṭ pēykku vārīrē' eṉavum varutal kāṇka. nāṭpeyarāl paliyiṉpeyar vaḻaṅkutal 'tiruvātiraikkaḷi' mutaliya peyar vaḻakkukaḷālum uṇarka. ivvāṟṟāl paraṇik kūḻpaṟṟiyē pirapantattiṟkup paraṇiyeṉap peyar pōntateṉpatu tuṇipu.

18. kapilar eṉṉum nūliṉ ārāycci
eṉ naṇpar vittuvāṉ tiru. vē. vēṅkaṭarājalu reṭṭi yāravarkaḷāl eḻutappeṟṟu, ceṉṉaip palkalaik kaḻakat tiṉarāl veḷiyiṭappaṭṭa 'kapilar' eṉṉum urainūl ceṉṟa yāṇṭilē anuppappeṟṟu eṉakkuk kiṭaittatu. kiṭaittum, yāṉ cilayāṇṭukaḷāka nōyāl nalivuṟṟu avvap poḻutu ceyyaṟ pālavāya iṉṟiyamaiyā viṉaikaḷaṉṟip piṟitoṉṟum āṟṟakilātiruntu varutaliṉ ataṉaip paṭittup pārātiruntuviṭṭēṉ. ip paṅkuṉittiṅkaḷiṉ iṟutiyiṟ piṟitoru vēlaiyum muṉṉillāmaiyiṉ cevvi vāyttu ataṉaip paṭittaṟiyalāṉēṉ. reṭṭiyāravarkaḷatu ārāycci yaṟivai muṉṉamē 'paraṇar' eṉṉum nūlāl aṟintu ḷēṉākaliṉ, ippoḻutu putuvatāka viyappoṉṟum tōṉṟavillai. reṭṭiyār tamatunūliṉ muṉṉuraiyil, iṟṟaikkup patiṉāṟāṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉ eṉṉāl eḻuti veḷiyiṭap peṟṟa 'kapilar' eṉṉum urainūlaiccuṭṭi, aḵtirukkavum iḵtu eḻutiyatu mikaiyākāteṉaṟkuk kāraṇaṅ kūṟuvārāy, "nāṭṭāravarkaḷ perumpālum kallūri māṇavariṉ payaṉaikkaruti annūlai eḻutiyuḷḷārkaḷ. iḵtu aṉṉatōr nōkkattāl eḻutappaṭṭataṉṟu" eṉṟuraittu, iraṇṭiṟkumuḷḷa vēṟupāṭu cilavaṟṟaik kuṟittiruppatuṭaṉ, nūliṉakattum cila viṭaṅkaḷil annūṟkaruttukkaḷait tām vēṇṭumāṟṟāl āṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉar. aḵtu eṉpāl avar vaittuḷḷa aṉpiṟkum matippiṟkum aṟikuṟiyām eṉṟē eṇṇukiṟēṉ. eṉiṉum, eṉatu nūliṉ karuttukkaḷai āṇṭuḷḷa vakaimaiyiṟ kaṟpārkkut tiripuṇarcci uṇṭākāvāṟu cilavaṟṟai viḷakkutal kaṭaṉeṉak karutiyum, ataṉōṭu eṉakku amaitiyilavākat tōṉṟum annūṟporuḷkaḷiṟ cilavaṟṟai yēṉum veḷippaṭuttal aṉṉavar ārāycci piṉṉun tūymaiyuṟutaṟkum, tamiḻ vaḷarccikkum uriyatāmeṉa ōrntum avaiyiṟṟaic ciṟu kaṭṭuraikaḷāka eḻuti veḷiyiṭat tuṇintēṉ. icceyal annūliṉ āciriyar pāluḷḷa naṭpiṉ kiḻamaikkum, matippiṟkum, tamiḻ vaḷarccikkum poruntiya tākumeṉpatē eṉatu tuṇipu.
annūliṟ putumaiyākak kāṇappaṭum cila collāṭci muṟaiyai mutalil ārāytaṟku eṭuttuk koḷkiṉṟēṉ. mukittal, aṅkaṉam, kāṇkiṉṟatu, naṟkīrar eṉpaṉa muṟaiyē ārāyappaṭum.
"paraṇar eṉṉum nūlai yāṉ eḻuti mukitta piṉṉar" eṉa annūliṉ muṉṉurai toṭaṅkumiṭattē 'mukitta' eṉṟoru col āḷappaṭṭuḷatu.  'paraṇar' eṉṉum nūliṉ muṉṉuraiyilum "avarkaḷaṉaivaratu varalāṟṟaiyum oruṅkē eḻuti mukippatu aritākumākaliṉ" eṉavum, "tolkāppiyattiṟku ōr urai eḻutakkaruti; atil eḻuttatikārattiṟku ippōtu urai eḻuti mukittirukkiṉṟēṉ" eṉavum īriṭattil ivviṉaiyiṉ āṭci uḷatu. itaṉai vaḻaṅkuvatu vaḻuvākātu eṉṟu kāṭṭutaṟ poruṭṭu "mukintatu eṉṉuñcol, vaḻakkilum paḻaiya ēṭukaḷilum vaḻaṅkutalāṉum, 'āṟṟiṟ pālattai mukittē ṉallēṉ' (kuṟṟālattala, kaṇṭakacētaṉa. 39) eṉṟu ceyyuḷilum vantuḷḷataṉāṉum, 'mukitutu' eṉṟu kaṉṉaṭattilum, 'mukiseṉu' eṉṟu teluṅkilum vaḻaṅkutalāṉum, mukieṉṉuñcol toṉmaiyatē eṉpatu aṟiyappaṭum" eṉakkīḻkkuṟippil viḷakkam eḻutappaṭṭuḷatu. (kapilar-muṉṉurai.) tamiḻ lekcikaṉilum iccoṟkup poruḷ kūṟumiṭattē 'āṟṟiṟpālattai' eṉṉum immēṟkōḷum, kaṉṉaṭattilum teluṅkilum itu vaḻaṅku māṟum kāṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. caṅkappulavarkaḷaippaṟṟi ārāycciyurai eḻutum āciriyaroruvar piṟkālattut talapurāṇam oṉṟil ōriṭattuk kāṇappaṭuvataṉṟik kaṭalpōṟ parantuḷḷa tamiḻ ilakkiya ilakkaṇaṅkaḷil yāṇṭum vaḻaṅkappaṭātatum, uraiyāciriyaṉmār evarāṉum tīṇṭappaṭātatum, evviṭattu ettiṟattu makkaḷālō aruki vaḻaṅkappaṭuvatumākiya ticaiccol oṉṟai mutaṉmaiyāka nāṭṭuvatum, ataṉaip paḻaiya tamiḻc col eṉṟu niṟuttappukuvatum nērmaiyuṭaiyavākat tōṉṟa villai. 'mukitta' eṉṟu eḻutiyavuṭaṉ ataṉai viḷakkuvāṉ pukuntatē atu tamiḻmakkaḷiṉ vaḻakkilillātatu eṉpataṟkuc cāṉṟākum. aṉṟiyum, mukittal viṉaiyai nūṉ muḻutum koṇṭuykkātu muṉṉuraiyaḷavil muṭittiruppatu eḻutiṉōrukkum aḵtu aruvaruppu viḷaittirukku meṉpataṉaik kāṭṭā niṟkum.
iṉi, 'aṅṅaṉam' mutaliyavākat tamiḻaṟiñar palarāṉum vaḻaṅkappaṭuñ coṟkaḷ itaṉakattum, 'paraṇar' eṉṉum nūliṉakattum aṅkaṉam, iṅkaṉam, eṅkaṉam, yāṅkaṉam eṉṟiṅṅaṉamē vaḻaṅkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ivvāṟu vaḻaṅkuvatē cevvitu eṉpatu ivaṟṟai eḻutiṉōr āyntukaṇṭa muṭipākum.
"	āṅkaṉam taṇikuva tāyiṉ yāṅkum"	   (naṟ.322)
'	īṅkaṉañ celka tāṉeṉa eṉṉai yāṅkaṟin taṉaṉō tāṅkaruṅ kāvalaṉ'	   (puṟa.208)
'yāṅkaṉam ottiyō vīṅkucelaṉ maṇṭilam'    (puṟa.8)
eṉṟu, āṅkaṉam īṅkaṉam yāṅkaṉam eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷē paḻaiya ceyyuṭkaḷil vantiruttalāṉum, ṅakaram moḻimutaṟ kaṇ vārāteṉṟu tolkāppiyam kūṟutalāṉum, aṅkaṉam āṅkaṉam iṅkaṉam īṅkaṉam eṅkaṉam yāṅkaṉam eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷē paḻamaiya eṉpatu aṟiyappaṭum. ātaliṉ, yāṉ innūlil aṅkaṉam iṅkaṉam yāṅkaṉam eṉṉuñ coṟ kaḷaiyē vaḻaṅkiyuḷḷēṉ." (kapilar-muṉṉurai) eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭiruttal kāṇka.
kuṟṟālat talapurāṇattil ōriṭattu vantu viṭṭatu eṉpatu koṇṭu 'mukittal' viṉaiyai āḷat toṭaṅkiṉōr pala nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷākap palarāṉum vaḻakkiṉum ceyyuḷiṉum vaḻaṅkappaṭṭu varuvatum, oru ciṟanta āciriyarāl ilakkaṇam vitikkappaṭṭatum ākiya collai avvaṭivil vaḻaṅka maṟuppatu viyappākavuḷḷatu. naṉṉūlāciriyar iṟṟaikku eḻunūṟāṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉpiruntavar eṉpatu yāvarkkum oppa muṭintatu. avar
"	cuṭṭiyā vekara viṉāvaḻi yavvai
oṭṭi ṅavvum mutalā kummē"
eṉṟu ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟiyiruttaliṉ avar kālattiṉ muṉpē aṅṅaṉam mutaliya colvaḻakku avvaṭivuṭaṉ nilaviya
teṉpatu peṟappaṭum. avvāṟu payila vaḻaṅkuvataṉai aṟavē yotukkip putiyatoru vāypāṭukoṇṭu vaḻaṅkuvatu marapu nilai tirittup poruḷuṇarccikku ūṟu ceyvatēyākum.
	"marapunilai tiriyiṟ piṟitupiṟi tākum"
eṉṉum tolkāppiyac cūttiramum, 'marapiṉai nilaitirittuc collupaveṉiṉ, ulakattuc collellām poruḷiḻantu piṟitu piṟitākum' eṉa ataṟkup pērāciriyar kūṟiya uraiyum nōkkaṟpālaṉa.
iṉi, paḻaiya ceyyuṭkaḷil 'aṅṅaṉam' mutaliyaṉa vaḻaṅkāmaiyiṉ uṇmaiyum, tolkāppiya vitiyiṉmaiyum āytaṟkuriyaṉa. ṅakara voṟṟu akaravuyiruṭaṉaṉṟip piṟavuyirkaḷōṭu tamiḻil yāṇṭum varutal illai. avvuṭaṉ kūṭiya 'ṅa' eṉṉum uyirmey tāṉum aṅṅaṉam iṅṅaṉam uṅṅaṉam eṅṅaṉam yāṅṅaṉam eṉṉum aintu coṟkaḷilaṉṟi vēṟiṭattu uṭaṉilai yāka mayaṅki varuvataṉṟu. ākaliṉ, ceyyuḷil etukait toṭaiyil accoṟkaḷai amaittal ariteṉpatu kūṟal vēṇṭā. ṅakarattuṭaṉ mayaṅkutaṟkuriya vēṟumey ataṉōṭu piṟappoṟṟumaiyum oliyoṟṟumaiyumuṭaiya kakaram oṉṟumēyām. ākaliṉ, ceyyuṭ ceyyum pulavar etukait toṭaiyil ṅakarattaik kakaramākat tirittu vaḻaṅkutal iyalpē. "yāṅṅaṉa mottiyō vīṅkucelaṉ maṇṭilam" eṉat tiriyātu kūṟiṉum etukai yātaṟkiḻukkiṉṟām. iṟaiyaṉārkaḷaviyaliṉ mūṉṟāñ cūttiram "āṅṅaṉam puṇarnta kiḻavōṉ" eṉṟē paṭikkap paṭṭu varukiṉṟatu. aṉṟiyum vaḻaṅkutaṟku arumaiyuṭaiya coṟkaḷē eḷiyavākat tirital iyalpu. marūuc coṟkaḷiṟ pala avvāṟu vantaṉavē. aṅkaṉam eṉpatē paḻaiya vuruvāyiṉ vaḻaṅkutaṟku eḷimaiyuṭaiya accol aṅṅaṉam eṉattirital poruttamiṉṟām. āyiṉ, ṅakaram moḻimutaṟkaṇ vārā teṉṟu tolkāppiyam kūṟutaliṉ 'ṅaṉam' eṉpatu toṉmaiyatātal eṅṅaṉam eṉiṉ, aṅṅaṉam mutaliya coṟkaḷai aṅṅaṉam eṉṟiṅṅaṉam pirittup puṇarttuvatu tolkāppiyar karuttaṉṟām. ākaliṉ, ṅakaram moḻimutaṟkaṇ varumeṉa avarvitittilar.
"	pakuti vikuti iṭainilai cāriyai
canti vikāram āṟiṉum ēṟpavai
muṉṉip puṇarppa muṭiyum eppataṅkaḷum"
eṉṟu naṉṉūlār oru collaiyē mutaṉilai mutaliyavākap pirittu muṭikkumāṟu koṇṭāṟ pōlat tolkāppiyar koṇṭilar. toṭarmoḻikaḷiluṅkūṭap paṇputtokai, viṉaittokai mutaliyavaṟṟai niṟutta collum kuṟittu varukiḷaviyumākap puṇarkkalākāteṉal avar karutteṉpatu "uyirum puḷḷiyum iṟutiyāki" eṉṉuñ cūttirattāṉ aṟiyap paṭum. 'ṅavvum mutalākum' eṉak kūṟiya naṉṉūlārum cuṭṭu viṉākkaḷiṉ piṉ varumeṉṟārēyaṉṟit taṉittu mutalāku meṉak kūṟiṉārallār. ākaliṉ, tolkāppiyaṉār ṅakaram moḻi mutalil varumeṉak kūṟāmaiyiṉ aṅṅaṉam eṉpatu paḻaiya vaḻakkākāteṉat tuṇital poruntāmai ōrka. āyiṉ, atu paḻaiya vaḻakkē eṉat tuṇītaṟkuttāṉ cāṉṟu eṉṉai eṉiṉ, kūṟuval. tolkāppiya eḻuttatikārattiṉ mutal iyalākiya nūṉ marapil uṭaṉilai meymmayakkam kūṟum nūṟpā
"	meynnilai cuṭṭiṉ ellā veḻuttum tammuṟ ṟāmvarūum raḻavalaṅ kaṭaiyē"
eṉpatu. itil, ra, ḻa eṉṉum iraṇṭum oḻinta patiṉāṟu meyyum tammuṉ tām vantu mayaṅkumeṉa āciriyar vitit tiruttaliṉ ṅakara oṟṟiṉmuṉ ṅakaramvantu mayaṅkum col iruntatākal vēṇṭumaṉṟē? tamiḻilē cuṭṭu viṉākkaḷiṉ piṉ ṅaṉam eṉpatu iyaintuvarum aṅṅaṉam mutaliya coṟkaḷaṉṟi ṅakaram uṭaṉilaiyāka mayaṅkaṟkuriya vēṟu col yātuḷatu? tiru. vēṅkaṭarājalu reṭṭiyāravarkaḷ pala yāṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉpē tolkāppiya eḻuttatikārattiṟku uraiyeḻuti muṭittuḷār eṉpatu 'paraṇar' eṉṉum nūliṉ muṉṉuraiyāl aṟiyappaṭukiṉṟatu. ataṉkaṇ, mēl eṭuttukkāṭṭiya "meyn nilai cuṭṭiṉ" eṉṉuñ cūttirattiṟkum uraiyum, utāraṇamum eḻutap paṭṭirukkavē vēṇṭum. avaiyiṟṟai iṭaiyē orukāl niṉaikkavu muṭiyāmaṟ ceytamaṟatiyiṉ vaṉmaiyē vaṉmai. am maṟatiyāl avar tāmeḻutiya puttakaṅkaḷ, kaṭṭuraikaḷ yāvaṟṟiṉum piḻaiyuḷḷa tōr muṭipiṉai mēṟ koṇṭārāvar.
tolkāppiyam, eṭṭuttokai mutaliya caṅkavilakkiyaṅ kaṭkup pala nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉṉarttōṉṟiyateṉpatu reṭṭiyāravarkaṭkum uṭaṉpāṭē. annūliṉālē aṟutiyiṭap paṭutaliṉ aṅṅaṉam mutaliya col vaḻakkum paḻamaiyateṉpatu pōtarum. ataṉaiyaṟintē naṉṉūlārum ataṟkumāṟiṉṟit tamatu matamumtōṉṟa ilakkaṇam kūṟippōntāreṉka.
iṉi, kapilar eṉṉum annūliṉkaṇ "purāṇak kataikaḷum pala kāṇkiṉṟaṉa; cilavaṟṟil ovvoru collē kāṇ kiṉṟatu; ciṟitum māṟupāṭillāta aṭikaḷum parakkak kāṇutaliṉ; aivar pulavar iyaṟṟiyaṉa eṉak kāṇkiṉṟaṉa vākaliṉ; kapilar pāṭiya kalippāṭṭukkaḷuḷḷum nāṭaka vaḻakkuk kāṇkiṉṟatu; nālacaiccīraippaṟṟiya ilakkaṇamum kāṇkiṉṟatu; ñāṉa veṭṭiyāṉ eṉṉum nūlilum kāṇkiṉṟatu; oṟṟu eṉa ṟakara ukaram kāṇkiṉṟatu; ḻakarattukku ṭakaram kāṇkiṉṟatu. kakara ḻakaram māṟik kāṇkiṉṟatu." (pakkam-33, 34, 35, 37, 38, 44, 49, 116, 117.) eṉṟiṅṅaṉam palaviṭattum 'kāṇ' eṉpataṉaṭiyākavarum viṉaiccoṟkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivai palaviṭattum parakkak kāṇappaṭutaliṉ, iṅṅaṉam eḻutuvatē ilakkaṇam eṉakkaruti eḻutappaṭṭavātal vēṇṭum. āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār
"	viṉaiyē ceyvatu ceyappaṭu poruḷē
 nilaṉē kālam karuvi yeṉṟā
iṉṉataṟ kitupaya ṉāka veṉṉum
aṉṉa marapi ṉiraṇṭoṭun tokaii
āyeṭ ṭeṉpa toḻiṉmuta ṉilaiyē"
eṉpataṉāl, viṉai tōṉṟutaṟkuriya kāraṇaṅkaḷaik kūṟi,
"	avaitām,
vaḻaṅkiyaṉ maruṅkiṟ kuṉṟuva kuṉṟum"
eṉpataṉāl, cila viṉaikkaṇ avaṟṟuṭ kuṉṟat takuvaṉa cila kuṉṟivarum eṉṟār. cēṉāvaraiyar "kuṉṟattakuvaṉavāvaṉa ceyappaṭu poruḷum ēṟpatum payaṉumām" eṉa ataṉai viḷakki, koṭiyāṭiṟṟu, vaḷivaḻaṅkiṟṟu eṉa avai kuṉṟi varutaṟku utāraṇaṅkāṭṭiṉar. eṉavē cila viṉaikaḷiṟ ceyappaṭu poruḷ kuṉṟivarutal iyalpu. kāṇṭal viṉaiyō ceyap paṭuporuḷ kuṉṟātatu. ceyappaṭu poruḷai viritturaikkum "kāppiṉ oppiṉ" eṉṉum tolkāppiyac cūttirattil "nōkkaliṉ" eṉa varutal kāṇka. kāppu mutalāyiṉavellām poruḷpaṟṟi ōtappaṭṭaṉavākaliṉ, nōkkal eṉpatil apporuḷ paṟṟivarum kāṇṭal, pārttal eṉpaṉavum aṭaṅkum. eṉavē, kāṇ eṉpataṉaṭiyākavarum viṉaiccoṟ kaḷai, purāṇak kataikaḷum pala kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa, cilavaṟṟil ovvoru collē kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu eṉṟiṅṅaṉam vaḻaṅkutalē marapākum. uraiyāciriyar palarum ammarapu piṟaḻāmalē vaḻaṅki varuvārāyiṉar.
"	ceyappaṭu poruḷaic ceytatu pōlat
toḻiṟpaṭak kiḷattalum vaḻakkiyaṉ marapē"
eṉṉuñ cūttiram vaḻakkiṉkaṇ orōvaḻic ceyappaṭu poruḷ viṉaimutal pōlak kūṟappaṭum marapu vaḻuvai amait taṟkeḻuntatē. avvaḻuvamaitiyaiyē mēṟkoṇṭu nūl uraikaḷil yāṇṭum eḻututal muṟaimaiyaṉṟām. iṉi, vēṟṟumoḻip payiṟci oṉṟē koṇṭu tamiḻiṉ iyalpiṉai viḷakkaluṟuvār cilar tolkāppiyattilē ceyappāṭṭu viṉaiyaippaṟṟiya kuṟippu illai eṉavum, ākaliṉ tamiḻiṉ kaṇ ceyappāṭṭuviṉai iṉṟeṉavum kūṟāniṟpar. ceyappaṭu poruḷ eṉṉum peyariliruntē ceyappāṭṭuviṉaiyuṇmai peṟappaṭuvataṉaiyum, tolkāppiya mutaṟ cūttirattiṉ toṭakkamē "eḻutteṉappaṭupa" eṉa ataṉaik kuṟippiṭu talaiyum,
"	collap paṭṭaṉa ellā māṇpum
maṟutalai yāyiṉum maṟṟatu citaivē"
eṉap piṟāṇṭum aḵtu āḷappaṭutalaiyum avar aṟiyār. reṭṭiyār ceyappāṭṭuviṉai palavaṟṟait tām vaḻaṅkutaliṉ akkoḷkaiyiṉar allareṉpatu peṟappaṭum. ōr araip
pakkattilē (pak.137) "kūṟappaṭṭiruttaliṉ, kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷa, tokukkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa, kāṭṭap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa" eṉṟiṅṅaṉam eṭṭiṭattil vaḻaṅkiyuḷḷamai kāṇka. aṅṅaṉamākavum kāṇṭal viṉai oṉṟiṉmaṭṭil avar karuttu māṟupaṭṭatu eṉṉaiyō? āyiṉ, kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu, kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉa ōriṭattum avar vaḻaṅkāmaṟ pōkavillai. eṉiṉum muṉṉatu peru varaviṟṟākavum, piṉṉatu ciṟuvaraviṟṟākavum iruttalai nōkkiṉ, kāṇkiṉṟatu eṉpataṉaiyē avar niṉaivu kūrntu vaḻaṅkiyuḷḷār eṉpatum, kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu eṉpatu payiṟci vayattāl avaraṟiyāmalē pōntatām eṉpatum peṟappaṭum. kaṟpōr putuvatākavuḷḷa taṉaiyē viyappuṭaṉ nōkku varātaliṉ kāṇkiṉṟatu eṉpatē avaruḷḷattil nilaipeṟuva tākum. oru palkalaikkaḻakattiṉ ārāycci viṉaiñarāl eḻutappaṭṭu, uyarnta tāḷiṟ pariya uruviṉatākap polivuṟa acciṭappaṭṭuḷḷa puttakattiṟ kāṇappaṭutaliṉ ettuṇaiyar ivviḻi vaḻakkiṉai viḻumiya vaḻakkākak koṇṭaṉarō eṉa uṉṉa accamuṇṭākiṉṟatu.
iṉi, tamiḻ nūṟkaḷil yāṇṭum nakkīrar eṉa vaḻaṅkuñcol itil naṟkīrar eṉa āḷappaṭṭuḷatu. itaṉ āciriyar muṉṉar eḻutiya 'paraṇar' eṉṉum nūliṉum nakkīrar eṉṟē palaviṭattum vantuḷatu. annūliṉ kīḻk kuṟippukkaḷuḷ oṉṟilētāṉ "natkīrar eṉpatu naṟkīrar eṉa vaḻaṅkiya colliṉ tiripākalām eṉat tōṉṟukiṉṟatu" eṉa vaṭanūl oṉṟilē kaṇṭa 'natkīrar' eṉṉum vaṭivukku viḷakkam eḻutuḻi naṟkīrar eṉṟu kuṟikkap paṭṭuḷatu. innūliṉkaṇ nakkīrar eṉṉum vaḻakku aṟavē kaiviṭappeṟṟu, naṟkīrar eṉpatē vaḻaṅkumāṟu mēṟkoḷḷap paṭṭuḷatu. "iṉi, nallantuvaṉār nalliṟaiyaṉār nalluruttiraṉār eṉa uyir mutaṉmoḻikaḷ vantaviṭattum, naṉṉākaṉār naṉṉākaiyār eṉamelleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻikaḷ vanta viṭattum, nalviḷakkaṉār nalveḷḷiyār nalvēṭṭaṉār eṉa iṭaiyeḻuttu mutaṉmoḻikaḷ vanta viṭattum nal eṉṉum aṭaimoḻiyē vantiruttaliṉ valleḻuttu mutaṉ moḻikaḷ varumiṭattum naleṉṉum aṭaimoḻi niṟṟalē muṟaiyāmāka lāṉum, naṟkāṭci nalloḻukkam naṉñāṉam eṉṟu ciṟappuṭaip poruḷkaḷaik kūṟumiṭattum nal eṉṉum aṭaimoḻiyē vantiruttalāṉum, piṟavāṟṟāṉum naṟkīrar eṉṉuñ collē amaivuṭaiyateṉṟum, nakkīrar eṉṉuñcol marūumoḻi eṉṟum tērntu, naṟkīrar eṉṉuñ collai mēṟ koṇṭuḷḷēṉ" eṉa attuṇipiṟku ētuk kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
naṟkīrar eṉṉuñ collē nakkīrar eṉat tirintatākal vēṇṭum eṉpatu eṉkaruttiṟkum icaintatē. nāṉkāṇṭu kaḷiṉ muṉ karantait tamiḻc caṅkattiṉ oru ciṟappukkūṭṭattil, 'nallantuvaṉār' eṉṉum poruḷpaṟṟiyāṉ pēciyapoḻutu, navvantuvaṉār eṉa vaḻaṅkutal poruntāteṉa viḷakkuḻi itaṉai viritturaikkalāṉēṉ. eṉiṉum cāṉṟōr vaḻakkoṭu muraṇi naṟkīrar eṉa vaḻaṅkutal takutiyaṉṟu eṉa eṇṇi yāṉ avvaḻakkiṉai mēṟkoṇṭilēṉ. valliṉa mutaṉmoḻikaḷ varumiṭattu māttiramē nakkaṇṇaiyār, nakkīraṉār, nacceḷḷaiyār, nattattaṉār, nappacalaiyār, nappaṇṇaṉār, nappālattaṉār eṉa nal eṉpatu tirintu varutaliṉ ataṟku ētu eṉṉeṉa ārāytalē muṟaimaiyām. vaḻaṅkutaṟku eṇmaiyatu ātalē mutalil ataṟkuk kāraṇamātal vēṇṭum. valleḻuttu āṟaṉuḷ kacatapa eṉṉum nāṉku meyyum uṭaṉilaiyākavaṉṟi vēṟu meyyuṭaṉ mayaṅkutal illai. ataṟkuk kāraṇam arumuyaṟciyuṭaṉ ataṉaikkūṟa vēṇṭiyiruttalē. tamiḻeḻuttukkaḷiṉ mayakka vitiyellām avai perumpālum eḷiya muyaṟciyuṭaṉ vaḻaṅkutaṟ payattavē. ṭa ṟa eṉṉum iraṇṭu meyyum ka ca pa eṉpavaṟṟōṭu mayaṅkumāyiṉum ataṉai vaḻaṅkutaṟkuc ciṟitu arumuyaṟci vēṇṭappaṭukiṉṟatu.
"	kappat tintiraṉ kāṭṭiya nūliṉ
meyppāṭ ṭiyaṟkai viḷaṅkak kāṇāy"
eṉa oru moḻiyilum iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ ṟakarattaip pakaramākat tirittiruppatu kāṇka. makaḷir pēccil nappeṇṭu, nappiḷḷai, naccol eṉpaṉa kēṭkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivvāṟṟāl, oru kuṟiliṉpiṉ varum ṟakaravoṟṟu vēṟu meykaḷōṭu mayaṅkiṉ ciṟitu vaḻaṅkutaṟku ariyatātalpeṟappaṭum. aḵtu uṭaṉilaiyāka mayaṅkuḻiyum attakaiyatē. iḷampirāyat tiṉaraip pēcuvittu ippoḻutum ataṉai aṟital cālum; eṉiṉum atukoṇṭē attakaiya coṟkaḷ palavaṟṟaiyum tirittu vaḻaṅkutal karuttaṉṟām.
nakkīrar mutaliya vaḻakkup paṇṭutoṭṭu nilaipeṟṟu varuvatoṉṟu. iraṇṭu tiruviḷaiyāṭaṟ purāṇaṅkaḷilum, cīkāḷattip purāṇam mutaliyavaṟṟilum nakkīrar eṉṉuñcol palaviṭattu vantuḷatu. curuṅkavuraikkiṉ paḻaiyavum putiyavumāṉa nūl, uraikaḷ yāvaṟṟiṉum naṟkīrar eṉpatu aṉṟu, nakkīrar eṉpatē kāṇappaṭuvatu eṉṉalām. eṭṭuttokai, pattup pāṭṭukkaḷilē pāṭiṉōr peyaraip pulappaṭuttum kuṟippuk kaḷil nakkīraṉār, nacceḷḷaiyār mutaliya peyarkaḷ vaḻaṅki yiruttaliṉ avai tokukkappeṟṟa attuṇaip paḻaṅkālan toṭaṅkiyēāṉṟōr palarāṉum avai aṅṅaṉam vaḻaṅkap
peṟṟaṉa eṉpatu pōtarum. muṟkālattu nallicaip pulavarkaḷ oruvar oruvaraip pārāṭṭum iyalpiṉar eṉpatu
"	pulaṉaḻuk kaṟṟa antaṇāḷaṉ
paranticai niṟkap pāṭiṉaṉ"
"	iṉṟum paraṇaṉ pāṭiṉaṉ"
"	antuvaṉ pāṭiya cantukeḻu neṭuvarai"
eṉṉum ivaimutaliyavaṟṟāl aṟiyappaṭum. avarkaḷ oruvar ceyyuḷiluḷḷa ciṟappuṭaiya toṭarkaḷiṉ cuvaiyai nukarntu iṉpuṟutalum, avaṟṟaiyum, avaṟṟai iyaṟṟiṉōraiyum pārāṭṭutalum uṭaiyar eṉpatu oruvar pāṭṭiluḷḷa coṟ
ṟoṭar aṅṅaṉamē palaruṭaiya pāṭṭukkaḷil varutalāṉum, patiṟṟuppattiluḷḷa ovvoru pāṭṭukkaḷum maṟam vīṅkupal pukaḻ, cāṉṟōr meymmaṟai eṉṟivvāṟu avaṟṟil vantuḷḷa ciṟappuṭaiya toṭarkaḷāṟ peyar kūṟappaṭutalāṉum, cila pulavarkaḷ aṇilāṭu muṉṟilār, irumpiṭarttalaiyār eṉṟiṅṅaṉam aṉṉōr pāṭṭiluḷḷa toṭarpaṟṟi eḻunta ciṟappup peyarkaḷāl vaḻaṅkap peṟutalāṉum aṟiyappaṭum. iṅṅaṉam ciṟanta pulavaroruvar peyariṉ muṉṉuḷḷa nal eṉṉum aṭaimoḻiyait tirittu nakkīraṉār eṉṟāṟpōla oruvar vaḻaṅkā niṟka ēṉōrum ataṉaip pārāṭṭi avvāṟu vaḻaṅkutaṟkuriya piṟapulavar peyarkaḷaiyum avvaṇam vaḻaṅkiṉārātal vēṇṭum. avvaḻakkē piṉṉarum pāṭṭu uraikaḷiṟpayiṉṟu varuvatāyiṟṟu. iṅṅaṉam payiṟcimikka valiyuṭaiya vaḻakkiṉaip puṟakkaṇittu 'naṟkīrar' eṉa aḻivaḻakkut toṭarvatu muṟaiyaṉṟām. ippeyariṉai iṅṅaṉam tirittōr iṉi nakkaṇṇaiyār, nacceḷḷaiyār mutaliya ēṉaip peyarkaḷaiyum tirittu vaḻaṅkum iyalpuṭaiyarāvar. iṅṅaṉam ivartām niṉaittapaṭiyellām coṟkaḷiṉ uruvaimāṟṟi vaḻaṅkuvarēl, atu teluṅku, malaiyāḷam pōlum piṟitoru moḻiyāvataṉṟit tamiḻākātu eṉka.

19. ceppamuṭaiyārkkuc ceyyum viṇṇappam
vittuvāṉ tiru vēṅkaṭarācalu avarkaḷ, 
ceṉṉaip palkalaikkaḻakam, ceṉṉai.
yāṉ eḻutiya 'kapilar' eṉṉum ārāycci nūlaippaṟṟit tiru. na.mu.vēṅkaṭacāmi nāṭṭāravarkaḷ 'tamiḻppoḻi'lil (13-2) eḻutiyirukkuṅ kaṭṭuraiyaip pārttēṉ. nāṭṭāravarkaḷ, eṉatu ārāycci tūymaiyuṟutaṟ poruṭṭum tamiḻ vaḷartaṟ
poruṭṭum annūlil (kapilar) taṅkaṭku amaitiyilavākat tōṉṟum poruḷkaḷuṭ cilavaṟṟai veḷiyiṭuvatākak kūṟi, mutaṟkaṇ, cila coṟkaḷaik kuṟittu atil eḻutiyirukkiṉṟārkaḷ. naṇpaṉatu māciṉaip pōkkit tūymaip paṭuttuvatu naṉmakkaḷ ceyalēyaṉṟō? atu karuti nāṭṭāravarkaḷiṉ peruntakaimaiyaip pārāṭṭutalōṭu avarkaḷ pāl naṉṟiyaṟitalum uṭaiyēṉ. avarkaḷ ārāyntirukkiṉṟa coṟkaḷāvaṉa:
1. 	mukittal
2. 	āṅkaṉam aṅkaṉam, īṅkaṉam iṅkaṉam, yāṅkaṉam eṅkaṉam
3. 	kāṇkiṉṟatu, kāṇkiṉṟaṉa
4. 	naṟkīrar
	eṉpaṉa.1 ivaṟṟuḷ, mukittal eṉṉuñ collaik kuṟittu avarkaḷ 	eḻutiyiruppataṉ curukkam:
1. 	mukittal eṉpatu ticaiccol.
2. 	atu tamiḻilakkiya ilakkaṇaṅkaḷil (oru  tala purāṇattil ōriṭattilaṉṟi)  		vaḻaṅkappaṭavillai.
3. 	caṅkap pulavaraippaṟṟiya ārāycci nūlil, piṟkālattu mika arukivantuḷḷa 	collai vaḻaṅkutal nērmaiyaṉṟu.
4.	 mukittal eṉṉuñcol aruvaruppiṉai viḷaippatu. iḵtu, ataṉai 	nūlmuḻuvatum vaḻaṅkāmaiyāṉē aṟiyappaṭum.
ivaṟṟai muṟaiyē ārāytum:1
1.	mukittal eṉṉuñcol ticaiccol eṉpatu etaṉāṟ pulaṉākiṉṟatō, aṟikilēṉ. atu teluṅkilum kaṉṉaṭattilum vaḻaṅkutalāṉ ticaiccollākum eṉiṉ, (avvāṟāyiṉ) tamiḻccoṟkaḷuṭ pallāyirañ coṟkaḷ ticaiccoṟkaḷē
yākum. paḻaiya tamiḻilakkiyaṅkaḷil vaḻaṅkātataṉāl, atu ticaiccollākum eṉiṉ, avaṟṟil vaḻaṅkātaṉavellām ticaic coṟkaḷēyākumaṉṟō? āyiṉ, nāṉ nīṅkaḷ, anta2, inta, enta, cari, aṉuppu, ciri mutaliyaṉavellām paḻaiya tamiḻilakkiyaṅkaḷil vaḻaṅkātaṉavākaliṉ ticaiccoṟkaḷēyākum. avvāṟāyiṉ collārāyccit tiṟaṉillāta pulavar, nāṉ, nīṅkaḷ mutaliya coṟkaḷai vaḻaṅkiṉum, ataṉaiyuṭaiya nāṭṭāravarkaḷ vaḻaṅkutal takātaṉṟē! aṟṟaṉṟu. nāṉ eṉpatu yāṉ eṉpataṉ marūu; nīṅkaḷ3 eṉpatu nīyir eṉpataṉ marūu; anta, inta, enta eṉpavai a, i, e eṉṉum eḻuttukkaḷiṉ marūu. ākaliṉ, avai tamiḻc coṟkaḷē. ciri eṉpatu paṇṭu illāviṭiṉum piṉṉart tamiḻiṟṟōṉṟiyatuvē eṉṟu kūṟuvārkaḷpōlum. iṉi, aṉuppu eṉpatu viṭukkum eṉpataṉ marūu eṉṟum, ciri eṉpatu nakai eṉpataṉ marūu eṉṟuṅ kūṟiviṭalāmpōlum.
mukittal eṉpatu ticaiccol eṉṟē koḷḷiṉum, ceyyuḷilum vaḻakkilum vaḻaṅkutaliṉ, ataṉai urainaṭaiyil vaḻaṅkutal tavaṟātal yāṅkaṉam? ticaiccel vaḻaṅkalākātu eṉṟu ilakkaṇam uḷatō? āyiṉ 'aṉuppu' eṉṉuñ collai nāṭṭāravarkaḷ vaḻaṅkutal takātaṉṟē?4 accol (aṉuppu) paṇṭaic ceyyuṭkaḷil yāṇṭum vantilāmaiyāṉum, teluṅkil maṭṭumē uṇmaiyāṉum atu teluṅkiṉiṉṟu vantatātal kūṭum.5
2. mukittal eṉṉuñcol talapurāṇam oṉṟil ōriṭattu maṭṭum vantuḷatu eṉṟu nāṭṭāravarkaḷ eḻutiyirukkiṟārkaḷ. aḵtu aruṇakirinātar tiruppukaḻil palaviṭattil vantuḷatu. tamiḻleksikaṉil iccollai nōkkiya nāṭṭāravarkaḷ atiṟkuṟittirukkun tiruppukaḻaṭikaḷaik kaṇṭiruttal kūṭumē. aṅkaṉamākavum avarkaḷ, 'piṟkālattut talapurāṇam oṉṟil ōriṭattuk kāṇappaṭuvatu' eṉṟu eḻutiyiruppatu viyappiṉai viḷaikkiṉṟatu. aruṇakirinātarkālam 14-ām nūṟṟāṇṭu eṉṟu teritaliṉ iṟṟaikku 500 yāṇṭukaṭku muṉṉarē iccol tamiḻil vaḻaṅki vantuḷatu teḷivākum. paḻaiya ōlaiyēṭukaḷiṉ iṟutiyil, muṟṟiṟṟu eṉṟātal, mukintatu eṉṟātal eḻutiyiruttalai ōlaic cuvaṭi nōkkiṉār naṉkaṟivar. ākaliṉ, iccolvaḻakkup putiyataṉṟu eṉpatu aṟiyappaṭu maṉṟē? niṟka. tamiḻilakkiya ilakkaṇaṅkaḷil tamiḻmoḻic coṟkaḷ ellām vantuḷḷaṉavō? vantilātavaṟṟaiyum vaḻaṅkumpōḻtu, vantuḷḷataṉai vaḻaṅkutal kūṭātu eṉṟal amaiyumā ṟeṉṉaiyō!
3. caṅkappulavaraippaṟṟi eḻutum urainūlil, piṟkālattu vanta collai vaḻaṅkutal takātu eṉṟārkaḷ. avvāṟu caṅkac ceyyuṭkaḷil vantilāta coṟkaḷai mēṟkoḷḷāteḻutiṉ aḵtu ikkālattu urainūlātalē illai. niṟka. caṅkappulavar ceyyuḷukku eḻutiyirukkum uraikaḷil, acceyyuṭkaḷil vantilātaṉavākiya uppukkaṇṭam, kaṇṭa carukkarai, kiṭāram, kuttalarici, cumaṭu mutaliya coṟkaḷai uraiyāḷar vaḻaṅkiyiruttal nāṭṭāravarkaḷ karuttiṟkuc ciṟitum amaiyātaṉṟē? caṅkap pulavar tañceyyuṭkaḷiṉ uraiyilē ittakaiya piṟkālattuc coṟkaḷ vantirukka, avaraik kuṟittu ikkālattil eḻutum urainūliṟ piṟkālattuc collai -caṅkakālattu vaḻaṅkāta collai - eḻututal kūṭātu eṉṟu nāṭṭāravarkaḷ colla villai. vaḻaṅkutal kūṭātu eṉpatu amaivuṭaiyatākumō?
4. mukittal eṉṉuñ collai nūṉ muḻuvatilum vaḻaṅkāta taṉāl accol eḻutiṉōrukkē aruvaruppiṉai viḷait tirukkum eṉṟārkaḷ. mukintatu eṉpatu muṟṟiṟṟu - pūrttiyāyiṟṟu eṉṉum poruḷatu. iccollai nūṉ muḻuvatilum evvāṟu vaḻaṅkutal kūṭum?
āṅkaṉam mutaliya coṟkaḷ:
āṅkaṉam mutaliya coṟkaḷaik kuṟittu nāṭṭāravarkaḷ eḻutiyiruppataṉ karuttukkaḷ:
1. naṉṉūlār aṅṅaṉam mutaliya coṟkaḷai vitikūṟi amaittuḷār.
2. aṅṅaṉam mutaliyavaṟṟai avvaṭivil vaḻaṅka maṟuppatu viyappākavuḷḷatu.
3. 'putiyatoru vāypāṭu koṇṭu vaḻaṅkuvatu marapu nilai tirittup poruḷuṇarccikku ūṟuceyvatē yākum'.
4. yāṅṅaṉam eṉpataṉai yāṅkaṉam eṉa etukait toṭaiyil pulavar tirittaṉar.
5. aṅkaṉam eṉpatu vaḻaṅkutaṟku eḷimaiyuṭaiyatu; aṅṅaṉam eṉpatu arumaiyuṭaiyatu. ākaliṉ, aṅkaṉam eṉpatu aṅṅaṉam eṉat tiriyātu.
6. 'aṅṅaṉam mutaliya coṟkaḷai a-ṅaṉam eṉṟiṅṅaṉam pirittup puṇarttuvatu tolkāppiyaṉār karuttaṉṟām. ākaliṉ, ṅakaram moḻimutaṟkaṇ varum eṉa avar vitittilar'.
7. 'meynnilai cuṭṭiṉ' eṉṉuñ cūttirattāl aṅṅaṉam eṉṉuñ collaiyē tolkāppiyaṉār kuṟittār.
8. āṅkaṉam iṅkaṉam eṉpatu piḻaiyuḷḷatōr muṭipu.
9. aṅṅaṉam eṉṉuñ colvaḻakkup paḻamaiyatē-eṉpaṉa.
ivaṟṟai muṟaiyē nōkkutum:
1. naṉṉūlār aṅṅaṉam iṅṅaṉam yāṅṅaṉam mutaliya coṟkaḷukku vitikūṟiyiruttalāṉ avai vaḻaṅkutal kūṭum eṉpatuvē pōtarutalaṉṟi, āṅkaṉam, īṅkaṉam, yāṅkaṉam mutaliya coṟkaḷai vaḻaṅkutal kūṭātu eṉpatu evvāṟu pōtarum? avar aṅṅaṉam mutaliyavaṟṟai amaittilarāyiṉ, avaṟṟai mēṟkoḷḷātu viṭuttal kūṭum. amait tuḷḷārākaliṉ avaṟṟai vaḻaṅkutal tavaṟaṉṟu. ituvēyaṉṟi, āṅkaṉam mutaliyavaṟṟai vaḻaṅkutal tavaṟeṉpatu peṟappaṭātu.
2. aṅṅaṉam mutaliya coṟkaḷai avvaṭivil vaḻaṅkutalai yāṉ yāṇṭum maṟuttilēṉ. yāṉ maṟuppatu viyappākavuḷatu, eṉṟu nāṭṭāravarkaḷ eḻutiyiruppatuvē viyappākavuḷḷatu. māṇavarporuṭṭu yāṉ eḻutiyirukkum pustakaṅkaḷil aṅṅaṉam iṅṅaṉam yāṅṅaṉam eṉpavaṟṟaiyē vaḻaṅkiyuḷḷēṉ.
	3. aṅṅaṉam, iṅṅaṉam mutaliyavai putiyavāypāṭā, āṅkaṉam....yāṅkaṉam mutaliyavai putiya vāypāṭā? caṅkac ceyyuṭkaḷilum tolkāppiyattilum vaḻaṅkappaṭṭuḷḷa āṅkaṉam.....yāṅkaṉam mutaliyavai putiyavāypāṭum piṟkālattu nūlkaḷilē vantuḷḷa aṅṅaṉam......yāṅṅaṉam mutaliyavai paḻaiyavāypāṭum ākumāyiṉ, piṉṉai yātutāṉ yātākātu?
caṅkacceyyuṭkaḷil āṅkaṉam.....yāṅkaṉam eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷiṉ poruḷai aṟinta aṟiñarkaṭku, yāṉ eḻutiyuḷḷa 'kapilaril vantirukkum āṅkaṉam....yāṅkaṉam eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷiṉ poruḷ viḷaṅkātō? atu poruḷuṇarccikku ūṟu ceyvatātal eppaṭi? aṅṅaṉam.......yāṅṅaṉam eṉṟu vaḻaṅkuvatē marapu eṉṟum avaṟṟai aṅkaṉam.....yāṅkaṉam eṉṟu vaḻaṅkuvatu marapaṉṟu eṉṟum kūṟi, 'marapunilai tiriyiṟ piṟitupiṟitākum' eṉṉun tolkāppiyac cūttirattaiyum, ataṟku, 'marapiṉai nilai tirittuc collupa eṉiṉ, ulakattuc collellām poruḷiḻantu piṟitu piṟitākum' eṉṉum pērāciriyarurai yaiyum nāṭṭāravarkaḷ ātāramākak kāṭṭiyirukkiṉṟārkaḷ. accūttiravuraiyiṉiṟutiyil, 'cevippulaṉāya ōcaikēṭṭuk kaṭpulaṉāyaporuḷ uṇarvatellām marapupaṟṟāka allatu maṟṟillai eṉṟavāṟu' eṉṟu pērāciriyarē eḻutiyiruppatil nāṭṭāravarkaḷiṉ nāṭṭam ceṉṟilatupōlum. ivvuraiyiṉaiyum mēlaiyiraṇṭu cūttiraṅkaḷiṉ poruḷaiyum nōkkuvōrkku īṇṭuk kūṟiya marapu eṉpatu iṉṉateṉat tāṉēpōtarum. 'irutiṇai aimpāṟ poruḷkaḷait tiṇaiyum pālum vaḻuvāmal taḻuvutal vēṇṭum' eṉṟu, 'nilantī nīrvaḷi' eṉṉuñ cūttirattiṟkūṟi, am marapu tirital ceyyuṭkaḷukku illai eṉpataṉai aṭutta cūttirattiṟ kūṟi, am marapu tiriyil poruḷ piṟitākum eṉpataṉai 'marapu nilaitiriyiṉ' eṉṉuñ cūttirattil āciriyar kūṟiyuḷḷār. uraiyāḷarum ivvāṟē poruḷ kūṟiyuḷḷaṉar. iṉi, nāṭṭāravarkaḷ kūṟuvatuvē iccūttirattiṉ poruḷ eṉṟu koḷḷiṉum, āṅkaṉam..........yāṅkaṉam eṉpaṉavē paḻaiya ceyyuṭkaḷil vantiruttaliṉ avaṟṟai āṅṅaṉam, aṅṅaṉam.........yāṅṅaṉam eṉṟu eḻutiṉōraṉṟē marapunilai tirittavarāvar? avai (aṅṅaṉam mutaliyavai) yaṉṟē poruḷuṇarccikku ūṟu viḷaittirukkum?
4. yāṅṅaṉam eṉpataṉaiyē etukait toṭaiyil yāṅkaṉam eṉṟu pulavar tirittaṉar eṉṟu nāṭṭāravarkaḷ eḻutiyiruppatu uṇmaiyākumā? paṇṭaik kālattil āciriyappākkaḷil etukai yiruntē tīravēṇṭum eṉṉum niyati uṇṭō? piṟkālattilum - ikkālattilum - āciriya vaṭikaḷai etukai yillāmal amaittuppāṭum vaḻakkāṟu uḷatē. tolkāppiyam centoṭaiyaik kūṟiyiruttalāṉaṉṟē paṇṭaippulavar tañceyyuḷil etukai mōṉaikaḷai niyatiyākak koḷḷātu viṭuttaṉar. piṟkālattup pulavarēpōṉṟu etukaikkākac coṟkaḷait tirikkum vaḻakkam paṇṭaip pulavarpāl uṇṭeṉpatu nāṭṭāravarkaḷāl yāṉaṟinta putucceytiyē.
'yāṅṅaṉam ottiyō vīṅkucelaṉ maṇṭilam' eṉat tiriyātu kūṟiṉum etukaiyātaṟku iḻukkiṉṟām. aṅṅaṉamākavum etukaimōṉaikaḷiṉ nirppantam illāta kālattil - illāta pāvil, pulavaroruvarallar- palarum 1-accollai vaḻaṅkiya pulavaraṉaivarumē-ṅakarattaik kakaramākat tirittē vaḻaṅkiṉār eṉpatu ettuṇai amaiviṉatu! ṅakarattōṭu piṟappoṟṟumaiyum oliyoṟṟumaiyum uṭaiyatu kakaram oṉṟē eṉṟu eḻutiyirukkiṟārkaḷ. kakaram ṅakarattōṭu oliyoṟṟumai uṭaiyatu eṉpatum eṉakkup putiyatē. 'ka'ṅakkaḷ muyaṟciyāṉum māttiraiyāṉum kuḷakkarai kuḷaṅkarai eṉṉum poruḷāṉum orupuṭaiyottu iṉamāyiṉa' eṉṟē eḻutiyuḷḷār mayilai nātar. nāṭṭāravarkaḷ oliyoṟṟumai yuṭaiyatu eṉṟatu eppaṭi eṉaviḷaṅkavillai. ṅakaramum araimāttiraiyatu, kakaramum araimarattiraiyatu. ākaliṉ avai oliyoṟṟumai yuṭaiyavākum eṉiṉ, cakaramum araimāttiraiyatē; ēṉ ellā meyyeḻuttukkaḷum araimāttiraiyavē. ākaliṉ ellā meyyeḻuttukkaḷum ṅakarattōṭu oliyoṟṟumai uṭaiyavēyākum. ṅakaramum kakaramum olivēṟṟumaiyuṭaiya vākalāṉaṉṟē vēṟu vēṟu iṉaṅkaḷāyiṉa. 'oḻinta melleḻuttaiyum iṭaiyeḻuttaiyum nōkkit tām valleṉṟicait talāṉum valleṉat talaivaḷiyāṟ piṟattalāṉum valleḻuttāyiṟṟu' eṉṟum, melleṉṟicait talāṉum melleṉṟu mūkkiṉvaḷiyāṟ piṟattaṉāṉum mel leḻuttāyiṟṟu' eṉṟum āciriyar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar eḻutiyirut taliṉ kakara ṅakaraṅkaḷ olivēṟṟumaiyuṭaiyavātal aṟiyap paṭumē.
iṟaiyaṉārkaḷaviyal mūṉṟāñ cūttiram 'āṅṅaṉam puṇarnta kiḻavōṉ' eṉṟē paṭikkap paṭṭu varukiṉṟatu eṉṟārkaḷ. accūttiram muḻuvatum īṇṭut tarukiṉṟēṉ.
'	āṅṅaṉam puṇarnta kiḻavōṉ ṟaṉvayiṟ
pāṅki ṉōriṟ kuṟitalaip peytalum
pāṅkilaṉ ṟamiyō ḷiṭantalaip paṭalumeṉ
ṟāṅka viraṇṭē talaippeyaṉ marapē'
eṉpatu accūttiram. cūttirattiṉ iraṇṭu mūṉṟu nāṉkām aṭikaḷai nōkkiṉ, mutalaṭi, 'āṅkaṉam puṇarnta kiḻavōṉ ṟaṉvayiṉ' eṉṟē amaintiruntirukkum eṉpatu eḷitiṟ pulaṉākum. uṇmaip pāṭam atuvēyātal ōlaiyēṭukaḷai nōkkiṉ aṟiyalākum. eṉṉeṉiṉ, paḻaiya patippukkaḷil,
'	āṅṅaṉam virippiṉ aḷaviṟan taṉavē
pāṅkuṟa vuṇarntōr paṉṉuṅ kālai'
eṉṟu patikkappaṭṭirunta tolkāppiyac cūttiram, ippoḻutu tiru. vē. turaicāmi aiyaravarkaḷaik koṇṭu paricōtittut tiru. kaṉakacapaip piḷḷaiyavarkaḷ veḷiyiṭṭa patippil,
'	āṅkaṉam virippiṉ aḷaviṟan taṉavē,
pāṅkuṟa vuṇarntōr paṉṉuṅ kālai'
eṉṟu patikkap paṭṭuḷatu. turaicāmiaiyaravarkaḷ pāṭattait tiruttiviṭṭārkaḷ eṉṟu nāṭṭāravarkaḷ kūṟiviṭuvārkaḷ pōlum.
akanāṉūṟṟil, 'yāṅṅaṉam' eṉṟu patippittuḷḷa mūṉṟu iṭaṅkaḷilum (ceyyuḷ 27, 90, 378) tiru es. vaiyāpurip piḷḷaiyavarkaḷiṭamirukkum paḻaiya ōlaiyēṭṭil 'yāṅkaṉam' eṉṟē irukkiṉṟatu. iḵtu ēṭeḻutiṉōr tavaṟu eṉṟu nāṭṭāravarkaḷ kūṟuvārkaḷāyiṉ eṉceyvatu!
accupputtakaṅkaḷil āṅṅaṉam yāṅṅaṉam eṉṟu patit tiruttaṟkuk kāraṇam avarkkuḷḷa naṉṉūṟpayiṟciyē yākum. mahāmahōpātyāya, ṭākṭar srīmat aiyaravarkaḷiṉ  caṅkanūṟpatippukkaḷil ivvāṟu vantilāmai nōkkat takkatu.
5. vaḻaṅkutaṟku eḷitākiya aṅkaṉam eṉṉuñ col, vaḻaṅkutaṟku aritākiya aṅṅaṉam eṉat tiriyātu eṉṟārkaḷ. itaṉai aṟiyiṉ, melloṟṟut taṉ iṉavalleḻuttōṭu kūṭi varuñcol vaḻaṅkutaṟku eḷiyatu eṉpatum melleḻuttu iraṭṭittu varum col vaḻaṅkutaṟku ariyatu eṉpatum avarkaḷ karuttātal naṉkupulaṉākum. ākavē, vaḻaṅkutaṟku eḷitākiya 
oṉṟu eṉpatu vaḻaṅkutaṟ karitākiya oṉṉu eṉṟum,
aṉṟu	    "		"	       aṉṉu eṉṟum,
kaṉṟu	    "		"	       kaṉṉu eṉṟum,
eṉṟu	    "		"	       eṉṉu eṉṟum,
vaḻaṅkutal eṉṉai? aṉṟu mutaliyavai malaiyāḷattil aṉṉu mutaliyavāyē vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. aṉṟiyum, maṅkalam kañci cantaṉam eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷ ammoḻiyil muṟaiyē maṅṅalam kaññi cannaṉam eṉṟu iruvaḻakkilum vaḻaṅku kiṉṟaṉa. oṉṉu aṉṉu kaṉṉu eṉṉu eṉpavaiyē vaḻaṅkutaṟ kariyavākaliṉ oṉṟu aṉṟu kaṉṟu eṉṟu eṉat tirintaṉa eṉpatum, avvāṟē, maṅṅalam kaññi cannaṉam eṉpavaiyē vaḻaṅkutaṟku eḷiyavākiya maṅkalam kañci cantaṉam eṉṟu tirintaṉa eṉpatum nāṭṭāravarkaḷ karuttātal vēṇṭum. vaḻaṅkutaṟku aritākiya aṅṅaṉam eṉpatuvē vaḻaṅkutaṟku eḷitākiya aṅkaṉam eṉat tirintatu eṉṟavarkaṭku, vaḻaṅkutaṟku ariyavākiya maṅṅalam cannaṉam eṉpavaiyē vaḻaṅkutaṟku eḷiyavākiya maṅkalam cantaṉam eṉṟāyiṉa eṉpataṉṟē amaitiyākum. iṉi, māṅṅā tēṅṅā eṉṟu malaiyāḷattil vaḻaṅkutal pōṉṟu tamiḻilum mikappaḻaiya kālattil vaḻaṅkiṉa eṉṟum avai vaḻaṅkutaṟku ariyavākaliṉ, māṅkāy tēṅkāy eṉṟu tiriyalāyiṉa eṉṟum nāṭṭāravarkaḷ kūṟuvārkaḷ. eṉṉaiyeṉiṉ, ṅṅaoli vaḻaṅkutaṟku ariyatum ṅka oli vaḻaṅkutaṟku eḷiyatum ākum eṉpatu avarkaḷ koḷkaiyākaliṉ eṉka.
6. aṅṅaṉam mutaliya coṟkaḷai a+ṅaṉam eṉṟiṅṅaṉam pirittup puṇarppatu tolkāppiyarkkuk karuttaṉṟākaliṉ, avar, ṅakaram moḻimutal varum eṉṟu kūṟiṟṟilar eṉṟārkaḷ. āyiṉ, aṅṅaṉam eṉpataṟkut tolkāppiyaṉār koṇṭa poruḷ itu eṉṟu nāṭṭāravarkaḷ kūṟavillai. attaṉmai, appaṭi eṉpatu avar koṇṭa poruḷ eṉpārkaḷāyiṉ, āṇṭu, akaram cuṭṭupporuḷ tarutaliṉ ṅakaram moḻimutal vantatākum. ākavē, aññālam annāḷ ammaṇi eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷaip pirittu, 
'	cuṭṭiṉ muṉṉar ña na ma t tōṉṟiṉ
oṭṭiya oṟṟiṭai mikutal vēṇṭum'
eṉṟu vitikūṟiya āciriyar,
'cuṭṭiṉ muṉṉar ṅa ña na ma t tōṉṟiṉ'
eṉṟu vitikūṟi aṅṅaṉam eṉpataṉaiyum amaittiruppārallavō? accam aññai aṇṇaṉ eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷiluḷḷa akarattaip pōlavē aṅṅaṉam eṉpatilum akarañ colluṟuppāy amaintuḷatu eṉṟālaṉṟi, aṅṅaṉam eṉpatil ṅakaram moḻimutal vantatākak karutappaṭāmai amaiyātu. avvāṟu koḷḷiṉ, aṅṅaṉam eṉpataṉ poruḷ yātu?
viṉaittokai paṇputtokaikaḷaip pirittup puṇark kalākātu eṉṉuṅ karuttāl ataṟkup puṇarcciviti kūṟātatu pōṉṟu aṅṅaṉam eṉpataṟkum puṇarcciviti kūṟiṟṟilar eṉṟārkaḷ. kolyāṉai aṭukaḷiṟu ōṭukāl eṉṉum viṉait tokaikaḷil uḷḷa yāṉai kaḷiṟu kāl eṉpaṉavum, karuṅ kutirai karumpārppāṉ centāmarai veṇkuṭai eṉṉum paṇputtokaikaḷil kutirai pārppāṉ tāmarai kuṭai eṉpaṉavum taṉittum vaḻaṅkutalpōṉṟu, aṅṅaṉam eṉpatilum ṅaṉam eṉpatu taṉittu vaḻaṅkutal vēṇṭumē. taṉittu vaḻaṅkiṉ, ṅakaram moḻimutalil varumeṉṟu kūṟavēṇṭumē. viṉaittokai paṇputtokaikaṭku āciriyar puṇarcciviti kūṟātataṉ kāraṇam- 'kol eṉat toḻiṉmai uṇara niṉṟatu kolyāṉai eṉat tokaiyāyavaḻi koṉṟa eṉak kālaṅ kāṭṭi niṉṟamaiyiṉ puṇarkkappaṭātāyiṟṟu.' 'karum eṉap paṇpuṇara niṉṟatu karuñcāṉṟāṉ eṉat tokaiyāyavaḻi, karum eṉpatu kariyāṉ eṉap pālkāṭṭi niṟṟaliṟ puṇarkkappaṭātāyiṟṟu' eṉṟu uraiyāciriyarum, 'āṭaraṅku.........eṉa nilam mutalākiya peyareccan tokka viṉaittokaikaḷai virikkuṅkāl āṭiṉavaraṅku eṉac ceyta eṉṉum peyareccattu īṟuvirinta akaraīṟu iṟappuṇarttiyum, āṭāniṉṟavaraṅku, āṭumaraṅku eṉac ceyyum eṉṉum peyareccattu īṟu virinta ummīṟu nikaḻvum etirvum uṇarttiyum avaṟṟāṉāya puṭai peyarcciyait tōṟṟuvittu iraṇṭu peyareccamum oru coṟkaṇ oruṅku tokkuniṟṟaliṉ ataṉai oru peyareccattiṉkaṇ aṭakkip puṇarkkalākāmaiyiṉ, puṇark kalākātu eṉṟār.' 'karumpārppāṉ karumpārppaṉi karum pārppār karuṅkutirai karuṅkutiraikaḷ eṉa varum ivaṟṟuṭ kariyaṉākiya pārppāṉ  kariyaḷākiya pārppaṉi, kariyarākiya pārppār kariyatākiya kutirai kariyaṉavākiya kutiraikaḷ eṉa aimpāliṉaiyum uṇarttum paṇpukoḷ peyar tokkavāṟu kāṇka. ivaṟṟuṭ karumai eṉṉum paṇpuppeyar tokkatēl karumaiyākiya pārppāṉ eṉa virittal vēṇṭum. aṅṅaṉam viriyāmaiyiṟ paṇpukoḷ peyar tokkateṉṟu uṇarka....... iṅṅaṉam aimpālun tokuttaṟku uriya mutaṉilaiyātaliṟ puṇarttalākāmai kūṟiṉār' eṉṟu nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum kūṟiyiruppavaṟṟāṉ naṉkaṟiyappaṭum. iṉṉatō riṭarppāṭu aṅṅaṉam eṉṉuñ colliluḷḷa akarac cuṭṭiṉkaṇ iṉmaiyiṉ, ataṟku vitikūṟutal eḷitiṉiyalumaṉṟē.' ivaṟṟāl, viṉaittokai paṇputtokaikaḷukku vitikūṟa iyalāmal viṭuttatupōla aṅṅaṉam eṉpataṉaiyum vitikūṟātu viṭuttār eṉṟu nāṭṭāravarkaḷ kūṟiyatu ciṟitum poruntāmai naṉkaṟiyappaṭum.
7. 'meynilai cuṭṭiṉ' eṉṉuñ cūttirattāl, aṅṅaṉam iṅṅaṉam mutaliya coṟkaḷait tolkāppiyaṉār amait tuḷḷār eṉṟārkaḷ. 'ra ḻa oḻinta maṟṟaiya meyyeḻuttukkaḷ tammoṭu tām mayaṅkum' eṉpatuvē cūttirattiṉ poruḷ. itaṉāl, ṅakarattoṭu ṅakaram mayaṅkutaṟku aṅṅaṉam iṅṅaṉam eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷaiyē āciriyar karutiṉār eṉṟu tuṇital amaiyātu. āyiṉ, uraiyāciriyar, eṅṅaṉam eṉṉuñ collaiyē utāraṇamākak kāṭṭiṉārāl eṉiṉ, ataṉai nōkkutum;
'	ya ra ḻa eṉṉum puḷḷi muṉṉar
mutalā keḻuttu ṅakaramoṭu tōṉṟum'
eṉṉuñ cūttirattiṉ uraiyil, ya ra ḻakkaḷukku muṉ ṅakaram varutaṟku utāraṇamāka avar (uraiyāciriyar,)
vēyṅaṉam
vērṅaṉam
vīḻṅaṉam
eṉpavaṟṟaik kāṭṭiyuḷḷār. ivvutāraṇaṅkaḷil, vēy, vēr, vīḻ eṉpavai nilaimoḻikaḷākaliṉ, ṅaṉam eṉpatu varumoḻi eṉa naṉkaṟiyappaṭum. ākavē, ṅakaram moḻimutal vantatākumaṉṟē? ivvutāraṇamē āciriyar karutiṉārāyiṉ, 'mutalā keḻuttu ṅakaramoṭu tōṉṟum' eṉṟu ṅakarattaip pirittuk kūṟutaliṉṟi, 'mutalākeḻuttut tōṉṟum eṉpa' eṉpatu pōlaṉṟē cūttirañ ceytiruppār? ṅakaram moḻi
mutal vārātataṉāṉaṉṟē ataṉai mutalākeḻuttōṭu cērkkātu pirittuk kūṟiṉār? iccūttirattiṟku uraiyāciriyar kāṭṭiya 'vēyṅaṉam' mutaliyavai utāraṇamākumō? itupōṉṟē, 'meynilai cuṭṭiṉ' eṉṉuñ cūttirattiṉ uraiyil avar kāṭṭiya 'eṅṅaṉam' eṉṉum utāraṇamum amaivuṭaiyataṉṟu. aṅṅaṉam mutaliya coṟkaḷ tolkāppiyaṉār kālattil vaḻaṅkiyirukkumēl tolkāppiyattilum caṅkanūlkaḷilum āṅṅaṉam īṅṅaṉam yāṅṅaṉam eṉpaṉavaṉṟē kāṇappaṭal vēṇṭum. avvāṟiṉṟi, āṅkaṉam īṅkaṉam yāṅkaṉam eṉpaṉavē kāṇappaṭutaṟku niyāyam yātu? āyiṉ, ṅakarattiṉmuṉ ṅakām varutaṟku utāraṇam yātō eṉiṉ, 'piṭiyūṭṭip piṉṉuṇṇuṅṅ kaḷiṟeṉavu muraittaṉarē' eṉṉum aṭiyil ṅakaram iraṇṭu vantiruttalpōlvatu utāraṇamākum. aṅṅaṉam eṉpatu pōlat taṉimey muṉṉar uyirmey vantatuvē amaiyum; iḵtu amaiyātu eṉiṉ, taṉimeyyōṭu taṉimeymayaṅku
talai iccūttirattāṟ koḷḷākkāl 'kaṇṇ karuviḷai,' 'poṉṉ poṟi cuṇaṅku' eṉpaṉapōla varupavaṟṟilellām meykaḷ mayaṅki niṟṟaṟku ilakkaṇamiṉṟākum. aṉṟiyum, taṉimeyyōṭu uyirmey mayaṅkiya coṟkaḷai utāraṇaṅ
kāṭṭiṉum āṇṭu meyyoṭu meymayaṅkiya tuṇaiyē utāraṇamākak koḷḷappaṭutal karutattakum. meymayakkam eṉpatu meyyoṭu meymayaṅkutalēyākum. iṉi, ṅakara
meyyōṭu ṅakarauyirmey mayaṅkiya col iruntiruppiṉ aḵtu utāraṇamātaṟkut taṭṭillai.
8. āṅkaṉam..........yāṅkaṉam eṉpavaṟṟaip piḻaiyuḷḷatōr muṭipu eṉṟārkaḷ. āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉārum caṅkappulavar palarum vaḻaṅkiyirukkuñ collē piḻaiyuḷḷatāyiṉ, piḻai yillāta colyātō, aṟiyēṉ.
9. aṅṅaṉam mutaliya coṟkaḷiṉ vaḻakkup paḻamaiyatē eṉṟārkaḷ. paḻamaiyatāyiṉ caṅkac ceyyuṭkaḷil vantirukkumē āṅkaṉam īṅkaṉam yāṅkaṉam eṉṉum vaṭivaccoṟkaḷē caṅkac ceyyuṭkaḷil kāṇkiṉṟaṉa. ōriṭattilātal āṅṅaṉam aṅṅaṉam eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷ kāṇkiṉṟila. caṅkac ceyyuṭkaḷil āṭciyuṭaiya coṟkaḷ putiya vāypāṭṭuc coṟkaḷātalum, avaṟṟil vantilāta coṟkaḷ paḻamaiyavātalum kālattiṉ kōlamē.
mukittal eṉṉuñ col paṇṭaiyilakkaṇa ilakkiyaṅkaḷil vantilātataṉāl ataṉai vaḻaṅkiyatu tavaṟu eṉṟārkaḷ. āṅkaṉam..........yāṅkaṉam eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷ paṇṭaiyilakkaṇa ilakkiyaṅkaḷil vantuḷḷaṉavākaliṉ avaṟṟai vaḻaṅkiyatum tavaṟāyiṟṟu, nāṭṭāravarkaḷ kūṟumanniyāyam niyāyamē!
III. kāṇkiṉṟatu eṉṉuñ collaik kuṟittu nāṭṭāravarkaḷ - eḻutiyiruppataṉ curukkam:-
1. kāṇṭalviṉai ceyappaṭuporuḷ kuṉṟātatākaliṉ, kāṇ eṉpataṉ aṭiyiṟ piṟakkuñ coṟkaḷaik kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu, kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟiṅṅaṉam vaḻaṅkutalēmuṟai.
2. vēṟṟumoḻip payiṟci oṉṟē koṇṭu tamiḻiṉ iyalpiṉai viḷakkaluṟuvār cilar............tamiḻiṉkaṇ ceyap pāṭṭuviṉai iṉṟu eṉavum kūṟāniṟpar.
3. kāṇkiṉṟatu eṉpataṉaiyē avar ('kapilar' āciriyar) niṉaivukūrntu vaḻaṅkiyuḷḷār; kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu eṉpatu payiṟcivayattāl avaraṟiyāmalē pōntatu.
4. itu (kāṇkiṉṟatu eṉpatu pōṉṟu ceyviṉaiyāka eḻutuvatu), iḻi vaḻakku.
5. ettuṇaiyar ivviḻivaḻakkiṉai viḻumiya vaḻakkākak koṇṭaṉarō eṉa uṉṉa accam uṇṭākiṉṟatu.
ivaṟṟai muṟaiyē ārāytum :-
1. kāṇ eṉpataṉ aṭiyiṟ piṟakkum viṉaic coṟkaḷaic ceyappāṭṭuviṉaiyākavē vaḻaṅkutal vēṇṭum eṉpataṟku,
‘	viṉaiyē ceyvatu ceyappaṭu poruḷē
......... ....... .....	........ ........	..
āyeṭ ṭeṉpa toḻiṉmuta ṉilaiyē’
eṉṉum cūttirattai vitiyākak kuṟittārkaḷ. oru toḻiṟku mutaṉilai (kāraṇam) eṭṭu eṉpatum, avai ivaiyeṉpatum iccūttirattāṟ pōtarutalaṉṟic ceyappaṭuporuḷ uḷḷa viṉaiyaic ceyappāṭṭuviṉai vaṭivil vaḻaṅkutal vēṇṭu meṉpatu pōtarutal illai. ceyappaṭuporuḷuḷḷa viṉaikku utāraṇaṅkāṭṭiya uraiyāḷarellārum vaṉaintāṉ eṉṟu ceyviṉaiyākak kāṭṭiṉarēyaṉṟi, vaṉaiyappaṭṭatu eṉṟu ceyappāṭṭuviṉaiyākak kāṭṭiṉārillai. ceyappaṭuporuḷ kuṉṟāviṉaiyaic ceyappāṭṭu viṉaiyāka vaḻaṅkutalvēṇṭum eṉpatu iccūttirattil evvāṟu pulaṉākiṉṟatō, aṟikilēṉ. nāṭṭāravarkaḷ aṟivippārkaḷāyiṉ avarkaḷpāl eṉṟum naṉṟipārāṭṭuṅ kaṭappāṭuṭaiyēṉāvēṉ.
‘viṉaiyē' eṉṉum iccūttirattil vantuḷḷa 'ceyappaṭu poruḷ' eṉṉuñ collāl aṟiyappaṭum eṉiṉ, atu, karuttāviṉ1 ceyalai aṭainta poruḷ eṉṟu karumattiṉai viḷakkiyatitaṉai. accollāl, tamiḻil ceyappāṭṭu viṉai vaḻaṅkutal uṇṭu eṉpatu aṟiyappaṭum. attuṇaiyēyaṉṟi, ceyappaṭuporuḷ kuṉṟāta viṉaiyaiyellām ceyappāṭṭu viṉaivaṭivil vaḻaṅkutal vēṇṭum eṉpatu peṟappaṭātē. ceyappaṭuporuḷaiyē naṉṉūlār ceyporuḷ eṉṟu vaḻaṅkiyuḷḷār 'ceyporuḷāṟun taruvatu viṉaiyē' eṉṟatu kāṇka. nāṭṭāravarkaḷum, tamiḻiṟ ceyappāṭṭu viṉai vaḻakku uṇṭu eṉpataik kūṟumiṭattē, 'ceyappaṭu poruḷ eṉṉum peyariliruntē ceyappāṭṭuviṉai yuṇmai peṟap paṭuvataṉaiyum' eṉṟu eḻutiyiruttaliṉ, accollāl, ceyappāṭṭu viṉai vaḻakku uṇṭu eṉpatumaṭṭumē aṟiyappaṭum.
2. vēṟṟumoḻip payiṟci oṉṟē koṇṭu, tamiḻil ceyappāṭṭuviṉai illai eṉṟār ivar eṉayāṉ aṟikilēṉ. āyiṉ, ceyappāṭṭu viṉaivaḻakkut tirāviṭa moḻikku iyalpāyataṉṟu; vaṭamoḻiyiṉiṉṟu vantatu eṉpārai yāṉ. aṟivēṉ.
‘	karmaṇiprayōkam pāṣāsailikkuc cērntataṉṟu’2
‘	pāṣaikkuc contamākak karmaṇiprayōkam illai’3
ippoḻutu vaḻaṅkivaruvatu vaṭamoḻiyiṉ cariyāṉa moḻi peyarppiṟku vēṇṭi iṭaikkālattil kiruttirimamāka ēṟpaṭuttiyatoru sampratāyamākum. itaṟku lakṣyam-mutalāvatu, vaṭamoḻi aṟiyātavar karmaṇiprayōkattai orukālum ātarittal illai. pēccumoḻiyil ceyviṉai vaḻakkē kēṭkumāṟuḷatu. paḻaiya nūlkaḷilmaṭṭumaṉṟu, eḻuttaccaṉ ḻutaliyōruṭaiya nūlkaḷilum vaṭamoḻit toṭarpu illātaviṭattuk karmaṇi prayōkam illai eṉpatu eṉ aṉupavam. "rakuvamsam kāḷitācaṉāl uṇṭākkappaṭṭatu" eṉpataṟku īṭāka, rakuvamsam kāḷitāsaṉ uṇṭākkiyatu" eṉṟāl, ciṟitum amaiyāmai yillai eṉpataṉṟu. immuṟaimaiyē moḻikku iyalpāyatu eṉṟum tōṉṟukiṉṟatu..............'
‘karmaṇi prayōkattaic cutta tirāviṭaṅkaḷāya viṉaic coṟkaḷil atikam oḻittal vēṇṭum.'1
īsvaraṉ ceyyuṉṉa tellām (ceykiṉṟatellām) sahikkayē (poṟuttuk koḷḷutalē) uḷḷu (uḷatu) eṉṟu kūṟāmal, īsvaraṉāl ceyyappeṭuṉṉatellām sahikkap peṭukaiyē uḷḷu eṉṟu pirayōkittāl, atu malaiyāḷam aṉṟu eṉṟē kūṟutal nērum'
eṉṟu, kēraḷapāṇiṇi, mahāmahimai, srīrājarājavarma kōyiṟṟampirāṉavarkaḷ kūṟiyirukkiṉṟārkaḷ.
‘kaṉṉaṭattil karmaṇi prayōkam visēṣarūṭamāka villai. vīṭukaṭṭappaṭṭatu eṉpataṟkup patil vīṭukaṭṭiṟṟu (maṉe kaṭṭitu) eṉṉum prayōkam ērāḷamāyirukkiṉṟatu'.2
‘maṇimayatālayaṅkaḷu cametu vaikāvutata aḷateyali' eṉṉum aṭiyil, camaikkappaṭṭaṉa eṉṉum poruḷil camaintaṉa (cametuvu) eṉṟu vantiruttal kāṇka.3
ikkāṭṭiyavaṟṟāl, ceyappāṭṭuviṉai vaḻakkut tirāviṭa moḻikaḷukku iyalpāyataṉṟu eṉpatum, ceyappāṭṭu viṉaiyai vaḻaṅka vēṇṭiyaviṭattuc ceyviṉaiyai vaḻaṅkum vaḻakkē porumpālum uḷatu eṉpatum pōtarum. pukaḻuṭam pōṭu viḷaṅkuṅ kā. namaccivāya mutaliyāravarkaḷ karuttum ituvē.
3. kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu eṉṟu ceyappāṭṭu viṉaiyilyāṉ eḻutiyiruppatu payiṟcimikutiyāl eṉṉai aṟiyāmalē nērntatu eṉṟu nāṭṭāravarkaḷ kūṟiyirukkiṉṟārkaḷ. avvāṟāyiṉ, aṅṅaṉam iṅṅaṉam yāṅṅaṉam eṉpaṉavum eṉṉai yaṟiyāmaṟ cilaviṭaṅkaḷil pōntiruttal vēṇṭumē. eṉatu ‘kapila'ril ōriṭattātal avvāṟu vantilatē. aṅṅaṉam mutaliyavai eṉakkup payiṟciyillātaṉa eṉpārkaḷ pōlum. āyiṉ, kalācālai māṇavarkkāka yāṉ eḻutiyirukkum pustakaṅkaḷil ōriṭattilātal aṅkaṉam............yāṅkaṉam eṉṉuñcol vantila; aṅṅaṉam mutaliya coṟkaḷē vantuḷḷaṉa. ivaṟṟāl, aṟiyāmal eḻutum vaḻakkam eṉpāl illai eṉpatai nāṭṭāravarkaḷ aṟintu koḷvārkaḷāka.
4. kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu eṉṟu ceyappāṭṭuviṉaiyāy vaḻaṅkāmal, kāṇkiṉṟatu eṉṟu ceyviṉaivaṭivil vaḻaṅkiyatu iḻivaḻakku eṉṟārkaḷ.
‘muṉaivaṉ kaṇṭatu mutaṉūlākum' eṉṟu vantuḷatu. ‘muṉaivaṉ kaṇṭatu' eṉpataṟku, 'muṉṉōṉāṟ ceyyap paṭṭatu' eṉṟu pērāciriyar eḻutiyirukkiṉṟār.
‘nōkkuva vellām avaiyē pōṟal' eṉṉumiṭattum, nōkkappaṭuva eṉṟu ceyappāṭṭuviṉaiyil vantilatu. ‘nōkkuva' eṉpataṟku, ‘taṉṉāṟ kāṇappaṭṭaṉa' eṉṟu uraiyāciriyar eḻutiyirukkiṉṟār. (nōkkal eṉpatu ceyappaṭuporuḷ kuṉṟāviṉai yākaliṉ, ataṉai, ceyappāṭṭuviṉaiyil vaḻaṅkutal vēṇṭum eṉpatu nāṭṭāravarkaḷ karuttu. pakkam 48 pārkka.)
‘kaṇṭatu moḻimō' eṉṟu kuṟuntokaiyil vantuḷatu
‘kāṇkiṉṟa nilamellām yāṉē eṉṉum
kāṇkiṉṟa vicumpellām yāṉē eṉṉum
kāṇkiṉṟa ventīyelām yāṉē eṉṉum
kāṇkiṉṟa vikkāṟṟellām yāṉē eṉṉum
kāṇkiṉṟa kaṭamellām yāṉē eṉṉum' (tiruvāy 5.6.3.)
eṉṟu oru ceyyuḷil palamuṟai vantuḷatu. ivaiyellām iḻivaḻakkēyō? ivai ceyyuḷākaliṉ amaiyum; urai naṭaiyil amaiyātu eṉpārkaḷō? avvāṟu ceyyuḷukkum uraikkum taṉittaṉiyē ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟiyiruttalai yāṉ aṟintilēṉ. uraiyaiyuñ ceyyuḷ eṉṉum vaḻakku uḷataṉṟē? niṟka. ikkālattut tamiḻp pulavarkaḷuḷ urainaṭai yeḻututaliṟ ciṟantavar kā. namaccivāyamutaliyā ravarkaḷeṉpatu tamiḻnāṭu aṟinta ceytiyē. avarkaḷ, tam urainūlkaḷil, kāṇkiṉṟatu eṉpataṉaiyē peritum vaḻaṅkiyirukkiṟārkaḷ.
‘aṅkē palappala kaṇavāykaḷum koṭumuṭikaḷum kāṇkiṉṟaṉa.' 1
‘iṭaiyiṭaiyē kaṇavāykaḷum nīrōṭaikaḷum kāṇkiṉṟaṉa.'1
‘kāṭṭuvilaṅkukaḷuṭ ciṟanta yāṉai, puli, karaṭi mutaliya vilaṅkukaḷ aṭivāraṅkaḷil maṭṭum kāṇkiṉṟaṉa.' 2  
‘orukālattil atu ciṟanta nakaramāyiruntatu eṉpataṟku aṟikuṟikaḷ āṅkāṅkē ippōtuṅ kāṇkiṉṟaṉa' eṉpaṉa kāṇka.3 ivai payiṉṟu vantuḷḷaṉa. kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu eṉpatuvum cilaviṭaṅkaḷil vantuḷatu. yāṉ eḻutiya 'kapilar' eṉṉum nūl muḻuvatum mutaliyāravarkaḷ kaṭaik kaṇittut tiruttañ ceytatē. itaṉai yāṉ muṉṉuraiyil kuṟittirukkiṉṟēṉ. tiru. celvakēcavarāya mutaliyāravarkaḷum ‘tiruvaḷḷuvar' eṉṉum nūlil ivvāṟu ceyviṉaiyāka vaḻaṅkiyuḷḷārkaḷ.
5. yāṉ eḻutiya kapilar eṉṉum nūl, uyarnta tāḷiṟpariya uruviṉatākap polivuṟa acciṭappaṭṭiruttaliṉ atiṟ kāṇum ivvaḻivaḻakkiṉai viḻumiya vaḻakkeṉṟu koṇṭu palar keṭṭoḻivarē eṉṟu nāṭṭāravarkaḷ añcukiṉṟārkaḷ. itu mēṉmakkaḷiyalpē. āyiṉ, tolkāppiyam kuṟuntokai tiruvāymoḻi mutaliyavaṟṟaik kaṟpōr cilar eṉṟu koṇṭu avaraikkuṟittu añcātiruppiṉum, tiru kā. namaccivāya mutaliyāravarkaḷiṉ urainūlkaḷaip payilum māṇavar pallāyiravarākaliṉ, iḷamaiyiṟ kaṟṟatu cilaiyileḻuttup pōl nilaipeṟumākaliṉ avaraṉaivaruṅ keṭṭoḻivarē eṉṟu nāṭṭāravarkaḷ karutuvārkaḷāyiṉ, avarkaḷuḷḷam evvāṟu añcum! antō!!
IV.  naṟkīrar eṉṉuñ collaikkuṟittu nāṭṭāravarkaḷ eḻutiyiruppataṉ karuttu.
1. 	nakkīrar eṉpatu paṇṭu toṭṭu nilaipeṟṟuvaruvatu
2. 	naṟkīrar eṉpatu aḻivaḻakku
3. nakkīrar eṉpatai naṟkīrar eṉṟu māṟṟiṉatu pōla nakkaṇṇaiyār nacceḷḷaiyār mutaliya peyarkaḷaiyum māṟṟi vaḻaṅkuvarēl, atu piṟitoru moḻiyātalaṉṟit tamiḻākātu.
1. nakkīraṉār nappālattaṉār mutaliya peyarkaḷil nal eṉpatuvē aṭaimoḻi eṉṟum, naṟkīraṉār eṉpatuvē nakkīraṉār eṉṟu maruviṟṟu eṉṟum yāṉ eḻutiyiruppatu taṅkaṭkum uṭaṉpāṭē eṉṟu nāṭṭāravarkaḷē eḻutiyiruk kiṟārkaḷ. itaṉāl naṟkīraṉār eṉpatuvē tiripillātatu-iyalpāyatu eṉpatu avarkaṭkum uṭaṉpāṭākum. aṅkaṉa mākavum, nakkīrar eṉpatu paṇṭutoṭṭu nilaipeṟṟu varuvatu eṉṟatu viyappiṉait tarukiṉṟatu. ivvāṟāyiṉ, mañcaṉ, añci(aintu), pōcci, ācci eṉpavaiyē paṇṭutoṭṭu nilaipeṟṟuvarupavai eṉṟum maintaṉ, aintu, pōyiṟṟu pōyttu, āyiṟṟu āyttu eṉpavai piṟkālattil (eṉṉaip pōṉṟavarkaḷāl) vaḻaṅkappaṭṭavai eṉṟum kūṟutal vēṇṭumē. tirintacol paṇṭaiyatākumā, tiriyāta col paṇṭaiya tākumā? iṉi, paṇṭu eṉṟatu kaṭaic caṅkakālattaik kuṟippatu eṉiṉ, nakkīrar eṉṉuñ col caṅkacceyyuṭkaḷil vantiruppa tākat teriyavillai. paṇṭita i. vai. aṉantarāmaiyaravarkaḷ, cilakālattiṟku muṉvarai naṟkīrar eṉpatuvē vaḻaṅkivantatāka eḻutiyirukkiṟārkaḷ.1 (avarkaḷ nakkīraṉ eṉpatuvē iyalpāya peyar eṉṟu koṇṭavarkaḷ; nāṭṭāravarkaḷ akkoḷkaiyiṉa rallar tamiḻp. 13pakkam. pārkka.) hālāsyamāhātmyattil naṟkīraṉ eṉṟu vaḻaṅkiyiruttaliṉ, appurāṇattai iyaṟṟiṉōr kālattil naṟkīrar eṉṟē vaḻaṅkivantatu eṉpatu teḷivākum. akkālattil nakkīrar eṉṟu (vaṭamoḻiyil) poruḷillāta toru collāka ataṉait tirittu vaḻaṅkutaṟku niyāya millai. cīvakacintāmaṇi 482-āñ ceyyuḷiṉ uraiyil nacciṉārkkiṉiyar eḻutiyirukkum vicēṭavuraiyaip paṭitta pulavarkaḷāṉē naṟkīrar eṉṉum vaḻakku oḻintirukkum. nacciṉārkkiṉiyar kālattil nakkīrar eṉṟu vaḻakku vaḻaṅkiyatu eṉpatil aiyamillai. avvaḷaviṟāṉē atu paṇṭutoṭṭu nilaipeṟṟu varuvatu eṉal amaivuṟātu. naṟcēntaṉār,2 naṟṟāmaṉār3 eṉṉum peyar akanāṉūṟṟilum naṟṟiṇaiyilum iṉṉum kāṇkiṉṟaṉa. naṟṟamaṉār4 eṉṉum peyaroṉṟu naṟṟiṇaiyiṟ kāṇkiṉṟatu. mēṟkūṟiyavaṟṟāl, nakkīraṉār eṉṉun toṭakkattaṉavē paṇṭutoṭṭu vaḻaṅkuvaṉa eṉpatu uṇmaiyiṉṟāy, varalāṟṟōṭu muraṇutal kāṇka.
2. naṟkīrar eṉṉuñcol aḻivaḻakku eṉṟārkaḷ. iyalpāya peyarai uṇmaivaṭivil vaḻaṅkutal aḻivaḻakkāyiṉ, ciṟanta vaḻakku yātō? naṟkīrar eṉpatu aḻivaḻakkāyiṉ, naṟcēntaṉār naṟṟāmaṉār eṉpavai aḻivaḻakkākāvō?
3. naṟkīrar eṉṟu eḻutiyatupōṉṟu naṟkaṇṇaiyār naṟceḷḷaiyār eṉṟum, eḻutuvēṉāyiṉ avai tamiḻmoḻic coṟkaḷākāmal, teluṅku malaiyāḷam pōlap piṟitumoḻi yākum eṉṟārkaḷ. perumpulavar piṉṉattūr a. nārāyaṇacāmi aiyaravarkaḷum macāvittuvāṉ rā. irākavaiyaṅkāravarkaḷum naṟcēntaṉār naṟṟāmaṉār eṉṉum peyarkaḷai muṉṉarē eḻutiyiruttaliṉ, avarkaḷē teluṅku malaiyāḷam pōlap piṟitorumoḻiyaip paṭaittuviṭṭārkaḷākaliṉ, putiya torumoḻiyaip paṭaikkum perumaiyum aṭiyēṉukkuk kiṭaikkāmaṟ pōyiṟṟu. nāṭṭāravarkaḷ eṉmāṭṭuḷḷa aṉpiṉ mikutiyiṉāl apperumaiyai eṉakkē koṭukka uṭaṉpaṭṭālum naṟṟiṇaiyaiyum akanāṉūṟṟaiyum kaṇṭa tamiḻulakam ataṟku uṭaṉpaṭātē.
'teṟṟeṉa' eṉpatu teḷ eṉṉum mutaṉilaiyaṭiyilum, nākku eṉpatu nāl eṉṉum pakutiyaṭiyilum piṟanta coṟkaḷ eṉpatu yāṉaṟintatu. itaṉait tamiḻp poḻililum eḻutiyuḷḷēṉ. atukoṇṭu, ‘teṭṭeṉa' eṉṟum, 'nāṟku' eṉṟum eḻututal vēṇṭum eṉpatu eṉkaruttaṉṟu. yāṉ avvāṟu eḻutiṟṟumilēṉ. aṟiñar vaḻaṅkiya coṟkaḷai - tiripillātavaṟṟai - avvāṟē vaḻaṅkutal nalaṉ eṉpatu eṉkaruttu. kaṉṉaṭamoḻi - paḻaṅkaṉṉaṭam eṉṟum putukkaṉṉaṭam eṉṟum iruperumpiriviṉatāy vaḻaṅkutal pōlavum, malaiyāḷamoḻi - paḻaiya malaiyāḷam arvācīṉa malaiyāḷam eṉṟum vaḻaṅkutalpōlavum, tamiḻmoḻiyum paṇṭaittamiḻ piṟkālattamiḻ eṉṟum pirivuṟṟirukkumēl āṅkaṉam.......yāṅkaṉam eṉṟum naṟkīrar eṉṟum eḻututal amaitiyākātu. avvāṟu piriviṉṟiyiruttalāṉē, tamiḻp pulavarkaḷ nīyir yāṉ eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷaiyum nummai niṉṉai eṉṉun toṭakkattavaṟṟaiyum āṭūu makaṭūu eṉṉuñ coṟ kaḷaiyum ivaipōṉṟa piṟavaṟṟaiyum vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟaṉar. pukaḻuṭampōṭu viḷaṅkuñ cūriyanārāyaṇa cāstiriyār tam urainūlil aḷapeṭaic coṟkaḷaiyum vaḻaṅkiṉār. nīvir nīyir nummai eṉpaṉapōṉṟa coṟkaḷait tiru nāṭṭāravarkaḷum eḻutivarukiṉṟārkaḷ. ivaiyellām vaḻakkiṟantaṉavāyiṉum paṇṭaic ceyyuṭkaḷil vantiruttalāṉ vaḻaṅkappaṭutaliṉ, aṭiyēṉum āṅkaṉam mutaliyavaṟṟai vaḻaṅkalāṉēṉ. camaṉ ceytu cīrtūkkuṅ kōlpōlamaintu viḷaṅkum periyōrkaḷ emmiruvar kūṟṟukkaḷaiyum ārāyntu, amaivillātavaṟṟai aruḷkūrntu terivikkumāṟu paṇivuṭaṉ vēṇṭukiṉṟēṉ.
‘kuṇattaik koḷḷārāka. ataṉāl eṉ? kuṟṟattaik koḷvārāka. ataṉāl eṉ? kuṇattaik koṇṭu kuṟṟattaik koḷḷātuviṭuvārāka. ataṉāltāṉ eṉ? kuṇattaik kuṟṟamākak koḷḷutaltāṉ poṟuttaṟku aritāyuḷatu'.
eṉṟu vaṭamoḻi mahākaviyoruvar kūṟiyatu niṉaikkat takkatā yirukkiṉṟatu.


20. tiru.vē. vē reṭṭiyāravarkaḷiṉ 
ārāycciyiṉ ceppamuṭaimai
vittuvāṉ tiru.vē.vēṅkaṭarācalu reṭṭiyāravarkaḷ eḻutiya 'kapilar' eṉṉum nūliṉ poruḷkaḷ palatiripuṇarcciyāl eḻutappaṭṭaṉavākat tōṉṟiṉamaiyiṉ, tamiḻ kaṟkum iḷaiñar poruṭṭu avaṟṟaic cila kaṭṭuraikaḷāka eḻuti veḷip paṭuttal kaṭaṉeṉak karuti, yāṉ eḻutiya mutaṟkaṭṭurai 'tamiḻp poḻil' tuṇar 13 malar 2-l veḷiyiṭap peṟṟatu. ataṉkaṇ, mukittal, aṅkaṉam kāṇkiṉṟatu, naṟkīrar eṉa reṭṭi yāravarkaḷ vaḻaṅkiya nāl vēṟu coṟkaḷ ārāyappaṭṭaṉa. avarkaḷ vaḻaṅkiya accol vaḻakku eṉṉāl maṟukkap paṭṭamaiyiṉ avarkaḷ aṟivārāycci kuṟaipāṭuṭaiya reṉṟātal, yāṉ niṟaivuṭaiyēṉeṉṟātal karutiṉēṉāka eṇṇātirukka avarkaḷaiyum ēṉōraiyum vēṇṭukiṉṟēṉ. eṉatu ciṟumaiyai eṉṉiṉ aṟintār piṟarilar eṉpatu tēṟṟam. aṅṅaṉam mikac curuṅkiya aṟiviṉēṉāyiṉum, etaṉaiyum uṇmai kāṇum nōkkuṭaṉ ārāyumiyalpum, eṉ aṟivu ārāyccik kuṟaivāl nērnta vaḻukkaḷaip piṟar eṭuttuk kāṭṭaluṟiṉ ataṟkicaintu avaṟṟait tiruttikkoḷḷum viruppamum uṭaiyēṉ. tamatu tavaṟuyātāyiṉum piṟar eṭuttuk kāṭṭiya vaḻi ataṉai ēṟṟukkōṭal naṉmakkaḷ iyal pākalāṉum, tamakkum piṟarkkum payaṉuṇṭāka nūlārāycci ceyvārkku aḵtu iṉṟiyamaiyāk kaṭappāṭu ākalāṉum reṭṭiyāravarkaḷ eṉṉāṟ  kāṭṭaluṟṟa piḻaikaḷukkāka eṉṉai muṉiyātu avaṟṟait tiruttikkoḷḷa muṟpaṭuvārkaḷeṉa eṇṇiṉēṉ. āyiṉ avarkaḷ 'ceppamuṭaiyārkkuc ceyyum viṇṇappam' eṉat talaippeyariṭṭu eḻutiya kaṭṭurai 'tamiḻp poḻil' 13-ām tuṇar,9,10,11-ām malarkaḷil veḷivantataṉaip paṭittu, eṉ eṇṇam paḻutāyiṉamai kaṇṭēṉ. avarkaḷ pala poruntāvuraikaḷāl tampiḻaipaṭṭa muṭipukaḷaiyē cātippāṉ muyaṉṟuḷḷārkaḷ. mīṭṭum avaiyiṟṟai maṟuttu uṇmai nilai nāṭṭutal kaṭaṉāyiṉamaiyiṉ ikkaṭṭurai eḻuta nērntatu. avarkaḷ eḻutiyavaṟṟaiyellām peyartteḻuti ārāyiṉ ivvurai mika viriyumākaliṉ iṉṟiyamaiyātaṉa eṭuttuk kāṭṭappaṭum. itaṉaip paṭippōr avarkaḷ eḻutiyataṉaiyum uṭaṉvaittu nōkkutal naṉṟām.
"mukittal eṉṉuñ col ticaiccol eṉpatu etaṉāṟ pulaṉākiṉṟatō, aṟikilēṉ. atu teluṅkilum kaṉṉaṭattilum vaḻaṅkutalāṉ ticaic collākum eṉiṉ, tamiḻc coṟkaḷuṭ pallāyirañ coṟkaḷ ticaic coṟkaḷēyākum" eṉpatu mutalākac cila kūṟi vantu, 'mukittal eṉpatu ticaiccol eṉṟē koḷḷiṉum' eṉṟu toṭaṅkic cila kūṟukiṉṟārkaḷ. ivaṟṟai nōkkuḻit ticaiccol eṉpatu paṟṟit teḷivāṉa karuttoṉṟum avarkaṭkillai yeṉpatu pulaṉākiṉṟatu.
"	centamiḻ cērnta paṉṉiru nilattun taṅkuṟip piṉavē ticaiccoṟ kiḷavi"1
eṉat ticaiccoṟkilakkaṇa muṇarttum tolkāppiyac cūttiravuraiyil iḷampūraṇarum, cēṉāvaraiyarum centamiḻ nāṭṭut teṉkīḻppāl mutalāka vaṭakīḻppāl iṟutiyākavuḷḷa poṅkar nāṭu mutalāya paṉṉiru nāṭukaḷaip paṉṉiru nilam eṉpataṟkup poruḷākak koṇṭaṉar. nacciṉārkkiṉiyar avaṟṟuṭaṉ, avaṟṟiṉ puṟañcūḻnta ciṅkaḷam mutaliya paṉṉiru nāṭukaḷaiyum koṇṭaṉar. teyvaccilaiyār avviru tiṟattup paṉṉiru nāṭukaḷaiyum eṭuttukkāṭṭiṉarēṉum, vaṭavēṅkaṭan teṉkumariyiṭaippaṭṭa tamiḻnāṭṭaip puṟañ cūḻnta paṉṉiru nāṭukaḷaik kōṭalē poruttamāmeṉṉuṅ karuttiṉareṉpatu avaruraiyāl aṟiyappaṭum. mayilainātarum naṉṉūluraiyil ciṅkaḷam mutaliya paṉṉiru nāṭṭiṉ peyarkaḷaiyuṇarttum ōr cūttirap pakutiyai eṭuttuk kāṭṭi, aḵtu akattiyaṉār iyaṟṟiyatākavum kuṟippiṭṭār. piṟkūṟiya paṉṉirunāṭṭil karunaṭam, teluṅku eṉpaṉavum aṭaṅkiyuḷḷaṉa. reṭṭiyāravarkaḷ teluṅkilum kaṉṉaṭattilumiruntu vantu vaḻaṅkuvaṉavē ticaic collākā eṉakkarutiṉ, poṅkarnāṭu mutaliyavaṟṟiṟ kuriyaṉavum ticaiccollākā eṉpatu avarkaḷ karuttātal pōtarum. maṟṟu, tolkāppiyaṉār kūṟiya ticaiccol iṟṟeṉṟu avarkaḷ viḷakkuvārkaḷāka.
iṉi, "paḻaiya tamiḻilakkiyaṅkaḷil vaḻaṅkātataṉāl atu(mukittal) ticaiccollākum eṉiṉ, avaṟṟil vaḻaṅkātaṉa vellām ticaiccoṟkaḷēyākumaṉṟō? āyiṉ, nāṉ, nīṅkaḷ, anta, inta, enta, cari, aṉuppu, ciri mutaliyaṉavellām paḻaiya tamiḻilakkiyaṅkaḷil vaḻaṅkātaṉavātaliṉ ticaic coṟkaḷēyākum" eṉavum, "mukittal eṉpatu ticaiccol eṉṟē koḷḷiṉum, ceyyuḷilum vaḻakkilum vaḻaṅkutaliṉ ataṉai urainaṭaiyil vaḻaṅkutal tavaṟātal yāṅṅaṉam? ticaiccollai vaḻaṅkalākātu eṉṟu ilakkaṇam uḷatō? eṉavum eḻutiyuḷḷārkaḷ. (yāṉ eḻutiyiruppataṉai iṅkē tarukiṉṟēṉ: "caṅkap pulavarkaḷaip paṟṟi ārāycciyurai eḻutum āciriyaroruvar piṟkālattut talapurāṇam oṉṟil ōriṭattuk kāṇappaṭuvataṉṟik kaṭal pōṟparantuḷḷa tamiḻilakkiya ilakkaṇaṅkaḷil yāṇṭum vaḻaṅkappaṭātatum, uraiyāciriyaṉmār evarālum tīṇṭappaṭātatum, evviṭattu ettiṟattu makkaḷālō aruki vaḻaṅkappaṭuvatumākiya ticaiccol oṉṟai mutaṉmaiyāka nāṭṭuvatum, ataṉaip paḻaiya tamiḻccol eṉṟu niṟuttappaṭuvatum nērmaiyuṭaiyavākat tōṉṟavillai. 'itil, paḻaiya tamiḻilakkiyaṅkaḷil vaḻaṅ kātaṉa vellām ticaiccollākumeṉṟum, ticaiccoṟ kaḷai vaḻaṅkalākātu eṉṟum yāṉ kūṟiyaṉamai peṟappaṭumā ṟeṅṅaṉam? yāṉ kūṟiyavaṟṟai nekiḻaviṭuttuk kūṟātavaṟṟaik kūṟiyavākak kaṟpittukkoṇṭu eḻutuvatē ceppamuṭaimai pōlum.
mukittal eṉṉuñ col tiruppukaḻil palaviṭattil vantuḷatu eṉavum, accollait tamiḻ 'lekcika'ṉil nōkkiya yāṉ atiṟ kuṟittirukkum tiruppukaḻaṭikaḷait kaṇṭiruttal kūṭum eṉavum, avvāṟākavum 'piṟkālattut talapurāṇam oṉṟil ōriṭattuk kāṇappaṭuvatu' eṉṟuyāṉ eḻutiyatu viyappiṉai viḷaikkiṉṟatu eṉavum, aruṇakirinātar kālam 14ām nūṟṟāṇṭu eṉṟu teritaliṉ iṟṟaikku 500 yāṇṭukaṭku muṉṉarē accol tamiḻil vaḻaṅkiya tākum eṉavum avarkaḷ eḻutiyuḷḷārkaḷ. yāṉ eḻutiyatu viyappu viḷaikkakkūṭiyateṉpatil aiyamillai. āyiṉ, tām eḻutiyataṉai nōkkiyē mutaṟkaṇ avarkaṭku viyappuṇṭātal vēṇṭum. "āṟṟiṟ pālattai mukittēṉallēṉ" (kuṟṟālattala. kaṇṭakacētaṉa 39) eṉṟu ceyyuḷilum vantuḷḷa taṉālum" eṉa eḻutiya avarkaḷ ataṉiṉum muṟpaṭṭa tirup pukaḻil accol vaḻaṅkiyiruttalai eṭuttukkāṭṭāmai eṉṉai? avarkaḷ karuttiṉ āḻam aṟitaṟkariyatākavuḷḷatu. maṟṟu, tiruppukaḻil vantuḷḷataṉāl atu paḻaiya tamiḻccol ākumeṉiṉ, calām, capācu mutalāyiṉavum paḻaiya tamiḻc collā meṉpatu avarkaḷ karuttiṟkicaintatu pōlum. "paḻaiya ōlaiyēṭukaḷiṉ iṟutiyil muṟṟiṟṟu eṉṟātal, mukintatu eṉṟātal  eḻutiyiruttalai ōlaiccuvaṭi nōkkiṉār naṉku aṟivar' eṉkiṟārkaḷ. ōlaiccuvaṭi nōkkiya perumai kaṟṟār palarukkuṇṭu. nōkkiyatōṭamaiyātu eṇṇiṟanta cuvaṭikaḷai ārāyntu veḷiyiṭṭavarkaḷ accollai ōriṭattum vaḻaṅkātu viṭṭamai eṉṉai? 'mukintatu eṉpatu muṟṟiṟṟu - pūrttiyāyiṟṟu eṉṉum poruḷatu. iccollai nūṉ muḻuvatilum evvāṟu vaḻaṅkutal kūṭum?' eṉa uraik kiṉṟārkaḷ. ataṉai vaḻaṅkutaṟku nūliṉkaṇ vēṟiṭamē vāykkavillaiyeṉpatu periyatōr viyappē! atu kiṭakka.
paḻaiya tamiḻilakkiyaṅkaḷil vaḻaṅkātaṉa eṉṟu avarkaḷ eṭuttukkāṭṭiya coṟkaḷil 'ciri' eṉpatum  oṉṟu. atu kuṟittu avarkaḷ 'ciri' eṉpatu nakai eṉpataṉ marūu eṉṟuṅ kūṟiviṭalām pōlum eṉṟu kūṟi nakaiyāṭukiṉṟārkaḷ. ataṉai nakai yeṉpataṉ marūu eṉṟu karutukiṉṟavar yārō? 'vilāviṟac cirittiṭṭēṉē' eṉṟu tirunāvukkaracarum, 'cirippār kaḷippār tēṉippār' eṉṟu māṇikkavācakarum, 'eṉṟāṉiṟai yōṉatu kēṭṭavar- cirittār' eṉṟu cēkkiḻārum 'cirittatu ceṅkaṭ cīyam' eṉṟu kampanāṭarum ataṉai vaḻaṅki yuḷḷārkaḷ. iṅṅaṉam tamiḻukku vēntarkaḷ eṉattakka paḻaiya perumakkaḷālum, nacciṉārkkiṉiyar mutaliya uraiyāciriyar kaḷālum payila vaḻaṅkappeṟṟuḷḷa iccollai "nakaiyeṉpataṉ marūu eṉṟu kūṟiviṭalām pōlum" eṉa reṭṭiyāravarkaḷ uraippataṉ nōkkam ituveṉap pulaṉākavillai.
mukittal eṉṉuñ collaik kuṟittut taṭai nikaḻttiya yāṉ 'anuppu', 'nīṅkaḷ' eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷai vaḻaṅkutal takātu eṉṟu cāṟṟukiṉṟārkaḷ. avarkaḷ karuttu, yāṉ ivaṟṟai vaḻaṅkutal tavaṟākāmai pōla avarkaḷ ataṉai vaḻaṅkutalum tavaṟākātu eṉṟu kāṭṭuvatō? avarkaḷ ataṉai vaḻaṅkuvatu tavaṟātal pōla yāṉ ivaṟṟai vaḻaṅkuvatum tavaṟākum eṉṟu kāṭṭuvatō? yāteṉa aṟiyakkūṭavillai. mukittal eṉṉuñ coṟ pōlaṉṟi, ivai tamiḻ nāṭeṅkaṇum poruḷaṟintu pēcappaṭuvaṉavātalaiyum, takutiyuṭaiya pulavar palarālum ivai pēcappaṭṭum eḻutappaṭṭum varutalaiyum ōrtal vēṇṭum. aṉṟiyum iccoṟkaḷaip paḻantamiḻilakkiyaṅkaḷil vantuḷḷa paḻaiya tamiḻc coṟkaḷ eṉa yāṉ ōriṭattum kūṟavillai eṉpataṉaiyum avarkaḷ cintittal vēṇṭum.
iṉi, aṟiñar palarum 'aṅṅaṉam' mutaliyavāka vaḻaṅki varuñ coṟkaḷai reṭṭiyāravarkaḷ 'aṅkaṉam' mutaliya uruviṉavākattirittu, avarkaḷ eḻutiya 'paraṇar' 'kapilar' eṉṉum nūlkaḷiṉum, 'tamiḻp poḻi' lil veḷiyiṭṭa kaṭṭuraikaḷilum vaḻaṅkiyiruppatōṭu, 'kapilar' muṉṉuraiyil avvāṟu vaḻaṅkiṉamaikkuk kāraṇamum kūṟiyuḷḷārkaḷ. atu, "āṅkaṉam, īṅkaṉam, yāṅkaṉam eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷē paḻaiya ceyyuṭkaḷil vantiruttalāṉum, ṅakaram moḻi mutaṟkaṇ vārāteṉṟu tolkāppiyam kūṟutalāṉum aṅkaṉam āṅkaṉam iṅkaṉam īṅkaṉam eṅkaṉam yāṅkaṉam eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷē paḻamaiya eṉpatu aṟiyappaṭum. ākaliṉ yāṉ innūlil aṅkaṉam, iṅkaṉam, yāṅkaṉam eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷaiyē vaḻaṅkiyuḷḷēṉ" eṉpatu. yāṉ ataṉai ārāynta piṉṉar, avarkaḷ ceppamuṭaiyārkkuc ceyta viṇṇappak kaṭṭuraiyil "aṅṅaṉam mutaliya coṟkaḷai avvaṭivil vaḻaṅkutalai yāṉ yāṇṭum maṟuttilēṉ. yāṉ maṟuppatu viyappākavuḷatu eṉṟu nāṭṭāravarkaḷ eḻutiyiruppatuvē viyappākavuḷḷatu. māṇavar poruṭṭu yāṉ eḻutiyirukkum pustakaṅkaḷil aṅṅaṉam iṅṅaṉam yāṅṅaṉam eṉpavaṟṟaiyē vaḻaṅkiyuḷḷēṉ" eṉa ōriṭattilum, "kalācālai māṇavarkkāka yāṉ eḻutiyirukkum pustakaṅkaḷil ōriṭattilātal aṅkaṉam yāṅkaṉam eṉṉuñ col vantila; aṅṅaṉam mutaliya coṟkaḷē vantuḷḷaṉa" eṉap piṟitōriṭattilum eḻutiyuḷḷārkaḷ. avvāṟiruppiṉ, avaṟṟaiyum, 'paraṇar' mutaliyavaṟṟaiyum pārkkum oruvar, māṇavarkkup puttakaṅkaḷ eḻutiya tiru. vēṅkaṭarājalu reṭṭiyāravarkaḷum vēṟu. periyōrkaḷukkākap paraṇar mutaliya nūṟkaḷ iyaṟṟiya tiru vēṅkaṭarājalu reṭṭiyār avarkaḷum vēṟu eṉṟutāṉ koḷvar. eṉṉai? muṉṉaiya puttakaṅkaḷil yāṇṭum 'aṅṅaṉam' mutaliya col vaḻakkum, piṉṉaiyavaṟṟil 'aṅkaṉam' mutaliya vaḻakkumē kāṇap paṭutaliṉ eṉka. reṭṭiyāravarkaḷum caṅkap pulavarkaḷākiya paraṇar kapilarum vēṟu, patiṉōrān tirumuṟaiyiluḷḷa cila nūṟkaḷiyaṟṟiya paraṇar kapilarum vēṟu eṉac coṟkaḷiṉ vaḻakku vēṟṟumai mutaliyaṉa koṇṭu tuṇintuḷḷārkaḷātaliṉ ammuṟai paṟṟip piṟar ceyyum ārāycci muṭipukaḷai avarkaḷ matittalē vēṇṭum! 'paraṇar' 'kapilar' eṉṉum nūṟkaḷiyaṟṟiya reṭṭiyāravarkaḷ vaḻaṅkiya coṟkaḷē eṉ ārāyccik kuriyavākum.
'yāṅṅaṉam ottiyō vīṅkucelaṉmaṇṭilam' eṉattiriyātu kūṟiṉum etukaiyātaṟku iḻukkiṉṟām. aṅṅaṉamākavum etukai mōṉaikaḷiṉ nirppantam illāta kālattil - illāta pāvil, pulavaroruvarallar palarum accollai vaḻaṅkiya pulavaraṉaivarumē - ṅakarattaik kakaramākat tirittē vaḻaṅkiṉār eṉpatu ettuṇai amaiviṉatu!' eṉṟu eḻutiyuḷḷārkaḷ. itil 'iḻukkiṉṟām' eṉpatu kāṟumuḷḷa pakuti muṉṉar eṉṉāl eḻutappaṭṭatē. pulavarellārumē ṅakarattaik kakaramākattirittu vaḻaṅkiṉār eṉal eṉ karutteṉṟu eṅṅaṉam aṟintārkaḷō? yāṉ eḻutiyatu orōvaḻik kakaravuruviṟ kāṇappaṭiṉ ataṟkuk kāraṇam kāṭṭiyapaṭiyēyām. "ceyyuṭ ceyyum pulavar etukait toṭaiyil ṅakarattaik kakaramākat tirittu vaḻaṅkutal iyalpē" eṉṟu eḻutiya yāṉ, ataṉaiyaṭuttu, "yāṅṅaṉa mottiyō vīṅku celaṉ maṇṭilam" eṉat tiriyātu kūṟiṉum etukai yātaṟkiḻukkiṉṟām" eṉṟum, iṟaiyaṉār kaḷaviyaliṉ mūṉṟāñ cūttiram 'āṅkaṉam puṇarnta kiḻavōṉ' eṉṟē paṭikkappaṭṭu varukiṉṟatu eṉṟum eḻutiyiruppavaṟṟai nōkkuvār. ṅakaravuruvil iruppatē iyalpām eṉpatum, kakaravuruviṟ kāṇappaṭuvaṉa cila ēṭeḻutuvōrāl nērntaṉa vākum eṉpatum aṉṟō eṉ karutteṉak koḷḷā niṟpar? reṭṭiyāravarkaḷ eṉ karuttukkaḷait tamakku vēṇṭiya vāṟellām tiritturaippatu muṟaiyākumā? 'paṇṭaip pulavartañ ceyyuḷil etukai mōṉaikaḷai niyatiyākak koḷḷātu viṭuttaṉar" eṉavum, 'yāṅṅaṉa mottiyō vīṅku celaṉ maṇṭilam' eṉat tiriyātu kūṟiṉum etukaiyātaṟku iḻukkiṉṟām' eṉavum kūṟiya avarkaḷē, "iṟaiyaṉārkaḷaviyal mūṉṟāñ cūttiram 'āṅṅaṉam puṇarnta kiḻavōṉ' eṉṟē paṭikkappaṭṭu varukiṉṟatu" eṉa yāṉ eṭuttuk kāṭṭiyavaḻi, ataṉai ēṟṟuk koḷḷa maṉamiṉṟi, "cūttirattiṉ iraṇṭu mūṉṟu nāṉkām aṭikaḷai nōkkiṉ, mutalaṭi, 'āṅkaṉam-' eṉṟē amaintirukkum eṉpatu eḷitiṟpulaṉākum" eṉkiṉṟārkaḷ. ettuṇai muraṇpāṭu kāṇmiṉ! etukaiyē vēṇṭuvatiṉṟu eṉṟum, mūṉṟām eḻuttu ṅakaramākavum kakaramākavum māṟi yiruppiṉum etukaiyāmeṉṟum paṇṭaiyōr karuttiṉaik kaṇṭuraitta avarkaḷ avviraṇṭaṉaiyum kāṟṟilē paṟakka viṭṭu, piṉ mūṉṟaṭikaḷil pāṅkiṉōril, pāṅkilaṉ, āṅka eṉa vantiruttaliṉ 'mutala' ṭiyilum āṅkaṉam eṉṟē irukka vēṇṭum meṉpatu cāla aḻakitu.
"paḻaiya patippukaḷil, āṅṅaṉam virippiṉ aḷaviṟantaṉavē pāṅkuṟavuṇarntōr paṉṉuṅkālai' eṉṟu patikkappaṭṭirunta tolkāppiyac cūttiram, ippoḻutu tiru.vē.turaicāmi aiyaravarkaḷaik koṇṭu paricōtittut tiru. kaṉakacapaip piḷḷaiyavarkaḷ veḷiyiṭṭa patippil, 'āṅkaṉam....kālai' eṉṟu patikkappaṭṭuḷatu. turaicāmi aiyaravarkaḷ pāṭattait tirutti viṭṭārkaḷ eṉṟu nāṭṭāravarkaḷ kūṟiviṭuvārkaḷ pōlum' "eṉṟum, "akanāṉūṟṟil ''yāṅṅaṉam' eṉṟu patippittuḷḷa mūṉṟu iṭaṅkaḷilum (ceyyuḷ 27,90,378) tiru.es.vaiyāpuri piḷḷaiyavarkaḷiṭamirukkum paḻaiya ōlaiyēṭṭil 'yāṅṅaṉam' eṉṟē irukkiṉṟatu. iḵtu ēṭeḻutiṉōr tavaṟu eṉṟu nāṭṭāravarkaḷ kūṟuvārkaḷāyiṉ eṉ ceytum" eṉṟum, "accup puttakaṅkaḷil āṅṅaṉam, yāṅṅaṉam eṉṟu patittirut taṟkuk kāraṇam avarkkuḷḷa naṉṉūṟ payiṟciyē yākum" eṉṟum eḻutiyuḷḷārkaḷ. itiliruntē tolkāpyimum, akanāṉūṟum paḻaiya ēṭṭuc cuvaṭikaḷiliruntu mutalil acciṭṭavarkaḷ 'āṅṅaṉam' eṉavum, 'yāṅṅaṉam' eṉavumē acciṭṭuḷḷārkaḷ eṉpatu pulaṉām. akanāṉūṟu mayilai tiru irājakōpāla aiyaṅkāravarkaḷālum, makāvittuvāṉ tiru rā. irākavaiyaṅkār avarkaḷālum ārāyappaṭṭatu. tolkāppiyac ceyyuḷiyalai nacciṉārkkiṉiyar uraiyuṭaṉ acciṭṭavarkaḷ makāvittuvāṉ tiru rā. irākavaiyaṅkār avarkaḷē. avarkaḷ eṅkeṅku kiṭaitta ettaṉai ēṭṭuccuvaṭikaḷai ārāyntu ataṉaip patittuḷḷārkaḷ eṉpatu apputtaka mukavuraiyāl aṟiyappaṭum. appatippilē, ceyyuḷiyal 51ām cūttirattil 'āṅṅaṉam virippiṉ' eṉṟum, 213 ām cūttirattil 'āṅṅaṉa maṟipa' eṉṟum, 242ām cūttirattil 'āṅṅaṉa moḻukiṉ' eṉṟum 'āṅṅaṉam' eṉṉuñ col avvuruviṉatākavē acciṭap peṟṟuḷatu. avarkaḷ naṉṉūṟ payiṟciyāl aṅṅaṉam ceytu viṭṭārkaḷeṉpatu reṭṭiyāravarkaḷiṉ karuttā kiṉṟatu. palarālum naṉku matikkappeṟṟa ciṟanta pulavarkaḷait tolkāppiyap payiṟciyillāreṉṟum, naṉṉūṟ payiṟci yoṉṟē uṭaiyāreṉṟum kuṟaitturaippatē tamatu perumaiyai mikuppatākumeṉa avarkaḷ karutiyuḷḷārkaḷ pōlum! avvaḷavu perumitamuṭaiya avarkaḷ "āṅṅaṉam-(eṉat) turaicāmi aiyaravarkaḷ pāṭattait tiruttiviṭṭārkaḷ eṉṟu nāṭṭāravarkaḷ kūṟiviṭuvārkaḷ pōlum" eṉa, aṅṅaṉaṅ kūṟiṉ  eṉatu nā ventuviṭumeṉpatu pōlap pukaṉṟuḷḷārkaḷ. tiru.kaṉakacapaip piḷḷaiyavarkaḷ veḷiyiṭṭa poruḷatikārap patippu muṉṉaiya patippukkaḷil uḷḷa nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa piḻaikaḷil mikaccila tiruttappeṟṟum, cila kuṟippukkaḷ cērkkap peṟṟum, aḻakākap patikkap peṟṟuḷateṉpatum, kuṟainta vilaiyil māṇākkarkaḷ peṟṟup payaṉaṭaiyumāṟu utavap peṟṟuḷateṉpatum, uṇmai. āṉāl aḵtu, ēṭupārttu muḻutum ceppañ ceyyappaṭṭatu eṉpatupōl māṟupāṭu piṟitillai. puttakattiluḷḷa appiḻai kaḷellām ēṭṭilum iruntaṉavāyiṉ, reṭṭiyāravarkaḷ 'ēṭu' 'ēṭu' eṉṟu, ataṉ āṇaikku aṭaṅkiyoḻukumāṟu kūṟuvatellām poruḷillā vaṟuṅkūṟṟēyākum. makāmakō pāttiyāya, ṭākṭar u.vē.cāminātaiyaravarkaḷiṉ peyaraiyum patippaiyum orōvaḻit tamakku araṇāka amaittuk koḷḷum reṭṭiyāravarkaḷ, aiyaravarkaḷ tamatu urainaṭaiyil ev viṭattum 'aṅṅaṉam' mutaliya coṟkaḷai 'aṅkaṉam' mutaliya vākat tiriyātu vaḻaṅkuṅ kāraṇattait tāmāka ārāyntō, avarkaḷōṭu ucāviyō aṟintu, tāmum ataṉaip piṉpaṟṟu vataṉṟō muṟaiyākum?
"	iṅṅaṉa miraṇṭu tiṅka ḷēkalu mēka vēlāṉ
aṅṅaṉam puṇarnta vaṉpi ṉavaṇmu laip pōkanīkki
eṅṅaṉa meḻunta tuḷḷa miruḷiṭai yēkaluṟṟāṉ
taṅkiya poṟiyiṉākkantaṉak kortē rāka niṉṟāṉ"1						(cīvakacintāmaṇi . 1359)
eṉpatu aiyaravarkaḷ patippittatu tāṉē? ivviṭattu naṉṉūṟ payiṟciyāṟ patippittārkaḷ  eṉṟē kūṟuvārkaḷ pōlum!
iṉi, avarkaḷ "vaḻaṅkutaṟku. eḷitākiya aṅkaṉam eṉṉuñ col vaḻaṅkutaṟku aritākiya aṅṅaṉam eṉat tiriyātu eṉṟārkaḷ. itaṉai aṟiyiṉ, melloṟṟuttaṉ iṉavalleḻuttōṭu kūṭivarum col vaḻaṅkutaṟku eḷiyatu eṉpatum, melleḻuttu iraṭṭittu varuñcol vaḻaṅkutaṟku ariyatu eṉpatum avarkaḷ karuttātal  naṉku pulaṉākum" eṉakkūṟi, oṉṟu, aṉṟu mutalāyiṉa oṉṉu, aṉṉu mutaliya vākattirintu vaḻaṅkutal eṉṉai? eṉa viṉāvukiṉṟārkaḷ. vaḻaṅkutaṟku eḷitākiya aṅkaṉam eṉpatu aritākiya aṅṅaṉam eṉat tiriyātu eṉak kūṟiyatiliruntu, avarkaḷ piṟ kūṟiyavellām eṉ karuttākumeṉa eṅṅaṉam aṟintu koṇṭārkaḷō terintilatu. eḻuttilum, collilum, toṭarilum cila tammaḷavil yāvarum vaḻaṅkutaṟku ariyaṉavāka
vuḷḷaṉa. ḻa eṉṉum eḻuttu ḷakaramākavum, yakaramākavum ciṟcilarāl vaḻaṅkappaṭutal kaṇkūṭu. cila pakutiyiluḷḷavarkaḷ ataṉai ṣa eṉavum olikkiṉṟaṉar. 'aṅṅaṉam' mutalāyiṉavum avvāṟē tiruntā nāviṉōrkku vaḻaṅkutaṟku ariyavātalai yāvarum uṇarvar. iḵtu uṇmaikku māṟākuvatō? iṉi, maṅkalam, kañci, cantaṉam, māṅkāy, tēṅkāy, eṉpaṉa malaiyāḷattil muṟaiyē maṅṅalam, kaññi, cannaṉam, māṅṅā, tēṅṅā eṉa vaḻaṅkutaliṉ ṅṅa oli ariteṉpatum' ṅka oli eḷiteṉpatum poruntā eṉkiṉṟārkaḷ. kañciyum cantaṉamum īṇṭaikku vēṇṭā. maṅṅalam, māṅṅā, tēṅṅā eṉa malaiyāḷattil vaḻaṅkutaṟkuriya kāraṇattai ammoḻi varalāṟu ārāyvār ārāyka. tiṅkaḷ, poṅkal, paṅkuṉi, kaṅkul, maṅkul, cuṅkam mutaliya coṟkaḷait tamiḻ makkaḷ tiṅṅaḷ, poṅṅal, mutaliyavākat tirittu eṅkēṉum vaḻaṅkukiṉṟārkaḷō? appaṭi vaḻaṅkiṉ ataṉai eṭuttuk kāṭṭutalum, aṉṟēl vāḷā amaintiruttalumaṉṟō nērmaiyākum?
iṉi, marapu nilai tirital eṉa yāṉ eḻutiyatu kuṟittum, 'ṅakarattuṭaṉ mayaṅkutaṟkuriya vēṟu meyataṉōṭu piṟap poṟṟumaiyum oliyoṟṟumaiyumuṭaiya kakaram oṉṟumēyām' eṉa yāṉ eḻutiyatil uḷḷa oliyoṟṟumai eṉpatu kuṟittum avarkaḷ kūṟiyavaṟṟukkellām māṟu uraippiṉ ivvurai veṟitē virivatātalaiyum, aṅṅaṉam, aṅkaṉam eṉpavaṟṟuḷ paḻamaiyatu yātu eṉṉum ārāyccikku avai iṉṟiyamai yātaṉa allavātalaiyum karuti, avaṟṟai ikkaṭṭuraiyil viṭṭoḻikkiṉṟēṉ. viṉaittokai, paṇputtokaikaḷaip pirittup puṇarttāmaikku uraikārarkaḷ kūṟiyuḷḷa kāraṇaṅ kaḷai avarkaḷ eṭuttukkāṭṭiyiruppataṉ ārāycciyum īṇṭaikku vēṇṭaṟ pālataṉṟu. tolkāppiyar aṅṅaṉam, aṅkaṉam eṉpavaṟṟuḷ yātēṉum oṉṟaṉ āṭci pulappaṭa viti kūṟiyiruppiṉ, aḵtoṉṟē, ataṟku māṟākak kūṟappaṭum kāraṇaṅkaḷai yellām citaittoḻittu uṇmaiyai nilaināṭṭuvataṟ kuriyatākum. ilakkiyaṅkaḷil oru col iruvakaiyāka vaḻaṅkiṉ avaṟṟuṭ paḻaiyatāya vaḻakku ituveṉat tuṇital perumpālum aritākum. aṭampu, aṭumpu, aṇimai, aṇumai, kuricil, kurucil, pariti, paruti; eṉa iccoṟkaḷ iru taṉmaiyākavum paḻaiya ceyyuṭkaḷil vantuḷḷamai kāṇka. ivaṟṟuḷ paḻaiya vaṭivu. aṉṟi, uṇmai vaṭivu ituveṉat tuṇitalākāmaiyiṉ ōr patippil oru taṉmaiyākavum, piṟitoru patippiṟ piṟitoru taṉmaiyākavum iccoṟkaḷ patikkappaṭṭu varukiṉṟaṉa. tāḻakkōl eṉap poruḷpaṭum ōr col 'tāḻ' eṉavum, 'tāḷ' eṉavum ilakkiyaṅkaḷil vaḻaṅkiṉum āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār ḻakāra vīṟṟil vaittu,
"	tāḻeṉ kiḷavi kōloṭu puṇariṉ akkiṭaivarutal urittu mākum"1
eṉa viti kūṟiyiruttaliṉ, tāḻ eṉpatē paḻaiya uruvāmeṉa aṟutiyiṭappaṭumaṉṟō? ivvāṟṟāl aṅṅaṉam mutaliya vaṟṟiṉ vaṭivu tuṇitaṟku itukāṟum nikaḻttiya taṭai viṭai yāvaṟṟiṉum pārkkil īṇṭeṭuttuk koḷḷappaṭum tolkāppiya viti paṟṟiya ārāycciyē iṉṟiyamaiyāc ciṟappiṟṟākum.
tolkāppiya nūṉmarapil uṭaṉilai meymmayakkattiṟku vitikūṟiya
'	meynnilai cuṭṭiṉ ellā veḻuttum
tammuṟ ṟāmvaruum raḻavalaṅ kaṭaiyē'2
eṉṉuñ cūttirattai yāṉ eṭuttukkāṭṭi, atil, ra,ḻa eṉṉum iraṇṭum oḻinta patiṉāṟu meyyum tammuṉ tām vantu mayaṅkumeṉa āciriyar vitittiruttaliṉ ṅakaravoṟṟiṉ muṉ ṅakaram vantu mayaṅkum col iruntatākal vēṇṭum eṉavum, tamiḻilē cuṭṭuviṉākkaḷiṉ piṉ ṅaṉam eṉpatu iyaintuvarum aṅṅaṉam mutaliya coṟkaḷaṉṟi ṅakaram uṭaṉilaiyāka mayaṅkaṟkuriya vēṟu col illai eṉavum kūṟiṉēṉ. aṅṅaṉam mutaliya coṟkaḷai a+ṅaṉam eṉṟiṅṅaṉam pirittup puṇarttuvatu tolkāppiyar karuttaṉṟākaliṉ ṅakaram moḻimutaṟkaṇ varumeṉa avar vitittilar eṉavum uraittēṉ.
ivaṟṟiṟku reṭṭiyāravarkaḷ kūṟiya maṟuppuraiyāvaṉa.
1. aṅṅaṉam eṉpataṟkut tolkāppiyar koṇṭa poruḷ attaṉmai, appaṭi eṉpaṉaveṉṉiṉ, āṇṭu akaram cuṭṭupporuḷ tarutaliṉ ṅakaram moḻi mutal vantatākum. ākavē, aññālam annāḷ ammaṇi eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷaip pirittu, 'cuṭṭiṉ muṉṉar ñanamat tōṉṟiṉ' eṉṟu viti kūṟiya āciriyar 'cuṭṭiṉ muṉṉar ṅañanamat tōṉṟiṉ, eṉṟu viti kūṟi, aṅṅaṉam eṉpataṉaiyum amaittiruppar.
2. "meynnilai cuṭṭiṉ" eṉṉuñ cūttirattil ṅakarattoṭu ṅakaram mayaṅkutaṟku aṅṅaṉam iṅṅaṉam eṉṉum coṟkaḷaiyē āciriyar karutiṉār eṉṟu tuṇital amaiyātu.
3. uraiyāciriyar ataṟku eṅṅaṉam eṉṉuñ collai utāraṇamākak kāṭṭiyatum poruntātu.
4. aṅṅaṉam mutaliya coṟkaḷ tolkāppiyaṉār kālattil vaḻaṅkiyirukkumēl tolkāppiyattilum caṅka nūlkaḷilum āṅṅaṉam īṅṅaṉam yāṅṅaṉam eṉpaṉa kāṇappaṭāmal āṅkaṉam īṅkaṉam yāṅkaṉam eṉpaṉavē kāṇappaṭutaṟku niyāyam illai.
5. ṅakarattiṉmuṉ ṅakaram varutaṟku utāraṇam 'piṭiyūṭṭip piṉṉuṇṇuṅṅ kaḷiṟeṉavu muraittaṉarē' eṉṉum aṭiyil ṅakaram iraṇṭu vantiruttal pōlvatākum. taṉi meyyōṭu taṉimey mayaṅkutalai iccūttirattiṟ koḷḷākkāl, 'kaṇṇ karuviḷai', 'poṉṉ poṟicuṇaṅku eṉpaṉa pōla varupavaṟṟi lellām meykaḷ mayaṅki niṟṟaṟku ilakkaṇamiṉṟākum.
ivaṟṟuḷ 4āvatākak kuṟikkappaṭṭa ceyti uṇmaikku māṟāyateṉpatu yāṉ mēleṭuttuk kāṭṭiyavaṟṟiliruntu pulaṉākum. maṟṟum, iccūttira ārāycciyiṉāl uṇmai tuṇiya vēṇṭuvatu muṟaiyākavum, ataṟku māṟākat tāmē tuṇintu koṇṭatoru piṟaḻcci muṭipukku iccūttirap poruḷai iṉaikka muyalvatākavum uḷḷatu. ākaliṉ ataṉaiyoḻittu, ēṉaiyaṉa iṅku ārāytaṟkuriyaṉa.
1. aṅṅaṉam eṉṟillātu, reṭṭiyāravarkaḷ koḷkaip paṭi aṅkaṉam eṉṟiruppiṉum, accol attaṉmai eṉap poruḷpaṭumākaliṉ, a eṉṉuñ cuṭṭu taṉmai eṉap poruḷ paṭum kaṉam eṉpataṉōṭu puṇarnta teṉal vēṇṭum. tolkāppiya uyir mayaṅkiyalil
"	viṉaiyeñcu kiḷaviyum uvamak kiḷaviyum
eṉave ṉeccamum cuṭṭiṉ iṟutiyum
 āṅka veṉṉum uraiyacaik kiḷaviyum
ñāṅkark kiḷanta valleḻuttu mikumē"1
eṉaakara vīṟṟilum,
"	ikara viṟutip peyarnilai muṉṉar
vēṟṟumai yāyiṉ valleḻuttu mikumē"1
"	iṉiyaṇi yeṉṉuṅ kālaiyum iṭaṉum
viṉaiyeñcu kiḷaviyum cuṭṭum aṉṉa"2
eṉa ikara vīṟṟilum,
"ukara viṟuti akara viyaṟṟē"3
"cuṭṭiṉ muṉṉarum attoḻiṟ ṟākum"4
eṉa ukara vīṟṟilum āciriyar viti kūṟiyiruttaliṉ, kaṉam eṉpatoru col taṉmai eṉap poruḷpaṭuvatāyiruntu, akaram mutaliya cuṭṭukkaḷōṭu puṇarntiruppiṉ, akkaṉam eṉpatu mutaliyavāka valimikkaṉṟō muṭintirukka vēṇṭum? avvāṟiṉmaiyiṉ, tam kūṟṟut tamatu koḷkaiyiṉaiyē citaittoḻippataṉai ōrātu, avarkaḷ aṅṅaṉam eṉpataṉ uṇmaiyai maṟukkap pukuntatu eṉṉaiyō?
aṅṅaṉam eṉpatu āciriyar karuttākātu eṉpataṟkum, uraiyāciriyar kāṭṭiya utāraṇam poruntātatu eṉpataṟkum maṟṟum avarkaḷ kūṟiyaṉa ārāyaṟpālaṉa.
"raḻa oḻinta maṟṟaiya meyyeḻuttukkaḷ tammoṭutām mayaṅkum eṉpatuvē cūttirattiṉ poruḷ. itaṉāl, ṅakarat toṭu ṅakaram mayaṅkutaṟku aṅṅaṉam iṅṅaṉam eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷaiyē āciriyar karutiṉār eṉṟu tuṇital amaiyātu. āyiṉ uraiyāciriyar, eṅṅaṉam eṉṉuñ collaiyē utāraṇamākak kāṭṭiṉārāl eṉiṉ, ataṉai nōkkutum;
"	yaraḻa eṉṉum puḷḷi muṉṉar
mutalā keḻuttu ṅakaramoṭu tōṉṟum"5
eṉṉuñ cūttirattiṉ uraiyil, yaraḻakkaḷukku muṉ ṅakaram varutaṟku utāraṇamāka avar, vēyṅaṉam, vērṅaṉam, vīḻṅaṉam eṉpavaṟṟaik kāṭṭiyuḷḷār. ivvutāraṇaṅkaḷil vēy, vēr, vīḻ, eṉpaṉa nilaimoḻikaḷākaliṉ, ṅaṉam eṉpatu varumoḻi eṉa naṉkaṟiyappaṭum. ākavē ṅakaram moḻi mutal vantatāku
maṉṟē? ivvutāraṇamē āciriyar karutiṉārāyiṉ, 'mutalā
keḻuttu ṅakaramoṭu tōṉṟum' eṉṟu ṅakarattaip pirittuk kūṟutaliṉṟi, 'mutalākeḻuttut tōṉṟum eṉpa' eṉpatu pōlaṉṟē cūttirañ ceytiruppār? ṅakaram moḻimutal vārātataṉāṉaṉṟē ataṉai mutalākeḻuttoṭu cērkkātu pirittuk kūṟiṉār? iccūttirattiṟku uraiyāciriyar kāṭṭiya 'vēyṅaṉam' mutaliyavai utāraṇamākumō? itu pōṉṟē, 'meynnilai cuṭṭiṉ' eṉṉuñ cūttirattiṉ uraiyil avar kāṭṭiya 'eṅṅaṉam' eṉṉum utāraṇamum amaivuṭaiyataṉṟu" eṉṟu kūṟiyuḷḷārkaḷ.
'yaraḻa' eṉṟu toṭaṅkum cūttiram, meynnilai cuṭṭiṉ' eṉṉuñ cūttirattai yoṭṭi muṟpaṭa amaintuḷatu. yaraḻa voṟṟiṉmuṉ ṅakaram vantu mayaṅkutaluṇṭeṉpatum, ṅakaram taṉṉōṭu tāṉ mayaṅkutaluṇṭeṉpatum, ivviru cūttirat tālum peṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. muṉṉataṟku vēyṅaṉam' mutaliyavaṟṟaiyum, piṉṉataṟku 'eṅṅaṉam' eṉpataṉaiyum uraiyāciriyarum, nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum utāraṇaṅ kāṭṭiṉar. iraṇṭu cūttirat tiṟkum iruvarum kāṭṭiya iruvakai utāraṇamum poruntā eṉṟu reṭṭiyāravarkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟārkaḷ. piṉṉataṟku avarkaḷ kāṭṭum utāraṇam piṉpu ārāytaṟkurittu. yaraḻa muṉ ṅakaram muyaṅkutaṟku uraiyāḷarkaḷ kāṭṭiya utāraṇam amaiyātu eṉṟavarkaḷ tām vēṟu kāṭṭavumillai. eppaṭiyum 'ṅaṉam' eṉpataṉai ēṟṟuk koḷḷalākātu eṉpatē avarkaḷ kaṭaip piṭi. ataṟku avarkaḷ kūṟumkāraṇam 'ṅaṉam' eṉpataṉaik koṇṭāl ṅakaram moḻikku mutalākiṉṟatu. ṅakaram moḻi mutalākumēl 'mutalākeḻuttu ṅakaramōṭu tōṉṟum' eṉa mutalākeḻuttiṉ vēṟāka ṅakarattaip pirittuk kūṟutal amaiyātu eṉpatē nallatu; avarkaḷ vātam evvaḷavu poruntumeṉṟu pārppōm. tolkāppiyam kūṟuvatu niṟka. aṅṅaṉam mutaliya coṟkaḷukku naṉṉūl viti kūṟutaliṉ atu tōṉṟutaṟku muṉpu toṭṭē accoṟkaḷ vaḻakkiliruntu varukiṉṟaṉa eṉpatu vāymaiyaṉṟō? pala nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷāka aṅṅaṉam avai vaḻaṅki varā niṟkavum, ṅaṉam eṉpatu orukālattum ōriṭattum taṉittu vaḻaṅkāmaikku avarkaḷ yātu kāraṇam kūṟuvārkaḷ? aḵtu aṅṅaṉam vaḻaṅkutaṟkum vaḻaṅkāmaikkum, avvāṟu vaḻaṅkutalum vaḻaṅkāmaiyumē kāraṇameṉṟu tāṉ avarkaḷ kūṟavēṇṭum! ivvāṟṟāl, tolkāppiyattil yaraḻamuṉ ṅakaram mayaṅkum eṉpataṟkum ṅakaram taṉṉoṭu tāṉ mayaṅkum eṉṟataṟkum, tolkāppiyam muḻutukkum uraiyeḻutiṉōrākiya uraiyāciriyarum nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum kāṭṭiya utāraṇaṅkaḷē poruntuvaṉavām eṉpatum,  tamiḻ kūṟum nallulakattu vaḻakkuñ ceyyuḷum ārāyntu nūliyaṟṟiya tolkāppiyaṉār yaraḻa oṟṟiṉ muṉṉum, ṅakara oṟṟiṉ muṉṉum ṅakaram vantu mayaṅkiya coṟkaḷ vaḻaṅkiṉamaikaṇṭu avaṟṟiṟku viti kūṟiyuḷḷār eṉpatum, avvāṟāyiṉum ṅaṉam eṉpatu yaraḻa vīṟṟuc coṟkaḷai yoṭṭiyum, cuṭṭu viṉākkaḷai yoṭṭiyum vaḻaṅkiyataṉṟit taṉittu vaḻaṅkāmaiyiṉ ṅakaram moḻi mutaṟkaṇ varumeṉṟu kūṟiṟṟilar eṉpatum, naṉṉūlār kālattil yaraḻavoṟṟiṉ muṉ ṅakaram mayaṅkiya coṟkaḷ vaḻakkāṟaṟṟup pōkac cuṭṭu viṉākkaḷiṉ piṉ ṅaṉam eṉpatu cērnta coṟkaḷē vaḻaṅkiṉamaiyiṉ avvaḻakkiṟku maṭṭil viti kūṟiṉār eṉpatum, ṅaṉam eṉpatu taṉittu vaḻaṅkāviṭiṉum taṉakkeṉa ōr poruḷuṭaiyatātal paṟṟit tamatu matampaṭa 'ṅavvum mutalākum' eṉṟār eṉpatum naṉkayaṟiyappaṭum uṇmaikaḷākum eṉka. ṅakara meyyuṭaṉ uyir kūṭiya paṉṉiraṇṭu uyirmey yeḻuttukkaḷil 'ṅā' mutaliya patiṉōreḻuttukkaḷum tamiḻ moḻiyil yāṇṭum varuvatillai. atupaṟṟiyē "ṅappōlvaḷai" eṉpatoru mutumoḻiyum tōṉṟiṟṟu. akaraṅkūṭiya ṅakarattāṉum 'ṅaṉam' eṉpataṟkaṉṟi vēṟu collukku uṟuppāy vara yārum kaṇṭatumillai. kēṭṭatumillai. ippoḻutu ataṉaiyum cekuttuviṭa reṭṭiyāravarkaḷ muyalkiṉṟārkaḷ. avveḻuttiṉ mītu avarkaṭku attakaiya ceṟṟam uṇṭāyatu eṉṉaiyō!
5. ṅakarattuṭaṉ ṅakaram mayaṅkutaṟku "uṇṇuṅṅkaḷiṟu" eṉṉum oṟṟaḷapeṭaiyai avarkaḷ utāraṇaṅkāṭṭiyatu muḻuppōliyām eṉpataṉaic ciṟumakārum aṟivar. āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār nūṉ marapiṉkaṇ meymmayakkaṅ kūṟuvāṉṟoṭaṅki,
"	ṭaṟalaḷa eṉṉum puḷḷi muṉṉark kacapa eṉṉum mūveḻut turiya"1
eṉpatu mutal
"	yaraḻa eṉṉum puḷḷi muṉṉar mutalā keḻuttu ṅakaramoṭu tōṉṟum"2
eṉpatu kāṟumuḷḷa ēḻu cūttiraṅkaḷāl vēṟṟu nilai meym mayakkaṅ kūṟi,
"	meynnilai cuṭṭiṉ ellā veḻuttum
tammuṟ ṟāmvarūum raḻavalaṅ kaṭaiyē"1
eṉṉum cūttirattāl meykaḷ tammoṭu tām mayaṅkum uṭaṉilai meymmayakkaṅ kūṟuvārāyiṉar. ivaiyellām meymmayakkamēyāyiṉum taṉi meyyuṭaṉ uyirmey mayaṅku
